          '# '
          '──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
          '# In-Memory "Database"\n'
          '# '
          '──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
          '\n'
          'db_authors: Dict[str, dict] = {}\n'
          'db_categories: Dict[str, dict] = {}\n'
          'db_books: Dict[str, dict] = {}\n'
          'db_members: Dict[str, dict] = {}\n'
          'db_loans: Dict[str, dict] = {}\n'
          'db_reservations: Dict[str, dict] = {}\n'
          '\n'
          'FINE_PER_DAY = 0.50  # $0.50 fine per overdue day\n'
          'RESERVATION_EXPIRY_HOURS = 72\n'
          '\n'
          '# '
          '──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
          '# Helper Utilities\n'
          '# '
          '──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
          '\n'
          'def new_id() -> str:\n'
          '    return str(uuid.uuid4())\n'
          '\n'
          'def now() -> datetime:\n'
          '    return datetime.utcnow()\n'
          '\n'
          'def paginate(items: list, page: int, page_size: int) -> dict:\n'
          '    total = len(items)\n'
          '    start = (page - 1) * page_size\n'
          '    end = start + page_size\n'
          '    total_pages = (total + page_size - 1) // page_size if total > 0 '
          'else 1\n'
          '    return {\n'
          '        "total": total,\n'
          '        "page": page,\n'
          '        "page_size": page_size,\n'
          '        "total_pages": total_pages,\n'
          '        "items": items[start:end],\n'
          '    }\n'
          '\n'
          'def calculate_fine(due_date: date, return_date: Optional[date] = '
          'None) -> float:\n'
          '    check_date = return_date or date.today()\n'
          '    if check_date > due_date:\n'
          '        overdue_days = (check_date - due_date).days\n'
          '        return round(overdue_days * FINE_PER_DAY, 2)\n'
          '    return 0.0\n'
          '\n'
          'def get_book_or_404(book_id: str) -> dict:\n'
          '    book = db_books.get(book_id)\n'
          '    if not book:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"Book '
          '\'{book_id}\' not found")\n'
          '    return book\n'
          '\n'
          'def get_member_or_404(member_id: str) -> dict:\n'
          '    member = db_members.get(member_id)\n'
          '    if not member:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"Member '
          '\'{member_id}\' not found")\n'
          '    return member\n'
          '\n'
          'def get_author_or_404(author_id: str) -> dict:\n'
          '    author = db_authors.get(author_id)\n'
          '    if not author:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"Author '
          '\'{author_id}\' not found")\n'
          '    return author\n'
          '\n'
          'def get_category_or_404(category_id: str) -> dict:\n'
          '    category = db_categories.get(category_id)\n'
          '    if not category:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"Category '
          '\'{category_id}\' not found")\n'
          '    return category\n'
          '\n'
          'def enrich_book(book: dict) -> dict:\n'
          '    book = dict(book)\n'
          '    book["authors"] = [db_authors[a] for a in '
          'book.get("author_ids", []) if a in db_authors]\n'
          '    book["categories"] = [db_categories[c] for c in '
          'book.get("category_ids", []) if c in db_categories]\n'
          '    book["active_loans_count"] = sum(\n'
          '        1 for l in db_loans.values()\n'
          '        if l["book_id"] == book["id"] and l["status"] == '
          'LoanStatus.ACTIVE\n'
          '    )\n'
          '    book["reservation_count"] = sum(\n'
          '        1 for r in db_reservations.values()\n'
          '        if r["book_id"] == book["id"] and r["status"] == '
          'ReservationStatus.PENDING\n'
          '    )\n'
          '    return book\n'
          '\n'
          'def sync_loan_statuses():\n'
          '    """Mark overdue loans and update available copies."""\n'
          '    for loan in db_loans.values():\n'
          '        if loan["status"] == LoanStatus.ACTIVE and loan["due_date"] '
          '< date.today():\n'
          '            loan["status"] = LoanStatus.OVERDUE\n'
          '            loan["fine_amount"] = calculate_fine(loan["due_date"])\n'
          '            loan["updated_at"] = now()\n'
          '\n'
          'def notify_member_reservation_ready(member_id: str, book_title: '
          'str):\n'
          '    """Background task: simulate sending notification."""\n'
          '    logger.info(f"[NOTIFY] Member {member_id}: Book '
          '\'{book_title}\' is now available!")\n'
          '\n'
          '# '
          '──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
          '# Root\n'
          '# '
          '──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get("/", tags=["Root"])\n'
          'def root():\n'
          '    return {\n'
          '        "service": "Library Management System",\n'
          '        "version": "1.0.0",\n'
          '        "docs": "/docs",\n'
          '        "health": "/health",\n'
          '    }\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get("/health", tags=["Root"])\n'
          'def health_check():\n'
          '    return {\n'
          '        "status": "healthy",\n'
          '        "timestamp": now(),\n'
          '        "counts": {\n'
          '            "books": len(db_books),\n'
          '            "members": len(db_members),\n'
          '            "loans": len(db_loans),\n'
          '        },\n'
          '    }\n'
          '\n'
          '# '
          '──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
          '# Authors\n'
          '# '
          '──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.post("/authors", response_model=Author, status_code=201, '
          'tags=["Authors"])\n'
          'def create_author(payload: AuthorCreate):\n'
          '    """Register a new author in the system."""\n'
          '    author_id = new_id()\n'
          '    author = {\n'
          '        "id": author_id,\n'
          '        **payload.dict(),\n'
          '        "full_name": f"{payload.first_name} {payload.last_name}",\n'
          '        "created_at": now(),\n'
          '        "updated_at": now(),\n'
          '    }\n'
          '    db_authors[author_id] = author\n'
          '    logger.info(f"Author created: {author[\'full_name\']}")\n'
          '    return author\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get("/authors", response_model=PaginatedResponse, '
          'tags=["Authors"])\n'
          'def list_authors(\n'
          '    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),\n'
          '    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=100),\n'
          '    search: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="Search by name '
          'or nationality"),\n'
          '    nationality: Optional[str] = Query(None),\n'
          '    sort_by: str = Query("last_name", enum=["first_name", '
          '"last_name", "nationality", "created_at"]),\n'
          '    order: SortOrder = Query(SortOrder.ASC),\n'
          '):\n'
          '    """List all authors with search, filter, and pagination."""\n'
          '    authors = list(db_authors.values())\n'
          '    if search:\n'
          '        search = search.lower()\n'
          '        authors = [\n'
          '            a for a in authors\n'
          '            if search in a["first_name"].lower()\n'
          '            or search in a["last_name"].lower()\n'
          '            or (a.get("nationality") and search in '
          'a["nationality"].lower())\n'
          '        ]\n'
          '    if nationality:\n'
          '        authors = [a for a in authors if a.get("nationality", '
          '"").lower() == nationality.lower()]\n'
          '    reverse = order == SortOrder.DESC\n'
          '    authors.sort(key=lambda x: (x.get(sort_by) or ""), '
          'reverse=reverse)\n'
          '    return paginate(authors, page, page_size)\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get("/authors/{author_id}", response_model=Author, '
          'tags=["Authors"])\n'
          'def get_author(author_id: str = Path(..., description="Author '
          'UUID")):\n'
          '    """Retrieve a single author by ID."""\n'
          '    return get_author_or_404(author_id)\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get("/authors/{author_id}/books", response_model=List[Book], '
          'tags=["Authors"])\n'
          'def get_author_books(author_id: str = Path(...)):\n'
          '    """Get all books written by a specific author."""\n'
          '    get_author_or_404(author_id)\n'
          '    return [b for b in db_books.values() if author_id in '
          'b.get("author_ids", [])]\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.put("/authors/{author_id}", response_model=Author, '
          'tags=["Authors"])\n'
          'def update_author(author_id: str, payload: AuthorUpdate):\n'
          '    """Update author information."""\n'
          '    author = get_author_or_404(author_id)\n'
          '    updates = payload.dict(exclude_unset=True)\n'
          '    author.update(updates)\n'
          '    if "first_name" in updates or "last_name" in updates:\n'
          '        author["full_name"] = f"{author[\'first_name\']} '
          '{author[\'last_name\']}"\n'
          '    author["updated_at"] = now()\n'
          '    return author\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.delete("/authors/{author_id}", response_model=MessageResponse, '
          'tags=["Authors"])\n'
          'def delete_author(author_id: str):\n'
          '    """Delete an author (only if no books are linked)."""\n'
          '    get_author_or_404(author_id)\n'
          '    linked_books = [b for b in db_books.values() if author_id in '
          'b.get("author_ids", [])]\n'
          '    if linked_books:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(\n'
          '            status_code=409,\n'
          '            detail=f"Cannot delete author: linked to '
          '{len(linked_books)} book(s). Remove links first."\n'
          '        )\n'
          '    del db_authors[author_id]\n'
          '    return {"message": "Author deleted successfully"}\n'
          '\n'
          '# '
          '──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
          '# Categories\n'
          '# '
          '──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.post("/categories", response_model=Category, status_code=201, '
          'tags=["Categories"])\n'
          'def create_category(payload: CategoryCreate):\n'
          '    """Create a new book category/genre."""\n'
          '    if payload.parent_category_id and payload.parent_category_id '
          'not in db_categories:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Parent '
          'category not found")\n'
          '    cat_id = new_id()\n'
          '    category = {\n'
          '        "id": cat_id,\n'
          '        **payload.dict(),\n'
          '        "book_count": 0,\n'
          '        "created_at": now(),\n'
          '        "updated_at": now(),\n'
          '    }\n'
          '    db_categories[cat_id] = category\n'
          '    return category\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get("/categories", response_model=List[Category], '
          'tags=["Categories"])\n'
          'def list_categories(include_empty: bool = Query(True)):\n'
          '    """List all categories. Optionally hide empty ones."""\n'
          '    cats = list(db_categories.values())\n'
          '    for cat in cats:\n'
          '        cat["book_count"] = sum(1 for b in db_books.values() if '
          'cat["id"] in b.get("category_ids", []))\n'
          '    if not include_empty:\n'
          '        cats = [c for c in cats if c["book_count"] > 0]\n'
          '    return cats\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get("/categories/{category_id}", response_model=Category, '
          'tags=["Categories"])\n'
          'def get_category(category_id: str):\n'
          '    """Get a single category by ID."""\n'
          '    cat = get_category_or_404(category_id)\n'
          '    cat["book_count"] = sum(1 for b in db_books.values() if '
          'category_id in b.get("category_ids", []))\n'
          '    return cat\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get("/categories/{category_id}/books", '
          'response_model=List[Book], tags=["Categories"])\n'
          'def get_books_by_category(category_id: str):\n'
          '    """Get all books in a specific category."""\n'
          '    get_category_or_404(category_id)\n'
          '    return [b for b in db_books.values() if category_id in '
          'b.get("category_ids", [])]\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.put("/categories/{category_id}", response_model=Category, '
          'tags=["Categories"])\n'
          'def update_category(category_id: str, payload: CategoryUpdate):\n'
          '    """Update a category."""\n'
          '    cat = get_category_or_404(category_id)\n'
          '    cat.update(payload.dict(exclude_unset=True))\n'
          '    cat["updated_at"] = now()\n'
          '    return cat\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.delete("/categories/{category_id}", '
          'response_model=MessageResponse, tags=["Categories"])\n'
          'def delete_category(category_id: str):\n'
          '    """Delete a category (only if no books are assigned to it)."""\n'
          '    get_category_or_404(category_id)\n'
          '    linked = [b for b in db_books.values() if category_id in '
          'b.get("category_ids", [])]\n'
          '    if linked:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail=f"Category in '
          'use by {len(linked)} book(s)")\n'
          '    del db_categories[category_id]\n'
          '    return {"message": "Category deleted successfully"}\n'
          '\n'
          '# '
          '──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
          '# Books\n'
          '# '
          '──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.post("/books", response_model=Book, status_code=201, '
          'tags=["Books"])\n'
          'def create_book(payload: BookCreate):\n'
          '    """Add a new book to the library collection."""\n'
          '    # Validate author IDs\n'
          '    for aid in payload.author_ids:\n'
          '        get_author_or_404(aid)\n'
          '    # Validate category IDs\n'
          '    for cid in payload.category_ids:\n'
          '        get_category_or_404(cid)\n'
          '    # Check for duplicate ISBN\n'
          '    if any(b["isbn"] == payload.isbn for b in db_books.values()):\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail=f"Book with '
          'ISBN \'{payload.isbn}\' already exists")\n'
          '    book_id = new_id()\n'
          '    book = {\n'
          '        "id": book_id,\n'
          '        **payload.dict(),\n'
          '        "available_copies": payload.total_copies,\n'
          '        "created_at": now(),\n'
          '        "updated_at": now(),\n'
          '    }\n'
          '    db_books[book_id] = book\n'
          '    logger.info(f"Book created: \'{payload.title}\' (ISBN: '
          '{payload.isbn})")\n'
          '    return book\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get("/books", response_model=PaginatedResponse, '
          'tags=["Books"])\n'
          'def list_books(\n'
          '    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),\n'
          '    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=100),\n'
          '    search: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="Search title, '
          'ISBN, or publisher"),\n'
          '    author_id: Optional[str] = Query(None),\n'
          '    category_id: Optional[str] = Query(None),\n'
          '    language: Optional[str] = Query(None),\n'
          '    available_only: bool = Query(False, description="Show only '
          'books with available copies"),\n'
          '    published_after: Optional[date] = Query(None),\n'
          '    published_before: Optional[date] = Query(None),\n'
          '    condition: Optional[BookCondition] = Query(None),\n'
          '    sort_by: str = Query("title", enum=["title", "published_date", '
          '"created_at", "pages"]),\n'
          '    order: SortOrder = Query(SortOrder.ASC),\n'
          '):\n'
          '    """\n'
          '    List all books with rich filtering, searching, and pagination.\n'
          '    Combine filters to narrow results.\n'
          '    """\n'
          '    sync_loan_statuses()\n'
          '    books = list(db_books.values())\n'
          '\n'
          '    if search:\n'
          '        q = search.lower()\n'
          '        books = [\n'
          '            b for b in books if\n'
          '            q in b["title"].lower() or\n'
          '            q in b.get("isbn", "").lower() or\n'
          '            q in (b.get("publisher") or "").lower()\n'
          '        ]\n'
          '    if author_id:\n'
          '        books = [b for b in books if author_id in '
          'b.get("author_ids", [])]\n'
          '    if category_id:\n'
          '        books = [b for b in books if category_id in '
          'b.get("category_ids", [])]\n'
          '    if language:\n'
          '        books = [b for b in books if b.get("language", "").lower() '
          '== language.lower()]\n'
          '    if available_only:\n'
          '        books = [b for b in books if b["available_copies"] > 0]\n'
          '    if published_after:\n'
          '        books = [b for b in books if b.get("published_date") and '
          'b["published_date"] >= published_after]\n'
          '    if published_before:\n'
          '        books = [b for b in books if b.get("published_date") and '
          'b["published_date"] <= published_before]\n'
          '    if condition:\n'
          '        books = [b for b in books if b.get("condition") == '
          'condition]\n'
          '\n'
          '    reverse = order == SortOrder.DESC\n'
          '    books.sort(key=lambda x: (x.get(sort_by) or ""), '
          'reverse=reverse)\n'
          '    return paginate(books, page, page_size)\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get("/books/{book_id}", response_model=BookDetail, '
          'tags=["Books"])\n'
          'def get_book(book_id: str = Path(..., description="Book UUID")):\n'
          '    """Get full detail for a single book including authors, '
          'categories, and loan info."""\n'
          '    book = get_book_or_404(book_id)\n'
          '    return enrich_book(book)\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get("/books/isbn/{isbn}", response_model=BookDetail, '
          'tags=["Books"])\n'
          'def get_book_by_isbn(isbn: str = Path(..., description="ISBN-10 or '
          'ISBN-13")):\n'
          '    """Look up a book directly by its ISBN."""\n'
          '    for book in db_books.values():\n'
          '        if book["isbn"] == isbn:\n'
          '            return enrich_book(book)\n'
          '    raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"No book with ISBN '
          '\'{isbn}\' found")\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.put("/books/{book_id}", response_model=Book, tags=["Books"])\n'
          'def update_book(book_id: str, payload: BookUpdate):\n'
          '    """Update book metadata. Cannot reduce total_copies below '
          'currently loaned count."""\n'
          '    book = get_book_or_404(book_id)\n'
          '    updates = payload.dict(exclude_unset=True)\n'
          '\n'
          '    if "author_ids" in updates:\n'
          '        for aid in updates["author_ids"]:\n'
          '            get_author_or_404(aid)\n'
          '    if "category_ids" in updates:\n'
          '        for cid in updates["category_ids"]:\n'
          '            get_category_or_404(cid)\n'
          '\n'
          '    if "total_copies" in updates:\n'
          '        active_loans = sum(\n'
          '            1 for l in db_loans.values()\n'
          '            if l["book_id"] == book_id and l["status"] in '
          '(LoanStatus.ACTIVE, LoanStatus.OVERDUE)\n'
          '        )\n'
          '        if updates["total_copies"] < active_loans:\n'
          '            raise HTTPException(\n'
          '                status_code=409,\n'
          '                detail=f"Cannot reduce copies to '
          "{updates['total_copies']}; {active_loans} copies currently on "
          'loan."\n'
          '            )\n'
          '        diff = updates["total_copies"] - book["total_copies"]\n'
          '        book["available_copies"] = max(0, book["available_copies"] '
          '+ diff)\n'
          '\n'
          '    book.update(updates)\n'
          '    book["updated_at"] = now()\n'
          '    return book\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.patch("/books/{book_id}/copies", response_model=Book, '
          'tags=["Books"])\n'
          'def adjust_copies(\n'
          '    book_id: str,\n'
          '    delta: int = Query(..., description="Positive to add, negative '
          'to remove copies")\n'
          '):\n'
          '    """Quickly add or remove physical copies of a book."""\n'
          '    book = get_book_or_404(book_id)\n'
          '    new_total = book["total_copies"] + delta\n'
          '    if new_total < 1:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Total copies '
          'cannot drop below 1")\n'
          '    active = sum(\n'
          '        1 for l in db_loans.values()\n'
          '        if l["book_id"] == book_id and l["status"] in '
          '(LoanStatus.ACTIVE, LoanStatus.OVERDUE)\n'
          '    )\n'
          '    if new_total < active:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail=f"{active} '
          'copies are currently on loan")\n'
          '    book["total_copies"] = new_total\n'
          '    book["available_copies"] = new_total - active\n'
          '    book["updated_at"] = now()\n'
          '    return book\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.delete("/books/{book_id}", response_model=MessageResponse, '
          'tags=["Books"])\n'
          'def delete_book(book_id: str):\n'
          '    """Remove a book from the system (must have no active '
          'loans)."""\n'
          '    book = get_book_or_404(book_id)\n'
          '    active = [\n'
          '        l for l in db_loans.values()\n'
          '        if l["book_id"] == book_id and l["status"] in '
          '(LoanStatus.ACTIVE, LoanStatus.OVERDUE)\n'
          '    ]\n'
          '    if active:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail=f"Book has '
          '{len(active)} active loan(s). Return them first.")\n'
          '    del db_books[book_id]\n'
          '    logger.info(f"Book deleted: {book_id}")\n'
          '    return {"message": f"Book \'{book[\'title\']}\' deleted '
          'successfully"}\n'
          '\n'
          '# '
          '──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
          '# Members\n'
          '# '
          '──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.post("/members", response_model=Member, status_code=201, '
          'tags=["Members"])\n'
          'def create_member(payload: MemberCreate):\n'
          '    """Register a new library member."""\n'
          '    if any(m["email"] == payload.email for m in '
          'db_members.values()):\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail=f"Email '
          '\'{payload.email}\' is already registered")\n'
          '    member_id = new_id()\n'
          '    member = {\n'
          '        "id": member_id,\n'
          '        **payload.dict(),\n'
          '        "full_name": f"{payload.first_name} {payload.last_name}",\n'
          '        "status": MemberStatus.ACTIVE,\n'
          '        "active_loans": 0,\n'
          '        "total_loans_ever": 0,\n'
          '        "outstanding_fines": 0.0,\n'
          '        "membership_expiry": payload.membership_expiry or '
          '(date.today() + timedelta(days=365)),\n'
          '        "created_at": now(),\n'
          '        "updated_at": now(),\n'
          '    }\n'
          '    db_members[member_id] = member\n'
          '    logger.info(f"Member registered: {member[\'full_name\']} '
          '({member[\'email\']})")\n'
          '    return member\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get("/members", response_model=PaginatedResponse, '
          'tags=["Members"])\n'
          'def list_members(\n'
          '    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),\n'
          '    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=100),\n'
          '    search: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="Search by name '
          'or email"),\n'
          '    status: Optional[MemberStatus] = Query(None),\n'
          '    has_overdue: Optional[bool] = Query(None, description="Filter '
          'members with overdue loans"),\n'
          '    sort_by: str = Query("last_name", enum=["first_name", '
          '"last_name", "email", "created_at"]),\n'
          '    order: SortOrder = Query(SortOrder.ASC),\n'
          '):\n'
          '    """List all members with search, filter, and pagination."""\n'
          '    sync_loan_statuses()\n'
          '    members = list(db_members.values())\n'
          '\n'
          '    # Sync active loan counts\n'
          '    for m in members:\n'
          '        m["active_loans"] = sum(\n'
          '            1 for l in db_loans.values()\n'
          '            if l["member_id"] == m["id"] and l["status"] in '
          '(LoanStatus.ACTIVE, LoanStatus.OVERDUE)\n'
          '        )\n'
          '        m["outstanding_fines"] = sum(\n'
          '            l["fine_amount"] for l in db_loans.values()\n'
          '            if l["member_id"] == m["id"] and l.get("fine_amount", '
          '0) > 0 and l["status"] != LoanStatus.RETURNED\n'
          '        )\n'
          '        # Auto-expire memberships\n'
          '        if m.get("membership_expiry") and m["membership_expiry"] < '
          'date.today():\n'
          '            if m["status"] == MemberStatus.ACTIVE:\n'
          '                m["status"] = MemberStatus.EXPIRED\n'
          '\n'
          '    if search:\n'
          '        q = search.lower()\n'
          '        members = [\n'
          '            m for m in members if\n'
          '            q in m["first_name"].lower() or\n'
          '            q in m["last_name"].lower() or\n'
          '            q in m["email"].lower()\n'
          '        ]\n'
          '    if status:\n'
          '        members = [m for m in members if m["status"] == status]\n'
          '    if has_overdue is True:\n'
          '        members = [m for m in members if any(\n'
          '            l["member_id"] == m["id"] and l["status"] == '
          'LoanStatus.OVERDUE\n'
          '            for l in db_loans.values()\n'
          '        )]\n'
          '    elif has_overdue is False:\n'
          '        members = [m for m in members if not any(\n'
          '            l["member_id"] == m["id"] and l["status"] == '
          'LoanStatus.OVERDUE\n'
          '            for l in db_loans.values()\n'
          '        )]\n'
          '\n'
          '    reverse = order == SortOrder.DESC\n'
          '    members.sort(key=lambda x: (x.get(sort_by) or ""), '
          'reverse=reverse)\n'
          '    return paginate(members, page, page_size)\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get("/members/{member_id}", response_model=Member, '
          'tags=["Members"])\n'
          'def get_member(member_id: str):\n'
          '    """Get a member\'s profile."""\n'
          '    return get_member_or_404(member_id)\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get("/members/{member_id}/loans", response_model=List[Loan], '
          'tags=["Members"])\n'
          'def get_member_loans(\n'
          '    member_id: str,\n'
          '    status: Optional[LoanStatus] = Query(None),\n'
          '    include_history: bool = Query(True),\n'
          '):\n'
          '    """Get loan history for a specific member."""\n'
          '    get_member_or_404(member_id)\n'
          '    sync_loan_statuses()\n'
          '    loans = [l for l in db_loans.values() if l["member_id"] == '
          'member_id]\n'
          '    if not include_history:\n'
          '        loans = [l for l in loans if l["status"] != '
          'LoanStatus.RETURNED]\n'
          '    if status:\n'
          '        loans = [l for l in loans if l["status"] == status]\n'
          '    loans.sort(key=lambda x: x["loan_date"], reverse=True)\n'
          '    return loans\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get("/members/{member_id}/reservations", '
          'response_model=List[Reservation], tags=["Members"])\n'
          'def get_member_reservations(member_id: str):\n'
          '    """Get all reservations for a member."""\n'
          '    get_member_or_404(member_id)\n'
          '    return [r for r in db_reservations.values() if r["member_id"] '
          '== member_id]\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.put("/members/{member_id}", response_model=Member, '
          'tags=["Members"])\n'
          'def update_member(member_id: str, payload: MemberUpdate):\n'
          '    """Update member information."""\n'
          '    member = get_member_or_404(member_id)\n'
          '    updates = payload.dict(exclude_unset=True)\n'
          '    if "email" in updates:\n'
          '        conflict = [m for m in db_members.values() if m["email"] == '
          'updates["email"] and m["id"] != member_id]\n'
          '        if conflict:\n'
          '            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="Email '
          'already in use by another member")\n'
          '    member.update(updates)\n'
          '    member["full_name"] = f"{member[\'first_name\']} '
          '{member[\'last_name\']}"\n'
          '    member["updated_at"] = now()\n'
          '    return member\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.patch("/members/{member_id}/suspend", response_model=Member, '
          'tags=["Members"])\n'
          'def suspend_member(member_id: str, reason: str = Query(..., '
          'min_length=5)):\n'
          '    """Suspend a member\'s library access."""\n'
          '    member = get_member_or_404(member_id)\n'
          '    if member["status"] == MemberStatus.SUSPENDED:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Member is '
          'already suspended")\n'
          '    member["status"] = MemberStatus.SUSPENDED\n'
          '    member["suspension_reason"] = reason\n'
          '    member["updated_at"] = now()\n'
          '    logger.info(f"Member {member_id} suspended: {reason}")\n'
          '    return member\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.patch("/members/{member_id}/reinstate", response_model=Member, '
          'tags=["Members"])\n'
          'def reinstate_member(member_id: str):\n'
          '    """Reinstate a suspended or expired member."""\n'
          '    member = get_member_or_404(member_id)\n'
          '    if member["status"] == MemberStatus.ACTIVE:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Member is '
          'already active")\n'
          '    member["status"] = MemberStatus.ACTIVE\n'
          '    if not member.get("membership_expiry") or '
          'member["membership_expiry"] < date.today():\n'
          '        member["membership_expiry"] = date.today() + '
          'timedelta(days=365)\n'
          '    member.pop("suspension_reason", None)\n'
          '    member["updated_at"] = now()\n'
          '    return member\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.delete("/members/{member_id}", response_model=MessageResponse, '
          'tags=["Members"])\n'
          'def delete_member(member_id: str):\n'
          '    """Delete a member account (must have no active loans or unpaid '
          'fines)."""\n'
          '    member = get_member_or_404(member_id)\n'
          '    active_loans = [\n'
          '        l for l in db_loans.values()\n'
          '        if l["member_id"] == member_id and l["status"] in '
          '(LoanStatus.ACTIVE, LoanStatus.OVERDUE)\n'
          '    ]\n'
          '    if active_loans:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail=f"Member has '
          '{len(active_loans)} active loan(s)")\n'
          '    unpaid_fines = sum(\n'
          '        l["fine_amount"] for l in db_loans.values()\n'
          '        if l["member_id"] == member_id and l.get("fine_amount", 0) '
          '> 0\n'
          '    )\n'
          '    if unpaid_fines > 0:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail=f"Member has '
          '${unpaid_fines:.2f} in outstanding fines")\n'
          '    del db_members[member_id]\n'
          '    return {"message": f"Member \'{member[\'full_name\']}\' deleted '
          'successfully"}\n'
          '\n'
          '# '
          '──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
          '# Loans (Borrow / Return)\n'
          '# '
          '──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.post("/loans", response_model=Loan, status_code=201, '
          'tags=["Loans"])\n'
          'def borrow_book(payload: LoanCreate):\n'
          '    """\n'
          '    Issue a book to a member.\n'
          '\n'
          '    **Rules:**\n'
          '    - Member must be active\n'
          '    - Book must have available copies\n'
          '    - Member must not exceed their loan limit\n'
          '    - Member must have no outstanding fines > $10\n'
          '    """\n'
          '    book = get_book_or_404(payload.book_id)\n'
          '    member = get_member_or_404(payload.member_id)\n'
          '\n'
          '    # Guard: member status\n'
          '    if member["status"] != MemberStatus.ACTIVE:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(\n'
          '            status_code=403,\n'
          '            detail=f"Member account is \'{member[\'status\']}\'. '
          'Only ACTIVE members can borrow."\n'
          '        )\n'
          '\n'
          '    # Guard: available copies\n'
          '    if book["available_copies"] < 1:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(\n'
          '            status_code=409,\n'
          '            detail="No copies available. Consider making a '
          'reservation."\n'
          '        )\n'
          '\n'
          '    # Guard: loan limit\n'
          '    active_loan_count = sum(\n'
          '        1 for l in db_loans.values()\n'
          '        if l["member_id"] == payload.member_id\n'
          '        and l["status"] in (LoanStatus.ACTIVE, LoanStatus.OVERDUE)\n'
          '    )\n'
          '    if active_loan_count >= member["max_loans_allowed"]:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(\n'
          '            status_code=409,\n'
          '            detail=f"Member has reached their loan limit of '
          '{member[\'max_loans_allowed\']}"\n'
          '        )\n'
          '\n'
          '    # Guard: outstanding fines\n'
          '    outstanding = sum(\n'
          '        l["fine_amount"] for l in db_loans.values()\n'
          '        if l["member_id"] == payload.member_id\n'
          '        and l.get("fine_amount", 0) > 0\n'
          '        and l["status"] == LoanStatus.OVERDUE\n'
          '    )\n'
          '    if outstanding > 10.0:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(\n'
          '            status_code=403,\n'
          '            detail=f"Member has ${outstanding:.2f} in outstanding '
          'fines. Please settle before borrowing."\n'
          '        )\n'
          '\n'
          '    # Guard: already has this book on loan\n'
          '    already = any(\n'
          '        l for l in db_loans.values()\n'
          '        if l["book_id"] == payload.book_id\n'
          '        and l["member_id"] == payload.member_id\n'
          '        and l["status"] in (LoanStatus.ACTIVE, LoanStatus.OVERDUE)\n'
          '    )\n'
          '    if already:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="Member already '
          'has this book on loan")\n'
          '\n'
          '    loan_id = new_id()\n'
          '    loan_date = date.today()\n'
          '    due_date = payload.due_date or (loan_date + '
          'timedelta(days=14))\n'
          '\n'
          '    if due_date <= loan_date:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Due date must '
          'be in the future")\n'
          '\n'
          '    loan = {\n'
          '        "id": loan_id,\n'
          '        "book_id": book["id"],\n'
          '        "book_title": book["title"],\n'
          '        "member_id": member["id"],\n'
          '        "member_name": f"{member[\'first_name\']} '
          '{member[\'last_name\']}",\n'
          '        "loan_date": loan_date,\n'
          '        "due_date": due_date,\n'
          '        "return_date": None,\n'
          '        "status": LoanStatus.ACTIVE,\n'
          '        "fine_amount": 0.0,\n'
          '        "notes": payload.notes,\n'
          '        "created_at": now(),\n'
          '        "updated_at": now(),\n'
          '    }\n'
          '    db_loans[loan_id] = loan\n'
          '\n'
          '    # Decrement available copies\n'
          '    book["available_copies"] -= 1\n'
          '    book["updated_at"] = now()\n'
          '\n'
          '    # Increment member stats\n'
          '    member["active_loans"] = active_loan_count + 1\n'
          '    member["total_loans_ever"] += 1\n'
          '    member["updated_at"] = now()\n'
          '\n'
          '    logger.info(f"Loan issued: \'{book[\'title\']}\' → '
          "{member['first_name']} {member['last_name']} (due "
          '{due_date})")\n'
          '    return loan\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get("/loans", response_model=PaginatedResponse, '
          'tags=["Loans"])\n'
          'def list_loans(\n'
          '    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),\n'
          '    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=100),\n'
          '    status: Optional[LoanStatus] = Query(None),\n'
          '    member_id: Optional[str] = Query(None),\n'
          '    book_id: Optional[str] = Query(None),\n'
          '    overdue_only: bool = Query(False),\n'
          '    due_before: Optional[date] = Query(None),\n'
          '):\n'
          '    """List all loans. Filter by status, member, book, or due '
          'date."""\n'
          '    sync_loan_statuses()\n'
          '    loans = list(db_loans.values())\n'
          '    if status:\n'
          '        loans = [l for l in loans if l["status"] == status]\n'
          '    if member_id:\n'
          '        loans = [l for l in loans if l["member_id"] == member_id]\n'
          '    if book_id:\n'
          '        loans = [l for l in loans if l["book_id"] == book_id]\n'
          '    if overdue_only:\n'
          '        loans = [l for l in loans if l["status"] == '
          'LoanStatus.OVERDUE]\n'
          '    if due_before:\n'
          '        loans = [l for l in loans if l["due_date"] <= due_before]\n'
          '    loans.sort(key=lambda x: x["due_date"])\n'
          '    return paginate(loans, page, page_size)\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get("/loans/{loan_id}", response_model=Loan, tags=["Loans"])\n'
          'def get_loan(loan_id: str):\n'
          '    """Get details of a specific loan."""\n'
          '    loan = db_loans.get(loan_id)\n'
          '    if not loan:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"Loan '
          '\'{loan_id}\' not found")\n'
          '    sync_loan_statuses()\n'
          '    return loan\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.post("/loans/{loan_id}/return", response_model=Loan, '
          'tags=["Loans"])\n'
          'def return_book(\n'
          '    loan_id: str,\n'
          '    payload: LoanReturn,\n'
          '    background_tasks: BackgroundTasks,\n'
          '):\n'
          '    """\n'
          '    Process a book return.\n'
          '\n'
          '    Calculates any applicable fine, updates available copies,\n'
          '    and notifies the first member on the reservation queue.\n'
          '    """\n'
          '    loan = db_loans.get(loan_id)\n'
          '    if not loan:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"Loan '
          '\'{loan_id}\' not found")\n'
          '    if loan["status"] == LoanStatus.RETURNED:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="This book has '
          'already been returned")\n'
          '\n'
          '    return_date = date.today()\n'
          '    fine = calculate_fine(loan["due_date"], return_date)\n'
          '\n'
          '    loan["return_date"] = return_date\n'
          '    loan["status"] = LoanStatus.RETURNED\n'
          '    loan["fine_amount"] = fine\n'
          '    loan["notes"] = (loan.get("notes") or "") + (f" | Return note: '
          '{payload.notes}" if payload.notes else "")\n'
          '    loan["updated_at"] = now()\n'
          '\n'
          '    # Restore available copy\n'
          '    book = db_books.get(loan["book_id"])\n'
          '    if book:\n'
          '        book["available_copies"] = min(book["available_copies"] + '
          '1, book["total_copies"])\n'
          '        book["condition"] = payload.condition_on_return\n'
          '        book["updated_at"] = now()\n'
          '\n'
          '    # Update member stats\n'
          '    member = db_members.get(loan["member_id"])\n'
          '    if member:\n'
          '        member["active_loans"] = max(0, member["active_loans"] - '
          '1)\n'
          '        member["outstanding_fines"] = '
          'round(member.get("outstanding_fines", 0) + fine, 2)\n'
          '        member["updated_at"] = now()\n'
          '\n'
          '    # Fulfill oldest pending reservation if book is now available\n'
          '    pending_reservations = sorted(\n'
          '        [r for r in db_reservations.values()\n'
          '         if r["book_id"] == loan["book_id"] and r["status"] == '
          'ReservationStatus.PENDING],\n'
          '        key=lambda x: x["reservation_date"]\n'
          '    )\n'
          '    if pending_reservations and book and book["available_copies"] > '
          '0:\n'
          '        next_res = pending_reservations[0]\n'
          '        next_res["status"] = ReservationStatus.FULFILLED\n'
          '        next_res["expiry_date"] = now() + '
          'timedelta(hours=RESERVATION_EXPIRY_HOURS)\n'
          '        background_tasks.add_task(\n'
          '            notify_member_reservation_ready,\n'
          '            next_res["member_id"],\n'
          '            book["title"]\n'
          '        )\n'
          '        logger.info(f"Reservation fulfilled for member '
          '{next_res[\'member_id\']}: \'{book[\'title\']}\'")\n'
          '\n'
          '    overdue_info = f" Fine applied: ${fine:.2f}" if fine > 0 else " '
          'No fine."\n'
          '    logger.info(f"Book returned: \'{loan[\'book_title\']}\' by '
          '{loan[\'member_name\']}.{overdue_info}")\n'
          '    return loan\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.patch("/loans/{loan_id}/extend", response_model=Loan, '
          'tags=["Loans"])\n'
          'def extend_loan(\n'
          '    loan_id: str,\n'
          '    extra_days: int = Query(7, ge=1, le=30, description="Number of '
          'days to extend the loan"),\n'
          '):\n'
          '    """\n'
          "    Extend an active loan's due date.\n"
          '\n'
          '    **Rules:** Cannot extend overdue loans or loans with pending '
          'reservations.\n'
          '    """\n'
          '    loan = db_loans.get(loan_id)\n'
          '    if not loan:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"Loan '
          '\'{loan_id}\' not found")\n'
          '    if loan["status"] == LoanStatus.RETURNED:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Cannot extend '
          'a returned loan")\n'
          '    sync_loan_statuses()\n'
          '    if loan["status"] == LoanStatus.OVERDUE:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Cannot extend '
          'an overdue loan. Please return the book.")\n'
          '\n'
          '    # Block extension if someone has reserved this book\n'
          '    pending = [\n'
          '        r for r in db_reservations.values()\n'
          '        if r["book_id"] == loan["book_id"] and r["status"] == '
          'ReservationStatus.PENDING\n'
          '    ]\n'
          '    if pending:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(\n'
          '            status_code=409,\n'
          '            detail=f"Cannot extend: {len(pending)} member(s) have '
          'reserved this book."\n'
          '        )\n'
          '\n'
          '    loan["due_date"] = loan["due_date"] + '
          'timedelta(days=extra_days)\n'
          '    loan["updated_at"] = now()\n'
          '    logger.info(f"Loan {loan_id} extended by {extra_days} days. New '
          'due: {loan[\'due_date\']}")\n'
          '    return loan\n'
          '\n'
          '# '
          '──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
          '# Reservations\n'
          '# '
          '──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.post("/reservations", response_model=Reservation, '
          'status_code=201, tags=["Reservations"])\n'
          'def create_reservation(payload: ReservationCreate):\n'
          '    """\n'
          '    Reserve a book that is currently unavailable.\n'
          '\n'
          '    Members are notified when the book becomes available.\n'
          '    """\n'
          '    book = get_book_or_404(payload.book_id)\n'
          '    member = get_member_or_404(payload.member_id)\n'
          '\n'
          '    if member["status"] != MemberStatus.ACTIVE:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Only active '
          'members can make reservations")\n'
          '\n'
          '    if book["available_copies"] > 0:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(\n'
          '            status_code=400,\n'
          '            detail="Book is currently available. Please borrow it '
          'directly instead of reserving."\n'
          '        )\n'
          '\n'
          '    # Check for duplicate reservation\n'
          '    existing = any(\n'
          '        r for r in db_reservations.values()\n'
          '        if r["book_id"] == payload.book_id\n'
          '        and r["member_id"] == payload.member_id\n'
          '        and r["status"] == ReservationStatus.PENDING\n'
          '    )\n'
          '    if existing:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="Member already '
          'has a pending reservation for this book")\n'
          '\n'
          '    queue_position = sum(\n'
          '        1 for r in db_reservations.values()\n'
          '        if r["book_id"] == payload.book_id and r["status"] == '
          'ReservationStatus.PENDING\n'
          '    ) + 1\n'
          '\n'
          '    res_id = new_id()\n'
          '    res_time = now()\n'
          '    reservation = {\n'
          '        "id": res_id,\n'
          '        "book_id": book["id"],\n'
          '        "book_title": book["title"],\n'
          '        "member_id": member["id"],\n'
          '        "member_name": f"{member[\'first_name\']} '
          '{member[\'last_name\']}",\n'
          '        "reservation_date": res_time,\n'
          '        "expiry_date": res_time + '
          'timedelta(hours=RESERVATION_EXPIRY_HOURS),\n'
          '        "status": ReservationStatus.PENDING,\n'
          '        "queue_position": queue_position,\n'
          '        "notes": payload.notes,\n'
          '        "created_at": res_time,\n'
          '    }\n'
          '    db_reservations[res_id] = reservation\n'
          '    logger.info(f"Reservation created: \'{book[\'title\']}\' for '
          '{member[\'first_name\']} (queue #{queue_position})")\n'
          '    return reservation\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get("/reservations", response_model=PaginatedResponse, '
          'tags=["Reservations"])\n'
          'def list_reservations(\n'
          '    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),\n'
          '    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=100),\n'
          '    status: Optional[ReservationStatus] = Query(None),\n'
          '    member_id: Optional[str] = Query(None),\n'
          '    book_id: Optional[str] = Query(None),\n'
          '):\n'
          '    """List all reservations with optional filters."""\n'
          '    reservations = list(db_reservations.values())\n'
          '    # Auto-expire old unfulfilled reservations\n'
          '    for r in reservations:\n'
          '        if r["status"] == ReservationStatus.PENDING and '
          'r["expiry_date"] < now():\n'
          '            r["status"] = ReservationStatus.EXPIRED\n'
          '\n'
          '    if status:\n'
          '        reservations = [r for r in reservations if r["status"] == '
          'status]\n'
          '    if member_id:\n'
          '        reservations = [r for r in reservations if r["member_id"] '
          '== member_id]\n'
          '    if book_id:\n'
          '        reservations = [r for r in reservations if r["book_id"] == '
          'book_id]\n'
          '\n'
          '    reservations.sort(key=lambda x: x["reservation_date"])\n'
          '    return paginate(reservations, page, page_size)\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get("/reservations/{reservation_id}", '
          'response_model=Reservation, tags=["Reservations"])\n'
          'def get_reservation(reservation_id: str):\n'
          '    """Get a specific reservation by ID."""\n'
          '    res = db_reservations.get(reservation_id)\n'
          '    if not res:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"Reservation '
          '\'{reservation_id}\' not found")\n'
          '    return res\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.delete("/reservations/{reservation_id}", '
          'response_model=MessageResponse, tags=["Reservations"])\n'
          'def cancel_reservation(reservation_id: str):\n'
          '    """Cancel a pending reservation."""\n'
          '    res = db_reservations.get(reservation_id)\n'
          '    if not res:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"Reservation '
          '\'{reservation_id}\' not found")\n'
          '    if res["status"] != ReservationStatus.PENDING:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=f"Cannot cancel '
          'a \'{res[\'status\']}\' reservation")\n'
          '    res["status"] = ReservationStatus.CANCELLED\n'
          '    return {"message": "Reservation cancelled successfully", '
          '"detail": f"Book: {res[\'book_title\']}"}\n'
          '\n'
          '# '
          '──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
          '# Reports & Statistics\n'
          '# '
          '──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get("/reports/stats", response_model=LibraryStats, '
          'tags=["Reports"])\n'
          'def get_library_stats():\n'
          '    """High-level statistics snapshot of the entire library."""\n'
          '    sync_loan_statuses()\n'
          '\n'
          '    total_copies = sum(b["total_copies"] for b in '
          'db_books.values())\n'
          '    available_copies = sum(b["available_copies"] for b in '
          'db_books.values())\n'
          '    active_loans = [l for l in db_loans.values() if l["status"] in '
          '(LoanStatus.ACTIVE, LoanStatus.OVERDUE)]\n'
          '    overdue_loans = [l for l in db_loans.values() if l["status"] == '
          'LoanStatus.OVERDUE]\n'
          '    active_members = [m for m in db_members.values() if m["status"] '
          '== MemberStatus.ACTIVE]\n'
          '    pending_reservations = [r for r in db_reservations.values() if '
          'r["status"] == ReservationStatus.PENDING]\n'
          '    outstanding_fines = sum(l.get("fine_amount", 0) for l in '
          'overdue_loans)\n'
          '\n'
          '    # Most borrowed book\n'
          '    from collections import Counter\n'
          '    book_borrows = Counter(l["book_id"] for l in '
          'db_loans.values())\n'
          '    most_borrowed_id = book_borrows.most_common(1)[0][0] if '
          'book_borrows else None\n'
          '    most_borrowed = db_books[most_borrowed_id]["title"] if '
          'most_borrowed_id else None\n'
          '\n'
          '    # Most active member\n'
          '    member_borrows = Counter(l["member_id"] for l in '
          'db_loans.values())\n'
          '    most_active_id = member_borrows.most_common(1)[0][0] if '
          'member_borrows else None\n'
          '    most_active = (\n'
          '        f"{db_members[most_active_id][\'first_name\']} '
          '{db_members[most_active_id][\'last_name\']}"\n'
          '        if most_active_id and most_active_id in db_members else '
          'None\n'
          '    )\n'
          '\n'
          '    return {\n'
          '        "total_books": len(db_books),\n'
          '        "total_copies": total_copies,\n'
          '        "available_copies": available_copies,\n'
          '        "total_members": len(db_members),\n'
          '        "active_members": len(active_members),\n'
          '        "active_loans": len(active_loans),\n'
          '        "overdue_loans": len(overdue_loans),\n'
          '        "total_reservations": len(pending_reservations),\n'
          '        "total_fines_outstanding": round(outstanding_fines, 2),\n'
          '        "most_borrowed_book": most_borrowed,\n'
          '        "most_active_member": most_active,\n'
          '    }\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get("/reports/overdue", response_model=List[Loan], '
          'tags=["Reports"])\n'
          'def get_overdue_loans():\n'
          '    """Get all currently overdue loans with calculated fines."""\n'
          '    sync_loan_statuses()\n'
          '    overdue = [l for l in db_loans.values() if l["status"] == '
          'LoanStatus.OVERDUE]\n'
          '    overdue.sort(key=lambda x: x["due_date"])\n'
          '    return overdue\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get("/reports/popular-books", tags=["Reports"])\n'
          'def get_popular_books(limit: int = Query(10, ge=1, le=50)):\n'
          '    """Get the most borrowed books ranked by total loan count."""\n'
          '    from collections import Counter\n'
          '    counts = Counter(l["book_id"] for l in db_loans.values())\n'
          '    result = []\n'
          '    for book_id, count in counts.most_common(limit):\n'
          '        if book_id in db_books:\n'
          '            result.append({\n'
          '                "book": db_books[book_id],\n'
          '                "total_loans": count,\n'
          '                "currently_available": '
          'db_books[book_id]["available_copies"],\n'
          '            })\n'
          '    return result\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get("/reports/active-members", tags=["Reports"])\n'
          'def get_most_active_members(limit: int = Query(10, ge=1, le=50)):\n'
          '    """Get members ranked by number of loans taken."""\n'
          '    from collections import Counter\n'
          '    counts = Counter(l["member_id"] for l in db_loans.values())\n'
          '    result = []\n'
          '    for member_id, count in counts.most_common(limit):\n'
          '        if member_id in db_members:\n'
          '            result.append({\n'
          '                "member": db_members[member_id],\n'
          '                "total_loans": count,\n'
          '                "active_loans": sum(\n'
          '                    1 for l in db_loans.values()\n'
          '                    if l["member_id"] == member_id and l["status"] '
          'in (LoanStatus.ACTIVE, LoanStatus.OVERDUE)\n'
          '                ),\n'
          '            })\n'
          '    return result\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get("/reports/fines", tags=["Reports"])\n'
          'def get_fines_report():\n'
          '    """Full report of all outstanding fines grouped by member."""\n'
          '    sync_loan_statuses()\n'
          '    fines_by_member: Dict[str, dict] = {}\n'
          '    for loan in db_loans.values():\n'
          '        if loan.get("fine_amount", 0) > 0 and loan["status"] != '
          'LoanStatus.RETURNED:\n'
          '            mid = loan["member_id"]\n'
          '            if mid not in fines_by_member:\n'
          '                member = db_members.get(mid, {})\n'
          '                fines_by_member[mid] = {\n'
          '                    "member_id": mid,\n'
          '                    "member_name": loan["member_name"],\n'
          '                    "email": member.get("email"),\n'
          '                    "total_fine": 0.0,\n'
          '                    "overdue_loans": [],\n'
          '                }\n'
          '            fines_by_member[mid]["total_fine"] = round(\n'
          '                fines_by_member[mid]["total_fine"] + '
          'loan["fine_amount"], 2\n'
          '            )\n'
          '            fines_by_member[mid]["overdue_loans"].append({\n'
          '                "loan_id": loan["id"],\n'
          '                "book_title": loan["book_title"],\n'
          '                "due_date": loan["due_date"],\n'
          '                "fine": loan["fine_amount"],\n'
          '            })\n'
          '    report = sorted(fines_by_member.values(), key=lambda x: '
          'x["total_fine"], reverse=True)\n'
          '    return {"total_fines_outstanding": round(sum(r["total_fine"] '
          'for r in report), 2), "members": report}\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get("/reports/inventory", tags=["Reports"])\n'
          'def get_inventory_report():\n'
          '    """Full inventory: every book with copy availability '
          'breakdown."""\n'
          '    sync_loan_statuses()\n'
          '    inventory = []\n'
          '    for book in db_books.values():\n'
          '        on_loan = sum(\n'
          '            1 for l in db_loans.values()\n'
          '            if l["book_id"] == book["id"] and l["status"] in '
          '(LoanStatus.ACTIVE, LoanStatus.OVERDUE)\n'
          '        )\n'
          '        overdue = sum(\n'
          '            1 for l in db_loans.values()\n'
          '            if l["book_id"] == book["id"] and l["status"] == '
          'LoanStatus.OVERDUE\n'
          '        )\n'
          '        reserved = sum(\n'
          '            1 for r in db_reservations.values()\n'
          '            if r["book_id"] == book["id"] and r["status"] == '
          'ReservationStatus.PENDING\n'
          '        )\n'
          '        inventory.append({\n'
          '            "book_id": book["id"],\n'
          '            "title": book["title"],\n'
          '            "isbn": book["isbn"],\n'
          '            "total_copies": book["total_copies"],\n'
          '            "available_copies": book["available_copies"],\n'
          '            "on_loan": on_loan,\n'
          '            "overdue_copies": overdue,\n'
          '            "pending_reservations": reserved,\n'
          '            "condition": book.get("condition"),\n'
          '            "location_shelf": book.get("location_shelf"),\n'
          '        })\n'
          '    inventory.sort(key=lambda x: x["available_copies"])\n'
          '    return {"total_books": len(inventory), "inventory": inventory}\n'
          '```\n'
          '\n'
          '---\n'
          '\n'
          "## 🗂️ What's Covered\n"
          '\n'
          '| Domain | Endpoints | Key Features |\n'
          '|---|---|---|\n'
          '| **Authors** | `POST /authors` `GET /authors` `GET /authors/{id}` '
          '`GET /authors/{id}/books` `PUT` `DELETE` | Search, sort, '
          'bibliography |\n'
          '| **Categories** | `POST /categories` `GET /categories` `GET '
          '/categories/{id}/books` `PUT` `DELETE` | Nested categories, book '
          'counts |\n'
          '| **Books** | `POST /books` `GET /books` `GET /books/{id}` `GET '

          'filtering, ISBN lookup, copy management |\n'
          '| **Members** | `POST /members` `GET /members` `GET /members/{id}` '

          '| **Reports** | `GET /reports/stats` `/overdue` `/popular-books` '
          '`/active-members` `/fines` `/inventory` | Full analytics suite |\n'
          '\n'
          '### 🔐 Business Rules Enforced\n'
          '- Members cannot borrow if **suspended/expired**\n'
          '- Members cannot borrow if **fines > $10**\n'
          '- Members cannot exceed their **max loan limit**\n'
          '- Books cannot be deleted with **active loans**\n'
          '- Loans cannot be **extended if someone reserved** the book\n'
          '- ISBNs must be **unique** across the catalog',

          '\n'
