          '    first_name: str = Field(..., min_length=1, max_length=80)\n'
          '    last_name: str = Field(..., min_length=1, max_length=80)\n'
          '    biography: Optional[str] = None\n'
          '    birth_date: Optional[date] = None\n'
          '    death_date: Optional[date] = None\n'
          '    nationality: Optional[str] = None\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'class AuthorCreate(AuthorBase):\n'
          '    pass\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'class AuthorUpdate(BaseModel):\n'
          '    first_name: Optional[str] = None\n'
          '    last_name: Optional[str] = None\n'
          '    biography: Optional[str] = None\n'
          '    birth_date: Optional[date] = None\n'
          '    death_date: Optional[date] = None\n'
          '    nationality: Optional[str] = None\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'class AuthorOut(AuthorBase):\n'
          '    model_config = ConfigDict(from_attributes=True)\n'
          '    id: int\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'class CategoryBase(BaseModel):\n'
          '    name: str = Field(..., min_length=1, max_length=80)\n'
          '    description: Optional[str] = None\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'class CategoryCreate(CategoryBase):\n'
          '    pass\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'class CategoryOut(CategoryBase):\n'
          '    model_config = ConfigDict(from_attributes=True)\n'
          '    id: int\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'class BookBase(BaseModel):\n'
          '    isbn: str = Field(..., min_length=10, max_length=17)\n'
          '    title: str = Field(..., min_length=1, max_length=255)\n'
          '    subtitle: Optional[str] = None\n'
          '    description: Optional[str] = None\n'
          '    publisher: Optional[str] = None\n'
          '    publication_year: Optional[int] = Field(None, ge=1000, '
          'le=2100)\n'
          '    language: str = "English"\n'
          '    page_count: Optional[int] = Field(None, ge=1)\n'
          '    total_copies: int = Field(1, ge=1)\n'
          '    cover_url: Optional[str] = None\n'
          '    location: Optional[str] = None\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'class BookCreate(BookBase):\n'
          '    author_ids: List[int] = Field(default_factory=list)\n'
          '    category_ids: List[int] = Field(default_factory=list)\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'class BookUpdate(BaseModel):\n'
          '    title: Optional[str] = None\n'
          '    subtitle: Optional[str] = None\n'
          '    description: Optional[str] = None\n'
          '    publisher: Optional[str] = None\n'
          '    publication_year: Optional[int] = None\n'
          '    language: Optional[str] = None\n'
          '    page_count: Optional[int] = None\n'
          '    total_copies: Optional[int] = None\n'
          '    cover_url: Optional[str] = None\n'
          '    location: Optional[str] = None\n'
          '    author_ids: Optional[List[int]] = None\n'
          '    category_ids: Optional[List[int]] = None\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'class BookOut(BookBase):\n'
          '    model_config = ConfigDict(from_attributes=True)\n'
          '    id: int\n'
          '    available_copies: int\n'
          '    authors: List[AuthorOut] = []\n'
          '    categories: List[CategoryOut] = []\n'
          '    created_at: datetime\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'class LoanCreate(BaseModel):\n'
          '    book_id: int\n'
          '    member_id: int\n'
          '    loan_days: int = Field(settings.DEFAULT_LOAN_DAYS, ge=1, '
          'le=90)\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'class LoanOut(BaseModel):\n'
          '    model_config = ConfigDict(from_attributes=True)\n'
          '    id: int\n'
          '    book_id: int\n'
          '    member_id: int\n'
          '    loan_date: datetime\n'
          '    due_date: datetime\n'
          '    return_date: Optional[datetime]\n'
          '    status: LoanStatus\n'
          '    renewal_count: int\n'
          '    fine_amount: float\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'class ReservationCreate(BaseModel):\n'
          '    book_id: int\n'
          '    days_valid: int = Field(7, ge=1, le=30)\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'class ReservationOut(BaseModel):\n'
          '    model_config = ConfigDict(from_attributes=True)\n'
          '    id: int\n'
          '    book_id: int\n'
          '    member_id: int\n'
          '    reservation_date: datetime\n'
          '    expiry_date: datetime\n'
          '    status: ReservationStatus\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'class ReviewCreate(BaseModel):\n'
          '    rating: int = Field(..., ge=1, le=5)\n'
          '    comment: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=2000)\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'class ReviewOut(BaseModel):\n'
          '    model_config = ConfigDict(from_attributes=True)\n'
          '    id: int\n'
          '    book_id: int\n'
          '    member_id: int\n'
          '    rating: int\n'
          '    comment: Optional[str]\n'
          '    created_at: datetime\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'class PaginatedResponse(BaseModel):\n'
          '    total: int\n'
          '    page: int\n'
          '    page_size: int\n'
          '    items: list\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'class StatisticsOut(BaseModel):\n'
          '    total_books: int\n'
          '    total_unique_titles: int\n'
          '    total_members: int\n'
          '    active_loans: int\n'
          '    overdue_loans: int\n'
          '    pending_reservations: int\n'
          '    total_fines_outstanding: float\n'
          '    most_borrowed_books: List[dict]\n'
          '    most_active_members: List[dict]\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '# '
          '---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n'
          '# Security / Auth\n'
          '# '
          '---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n'
          'pwd_context = CryptContext(schemes=["bcrypt"], deprecated="auto")\n'
          'oauth2_scheme = '
          'OAuth2PasswordBearer(tokenUrl=f"{settings.API_PREFIX}/auth/login")\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'def hash_password(password: str) -> str:\n'
          '    return pwd_context.hash(password)\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'def verify_password(plain: str, hashed: str) -> bool:\n'
          '    return pwd_context.verify(plain, hashed)\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'def create_access_token(data: dict, expires_delta: '
          'Optional[timedelta] = None) -> str:\n'
          '    to_encode = data.copy()\n'
          '    expire = datetime.utcnow() + (expires_delta or '
          'timedelta(minutes=settings.ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES))\n'
          '    to_encode.update({"exp": expire})\n'
          '    return jwt.encode(to_encode, settings.SECRET_KEY, '
          'algorithm=settings.ALGORITHM)\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'def get_current_user(\n'
          '    token: Annotated[str, Depends(oauth2_scheme)],\n'
          '    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n'
          ') -> User:\n'
          '    credentials_exception = HTTPException(\n'
          '        status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,\n'
          '        detail="Could not validate credentials",\n'
          '        headers={"WWW-Authenticate": "Bearer"},\n'
          '    )\n'
          '    try:\n'
          '        payload = jwt.decode(token, settings.SECRET_KEY, '
          'algorithms=[settings.ALGORITHM])\n'
          '        username: str = payload.get("sub")\n'
          '        if username is None:\n'
          '            raise credentials_exception\n'
          '    except JWTError:\n'
          '        raise credentials_exception\n'
          '    user = db.query(User).filter(User.username == '
          'username).first()\n'
          '    if user is None or not user.is_active:\n'
          '        raise credentials_exception\n'
          '    return user\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'def require_roles(*roles: UserRole):\n'
          '    def role_checker(current: User = Depends(get_current_user)) -> '
          'User:\n'
          '        if current.role not in [r.value for r in roles]:\n'
          '            raise HTTPException(\n'
          '                status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,\n'
          '                detail=f"Requires one of roles: {[r.value for r in '
          'roles]}",\n'
          '            )\n'
          '        return current\n'
          '    return role_checker\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'require_staff = require_roles(UserRole.ADMIN, UserRole.LIBRARIAN)\n'
          'require_admin = require_roles(UserRole.ADMIN)\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '# '
          '---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n'
          '# Lifespan / App Initialization\n'
          '# '
          '---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n'
          '@asynccontextmanager\n'
          'async def lifespan(app: FastAPI):\n'
          '    logger.info("Starting up Library Management API…")\n'
          '    Base.metadata.create_all(bind=engine)\n'
          '    # Seed an admin user if none exists\n'
          '    db = SessionLocal()\n'
          '    try:\n'
          '        if not db.query(User).filter(User.role == '
          'UserRole.ADMIN.value).first():\n'
          '            admin = User(\n'
          '                email="admin@library.local",\n'
          '                username="admin",\n'
          '                full_name="Library Admin",\n'
          '                hashed_password=hash_password("admin12345"),\n'
          '                role=UserRole.ADMIN.value,\n'
          '            )\n'
          '            db.add(admin)\n'
          '            db.commit()\n'
          '            logger.info("Seeded default admin user: admin / '
          'admin12345")\n'
          '    finally:\n'
          '        db.close()\n'
          '    yield\n'
          '    logger.info("Shutting down Library Management API…")\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'app = FastAPI(\n'
          '    title=settings.PROJECT_NAME,\n'
          '    version=settings.VERSION,\n'
          '    description="A complete REST API for a Library Management '
          'System.",\n'
          '    lifespan=lifespan,\n'
          ')\n'
          '\n'
          'app.add_middleware(\n'
          '    CORSMiddleware,\n'
          '    allow_origins=["*"],\n'
          '    allow_credentials=True,\n'
          '    allow_methods=["*"],\n'
          '    allow_headers=["*"],\n'
          ')\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '# '
          '---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n'
          '# Exception Handlers\n'
          '# '
          '---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n'
          '@app.exception_handler(RequestValidationError)\n'
          'async def validation_exception_handler(request: Request, exc: '
          'RequestValidationError):\n'
          '    return JSONResponse(\n'
          '        status_code=422,\n'
          '        content={"detail": "Validation error", "errors": '
          'exc.errors()},\n'
          '    )\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '# '
          '---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n'
          '# Utility / Background Tasks\n'
          '# '
          '---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n'
          'def calculate_overdue_fine(loan: Loan) -> float:\n'
          '    if loan.return_date and loan.return_date <= loan.due_date:\n'
          '        return 0.0\n'
          '    reference = loan.return_date or datetime.utcnow()\n'
          '    if reference <= loan.due_date:\n'
          '        return 0.0\n'
          '    overdue_days = (reference - loan.due_date).days\n'
          '    return round(overdue_days * settings.OVERDUE_FINE_PER_DAY, 2)\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'def update_overdue_loans(db: Session) -> int:\n'
          '    now = datetime.utcnow()\n'
          '    overdue = db.query(Loan).filter(\n'
          '        Loan.status == LoanStatus.ACTIVE.value,\n'
          '        Loan.due_date < now,\n'
          '    ).all()\n'
          '    for loan in overdue:\n'
          '        loan.status = LoanStatus.OVERDUE.value\n'
          '        loan.fine_amount = calculate_overdue_fine(loan)\n'
          '    db.commit()\n'
          '    return len(overdue)\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '# '
          '---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n'
          '# Root & Health\n'
          '# '
          '---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n'
          '@app.get("/", tags=["root"])\n'
          'def root():\n'
          '    return {\n'
          '        "name": settings.PROJECT_NAME,\n'
          '        "version": settings.VERSION,\n'
          '        "docs_url": "/docs",\n'
          '        "openapi_url": "/openapi.json",\n'
          '    }\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get("/health", tags=["root"])\n'
          'def health():\n'
          '    return {"status": "ok", "time": datetime.utcnow().isoformat()}\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '# '
          '---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n'
          '# Auth Endpoints\n'
          '# '
          '---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n'
          '@app.post(f"{settings.API_PREFIX}/auth/register", '
          'response_model=UserOut, status_code=201, tags=["auth"])\n'
          'def register(payload: UserCreate, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):\n'
          '    if db.query(User).filter(or_(User.email == payload.email, '
          'User.username == payload.username)).first():\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="Email or '
          'username already exists")\n'
          '    user = User(\n'
          '        email=payload.email,\n'
          '        username=payload.username,\n'
          '        full_name=payload.full_name,\n'
          '        hashed_password=hash_password(payload.password),\n'
          '        phone=payload.phone,\n'
          '        address=payload.address,\n'
          '        role=UserRole.MEMBER.value,  # only admins can elevate '
          'later\n'
          '    )\n'
          '    db.add(user)\n'
          '    db.commit()\n'
          '    db.refresh(user)\n'
          '    return user\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.post(f"{settings.API_PREFIX}/auth/login", '
          'response_model=Token, tags=["auth"])\n'
          'def login(form: OAuth2PasswordRequestForm = Depends(), db: Session '
          '= Depends(get_db)):\n'
          '    user = db.query(User).filter(User.username == '
          'form.username).first()\n'
          '    if not user or not verify_password(form.password, '
          'user.hashed_password):\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Invalid '
          'credentials")\n'
          '    if not user.is_active:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Account is '
          'disabled")\n'
          '    token = create_access_token({"sub": user.username, "role": '
          'user.role})\n'
          '    return Token(access_token=token, '
          'expires_in=settings.ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES * 60)\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get(f"{settings.API_PREFIX}/auth/me", response_model=UserOut, '
          'tags=["auth"])\n'
          'def whoami(current: User = Depends(get_current_user)):\n'
          '    return current\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '# '
          '---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n'
          '# Users\n'
          '# '
          '---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n'
          '@app.get(f"{settings.API_PREFIX}/users", '
          'response_model=PaginatedResponse, tags=["users"])\n'
          'def list_users(\n'
          '    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),\n'
          '    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=100),\n'
          '    role: Optional[UserRole] = None,\n'
          '    search: Optional[str] = None,\n'
          '    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n'
          '    _: User = Depends(require_staff),\n'
          '):\n'
          '    query = db.query(User)\n'
          '    if role:\n'
          '        query = query.filter(User.role == role.value)\n'
          '    if search:\n'
          '        like = f"%{search}%"\n'
          '        query = query.filter(or_(User.email.ilike(like), '
          'User.username.ilike(like), User.full_name.ilike(like)))\n'
          '    total = query.count()\n'
          '    items = query.offset((page - 1) * '
          'page_size).limit(page_size).all()\n'
          '    return PaginatedResponse(\n'
          '        total=total, page=page, page_size=page_size,\n'
          '        items=[UserOut.model_validate(u) for u in items],\n'
          '    )\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get(f"{settings.API_PREFIX}/users/{{user_id}}", '
          'response_model=UserOut, tags=["users"])\n'
          'def get_user(user_id: int, db: Session = Depends(get_db), current: '
          'User = Depends(get_current_user)):\n'
          '    if current.id != user_id and current.role not in '
          '(UserRole.ADMIN.value, UserRole.LIBRARIAN.value):\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Not '
          'authorized")\n'
          '    user = db.query(User).filter(User.id == user_id).first()\n'
          '    if not user:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="User not '
          'found")\n'
          '    return user\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.patch(f"{settings.API_PREFIX}/users/{{user_id}}", '
          'response_model=UserOut, tags=["users"])\n'
          'def update_user(\n'
          '    user_id: int,\n'
          '    payload: UserUpdate,\n'
          '    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n'
          '    current: User = Depends(get_current_user),\n'
          '):\n'
          '    if current.id != user_id and current.role != '
          'UserRole.ADMIN.value:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Not '
          'authorized")\n'
          '    user = db.query(User).filter(User.id == user_id).first()\n'
          '    if not user:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="User not '
          'found")\n'
          '    for k, v in payload.model_dump(exclude_unset=True).items():\n'
          '        setattr(user, k, v)\n'
          '    db.commit()\n'
          '    db.refresh(user)\n'
          '    return user\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.delete(f"{settings.API_PREFIX}/users/{{user_id}}", '
          'status_code=204, tags=["users"])\n'
          'def delete_user(user_id: int, db: Session = Depends(get_db), _: '
          'User = Depends(require_admin)):\n'
          '    user = db.query(User).filter(User.id == user_id).first()\n'
          '    if not user:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="User not '
          'found")\n'
          '    db.delete(user)\n'
          '    db.commit()\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '# '
          '---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n'
          '# Authors\n'
          '# '
          '---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n'
          '@app.post(f"{settings.API_PREFIX}/authors", '
          'response_model=AuthorOut, status_code=201, tags=["authors"])\n'
          'def create_author(payload: AuthorCreate, db: Session = '
          'Depends(get_db), _: User = Depends(require_staff)):\n'
          '    author = Author(**payload.model_dump())\n'
          '    db.add(author)\n'
          '    db.commit()\n'
          '    db.refresh(author)\n'
          '    return author\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get(f"{settings.API_PREFIX}/authors", '
          'response_model=PaginatedResponse, tags=["authors"])\n'
          'def list_authors(\n'
          '    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),\n'
          '    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=100),\n'
          '    search: Optional[str] = None,\n'
          '    nationality: Optional[str] = None,\n'
          '    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n'
          '):\n'
          '    query = db.query(Author)\n'
          '    if search:\n'
          '        like = f"%{search}%"\n'
          '        query = query.filter(or_(Author.first_name.ilike(like), '
          'Author.last_name.ilike(like)))\n'
          '    if nationality:\n'
          '        query = '
          'query.filter(Author.nationality.ilike(f"%{nationality}%"))\n'
          '    total = query.count()\n'
          '    items = query.order_by(Author.last_name).offset((page - 1) * '
          'page_size).limit(page_size).all()\n'
          '    return PaginatedResponse(\n'
          '        total=total, page=page, page_size=page_size,\n'
          '        items=[AuthorOut.model_validate(a) for a in items],\n'
          '    )\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get(f"{settings.API_PREFIX}/authors/{{author_id}}", '
          'response_model=AuthorOut, tags=["authors"])\n'
          'def get_author(author_id: int, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):\n'
          '    author = db.query(Author).filter(Author.id == '
          'author_id).first()\n'
          '    if not author:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Author not '
          'found")\n'
          '    return author\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.patch(f"{settings.API_PREFIX}/authors/{{author_id}}", '
          'response_model=AuthorOut, tags=["authors"])\n'
          'def update_author(\n'
          '    author_id: int,\n'
          '    payload: AuthorUpdate,\n'
          '    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n'
          '    _: User = Depends(require_staff),\n'
          '):\n'
          '    author = db.query(Author).filter(Author.id == '
          'author_id).first()\n'
          '    if not author:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Author not '
          'found")\n'
          '    for k, v in payload.model_dump(exclude_unset=True).items():\n'
          '        setattr(author, k, v)\n'
          '    db.commit()\n'
          '    db.refresh(author)\n'
          '    return author\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.delete(f"{settings.API_PREFIX}/authors/{{author_id}}", '
          'status_code=204, tags=["authors"])\n'
          'def delete_author(author_id: int, db: Session = Depends(get_db), _: '
          'User = Depends(require_admin)):\n'
          '    author = db.query(Author).filter(Author.id == '
          'author_id).first()\n'
          '    if not author:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Author not '
          'found")\n'
          '    db.delete(author)\n'
          '    db.commit()\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get(f"{settings.API_PREFIX}/authors/{{author_id}}/books", '
          'response_model=List[BookOut], tags=["authors"])\n'
          'def author_books(author_id: int, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):\n'
          '    author = '
          'db.query(Author).options(joinedload(Author.books)).filter(Author.id '
          '== author_id).first()\n'
          '    if not author:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Author not '
          'found")\n'
          '    return author.books\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '# '
          '---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n'
          '# Categories\n'
          '# '
          '---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n'
          '@app.post(f"{settings.API_PREFIX}/categories", '
          'response_model=CategoryOut, status_code=201, tags=["categories"])\n'
          'def create_category(payload: CategoryCreate, db: Session = '
          'Depends(get_db), _: User = Depends(require_staff)):\n'
          '    if db.query(Category).filter(Category.name == '
          'payload.name).first():\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="Category '
          'already exists")\n'
          '    cat = Category(**payload.model_dump())\n'
          '    db.add(cat)\n'
          '    db.commit()\n'
          '    db.refresh(cat)\n'
          '    return cat\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get(f"{settings.API_PREFIX}/categories", '
          'response_model=List[CategoryOut], tags=["categories"])\n'
          'def list_categories(db: Session = Depends(get_db)):\n'
          '    return db.query(Category).order_by(Category.name).all()\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.delete(f"{settings.API_PREFIX}/categories/{{category_id}}", '
          'status_code=204, tags=["categories"])\n'
          'def delete_category(category_id: int, db: Session = '
          'Depends(get_db), _: User = Depends(require_admin)):\n'
          '    cat = db.query(Category).filter(Category.id == '
          'category_id).first()\n'
          '    if not cat:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Category not '
          'found")\n'
          '    db.delete(cat)\n'
          '    db.commit()\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '# '
          '---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n'
          '# Books\n'
          '# '
          '---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n'
          '@app.post(f"{settings.API_PREFIX}/books", response_model=BookOut, '
          'status_code=201, tags=["books"])\n'
          'def create_book(payload: BookCreate, db: Session = Depends(get_db), '
          '_: User = Depends(require_staff)):\n'
          '    if db.query(Book).filter(Book.isbn == payload.isbn).first():\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="ISBN already '
          'exists")\n'
          '    book = Book(**payload.model_dump(exclude={"author_ids", '
          '"category_ids"}))\n'
          '    book.available_copies = book.total_copies\n'
          '    if payload.author_ids:\n'
          '        book.authors = '
          'db.query(Author).filter(Author.id.in_(payload.author_ids)).all()\n'
          '    if payload.category_ids:\n'
          '        book.categories = '
          'db.query(Category).filter(Category.id.in_(payload.category_ids)).all()\n'
          '    db.add(book)\n'
          '    db.commit()\n'
          '    db.refresh(book)\n'
          '    return book\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get(f"{settings.API_PREFIX}/books", '
          'response_model=PaginatedResponse, tags=["books"])\n'
          'def list_books(\n'
          '    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),\n'
          '    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=100),\n'
          '    search: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="Search across '
          'title, subtitle, isbn"),\n'
          '    author_id: Optional[int] = None,\n'
          '    category_id: Optional[int] = None,\n'
          '    language: Optional[str] = None,\n'
          '    available_only: bool = False,\n'
          '    year_from: Optional[int] = None,\n'
          '    year_to: Optional[int] = None,\n'
          '    sort_by: str = Query("title", '
          'pattern="^(title|publication_year|created_at)$"),\n'
          '    order: str = Query("asc", pattern="^(asc|desc)$"),\n'
          '    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n'
          '):\n'
          '    query = db.query(Book)\n'
          '    if search:\n'
          '        like = f"%{search}%"\n'
          '        query = query.filter(or_(\n'
          '            Book.title.ilike(like),\n'
          '            Book.subtitle.ilike(like),\n'
          '            Book.isbn.ilike(like),\n'
          '        ))\n'
          '    if author_id:\n'
          '        query = query.join(Book.authors).filter(Author.id == '
          'author_id)\n'
          '    if category_id:\n'
          '        query = query.join(Book.categories).filter(Category.id == '
          'category_id)\n'
          '    if language:\n'
          '        query = query.filter(Book.language.ilike(f"%{language}%"))\n'
          '    if available_only:\n'
          '        query = query.filter(Book.available_copies > 0)\n'
          '    if year_from:\n'
          '        query = query.filter(Book.publication_year >= year_from)\n'
          '    if year_to:\n'
          '        query = query.filter(Book.publication_year <= year_to)\n'
          '\n'
          '    sort_col = getattr(Book, sort_by)\n'
          '    query = query.order_by(sort_col.desc() if order == "desc" else '
          'sort_col.asc())\n'
          '\n'
          '    total = query.count()\n'
          '    items = query.offset((page - 1) * '
          'page_size).limit(page_size).all()\n'
          '    return PaginatedResponse(\n'
          '        total=total, page=page, page_size=page_size,\n'
          '        items=[BookOut.model_validate(b) for b in items],\n'
          '    )\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get(f"{settings.API_PREFIX}/books/{{book_id}}", '
          'response_model=BookOut, tags=["books"])\n'
          'def get_book(book_id: int, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):\n'
          '    book = db.query(Book).filter(Book.id == book_id).first()\n'
          '    if not book:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Book not '
          'found")\n'
          '    return book\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get(f"{settings.API_PREFIX}/books/isbn/{{isbn}}", '
          'response_model=BookOut, tags=["books"])\n'
          'def get_book_by_isbn(isbn: str, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):\n'
          '    book = db.query(Book).filter(Book.isbn == isbn).first()\n'
          '    if not book:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Book not '
          'found")\n'
          '    return book\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.patch(f"{settings.API_PREFIX}/books/{{book_id}}", '
          'response_model=BookOut, tags=["books"])\n'
          'def update_book(\n'
          '    book_id: int,\n'
          '    payload: BookUpdate,\n'
          '    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n'
          '    _: User = Depends(require_staff),\n'
          '):\n'
          '    book = db.query(Book).filter(Book.id == book_id).first()\n'
          '    if not book:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Book not '
          'found")\n'
          '    data = payload.model_dump(exclude_unset=True)\n'
          '    new_total = data.pop("total_copies", None)\n'
          '    author_ids = data.pop("author_ids", None)\n'
          '    category_ids = data.pop("category_ids", None)\n'
          '\n'
          '    for k, v in data.items():\n'
          '        setattr(book, k, v)\n'
          '\n'
          '    if new_total is not None:\n'
          '        active_loans = db.query(Loan).filter(\n'
          '            Loan.book_id == book.id,\n'
          '            Loan.status.in_([LoanStatus.ACTIVE.value, '
          'LoanStatus.OVERDUE.value]),\n'
          '        ).count()\n'
          '        if new_total < active_loans:\n'
          '            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=f"Total '
          'copies cannot be less than active loans ({active_loans})")\n'
          '        diff = new_total - book.total_copies\n'
          '        book.total_copies = new_total\n'
          '        book.available_copies = max(0, book.available_copies + '
          'diff)\n'
          '\n'
          '    if author_ids is not None:\n'
          '        book.authors = '
          'db.query(Author).filter(Author.id.in_(author_ids)).all()\n'
          '    if category_ids is not None:\n'
          '        book.categories = '
          'db.query(Category).filter(Category.id.in_(category_ids)).all()\n'
          '\n'
          '    db.commit()\n'
          '    db.refresh(book)\n'
          '    return book\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.delete(f"{settings.API_PREFIX}/books/{{book_id}}", '
          'status_code=204, tags=["books"])\n'
          'def delete_book(book_id: int, db: Session = Depends(get_db), _: '
          'User = Depends(require_admin)):\n'
          '    book = db.query(Book).filter(Book.id == book_id).first()\n'
          '    if not book:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Book not '
          'found")\n'
          '    if any(loan.status in (LoanStatus.ACTIVE.value, '
          'LoanStatus.OVERDUE.value) for loan in book.loans):\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Cannot delete '
          'a book with active loans")\n'
          '    db.delete(book)\n'
          '    db.commit()\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '# '
          '---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n'
          '# Loans\n'
          '# '
          '---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n'
          '@app.post(f"{settings.API_PREFIX}/loans", response_model=LoanOut, '
          'status_code=201, tags=["loans"])\n'
          'def create_loan(payload: LoanCreate, db: Session = Depends(get_db), '
          '_: User = Depends(require_staff)):\n'
          '    book = db.query(Book).filter(Book.id == '
          'payload.book_id).first()\n'
          '    if not book:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Book not '
          'found")\n'
          '    member = db.query(User).filter(User.id == '
          'payload.member_id).first()\n'
          '    if not member or not member.is_active:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Member not '
          'found or inactive")\n'
          '\n'
          '    active_count = db.query(Loan).filter(\n'
          '        Loan.member_id == member.id,\n'
          '        Loan.status.in_([LoanStatus.ACTIVE.value, '
          'LoanStatus.OVERDUE.value]),\n'
          '    ).count()\n'
          '    if active_count >= settings.MAX_LOANS_PER_MEMBER:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=f"Member has '
          'reached max loans ({settings.MAX_LOANS_PER_MEMBER})")\n'
          '    if book.available_copies < 1:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="No copies '
          'available")\n'
          '\n'
          '    loan = Loan(\n'
          '        book_id=book.id,\n'
          '        member_id=member.id,\n'
          '        loan_date=datetime.utcnow(),\n'
          '        due_date=datetime.utcnow() + '
          'timedelta(days=payload.loan_days),\n'
          '        status=LoanStatus.ACTIVE.value,\n'
          '    )\n'
          '    book.available_copies -= 1\n'
          '    db.add(loan)\n'
          '    db.commit()\n'
          '    db.refresh(loan)\n'
          '    return loan\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get(f"{settings.API_PREFIX}/loans", '
          'response_model=PaginatedResponse, tags=["loans"])\n'
          'def list_loans(\n'
          '    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),\n'
          '    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=100),\n'
          '    member_id: Optional[int] = None,\n'
          '    book_id: Optional[int] = None,\n'
          '    loan_status: Optional[LoanStatus] = None,\n'
          '    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n'
          '    current: User = Depends(get_current_user),\n'
          '):\n'
          '    query = db.query(Loan)\n'
          '    # Members can only see their own loans\n'
          '    if current.role == UserRole.MEMBER.value:\n'
          '        query = query.filter(Loan.member_id == current.id)\n'
          '    elif member_id:\n'
          '        query = query.filter(Loan.member_id == member_id)\n'
          '    if book_id:\n'
          '        query = query.filter(Loan.book_id == book_id)\n'
          '    if loan_status:\n'
          '        query = query.filter(Loan.status == loan_status.value)\n'
          '\n'
          '    total = query.count()\n'
          '    items = query.order_by(Loan.loan_date.desc()).offset((page - 1) '
          '* page_size).limit(page_size).all()\n'
          '    return PaginatedResponse(\n'
          '        total=total, page=page, page_size=page_size,\n'
          '        items=[LoanOut.model_validate(l) for l in items],\n'
          '    )\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get(f"{settings.API_PREFIX}/loans/{{loan_id}}", '
          'response_model=LoanOut, tags=["loans"])\n'
          'def get_loan(loan_id: int, db: Session = Depends(get_db), current: '
          'User = Depends(get_current_user)):\n'
          '    loan = db.query(Loan).filter(Loan.id == loan_id).first()\n'
          '    if not loan:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Loan not '
          'found")\n'
          '    if current.role == UserRole.MEMBER.value and loan.member_id != '
          'current.id:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Not '
          'authorized")\n'
          '    return loan\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.post(f"{settings.API_PREFIX}/loans/{{loan_id}}/return", '
          'response_model=LoanOut, tags=["loans"])\n'
          'def return_loan(loan_id: int, db: Session = Depends(get_db), _: '
          'User = Depends(require_staff)):\n'
          '    loan = db.query(Loan).filter(Loan.id == loan_id).first()\n'
          '    if not loan:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Loan not '
          'found")\n'
          '    if loan.status == LoanStatus.RETURNED.value:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Loan already '
          'returned")\n'
          '\n'
          '    loan.return_date = datetime.utcnow()\n'
          '    loan.fine_amount = calculate_overdue_fine(loan)\n'
          '    loan.status = LoanStatus.RETURNED.value\n'
          '    loan.book.available_copies = min(loan.book.total_copies, '
          'loan.book.available_copies + 1)\n'
          '    db.commit()\n'
          '    db.refresh(loan)\n'
          '    return loan\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.post(f"{settings.API_PREFIX}/loans/{{loan_id}}/renew", '
          'response_model=LoanOut, tags=["loans"])\n'
          'def renew_loan(\n'
          '    loan_id: int,\n'
          '    extra_days: int = Query(settings.DEFAULT_LOAN_DAYS, ge=1, '
          'le=60),\n'
          '    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n'
          '    current: User = Depends(get_current_user),\n'
          '):\n'
          '    loan = db.query(Loan).filter(Loan.id == loan_id).first()\n'
          '    if not loan:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Loan not '
          'found")\n'
          '    if current.role == UserRole.MEMBER.value and loan.member_id != '
          'current.id:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Not '
          'authorized")\n'
          '    if loan.status != LoanStatus.ACTIVE.value:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Only active '
          'loans can be renewed")\n'
          '    if loan.renewal_count >= 2:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Max renewals '
          'reached")\n'
          '    # If others are waiting, deny renewal\n'
          '    waiting = db.query(Reservation).filter(\n'
          '        Reservation.book_id == loan.book_id,\n'
          '        Reservation.status == ReservationStatus.PENDING.value,\n'
          '    ).count()\n'
          '    if waiting > 0:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Book is '
          'reserved by others; renewal not allowed")\n'
          '    loan.due_date += timedelta(days=extra_days)\n'
          '    loan.renewal_count += 1\n'
          '    db.commit()\n'
          '    db.refresh(loan)\n'
          '    return loan\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.post(f"{settings.API_PREFIX}/loans/{{loan_id}}/mark-lost", '
          'response_model=LoanOut, tags=["loans"])\n'
          'def mark_loan_lost(loan_id: int, db: Session = Depends(get_db), _: '
          'User = Depends(require_staff)):\n'
          '    loan = db.query(Loan).filter(Loan.id == loan_id).first()\n'
          '    if not loan:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Loan not '
          'found")\n'
          '    loan.status = LoanStatus.LOST.value\n'
          '    loan.fine_amount = (loan.fine_amount or 0) + 50.0  # '
          'replacement fee placeholder\n'
          '    loan.book.total_copies = max(0, loan.book.total_copies - 1)\n'
          '    db.commit()\n'
          '    db.refresh(loan)\n'
          '    return loan\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.post(f"{settings.API_PREFIX}/loans/refresh-overdue", '
          'tags=["loans"])\n'
          'def refresh_overdue(\n'
          '    background: BackgroundTasks,\n'
          '    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n'
          '    _: User = Depends(require_staff),\n'
          '):\n'
          '    background.add_task(update_overdue_loans, db)\n'
          '    return {"detail": "Overdue loans refresh scheduled"}\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '# '
          '---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n'
          '# Reservations\n'
          '# '
          '---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n'
          '@app.post(f"{settings.API_PREFIX}/reservations", '
          'response_model=ReservationOut, status_code=201, '
          'tags=["reservations"])\n'
          'def create_reservation(\n'
          '    payload: ReservationCreate,\n'
          '    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n'
          '    current: User = Depends(get_current_user),\n'
          '):\n'
          '    book = db.query(Book).filter(Book.id == '
          'payload.book_id).first()\n'
          '    if not book:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Book not '
          'found")\n'
          '    if book.available_copies > 0:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Book is '
          'currently available; borrow instead of reserving")\n'
          '    existing = db.query(Reservation).filter(\n'
          '        Reservation.book_id == book.id,\n'
          '        Reservation.member_id == current.id,\n'
          '        Reservation.status == ReservationStatus.PENDING.value,\n'
          '    ).first()\n'
          '    if existing:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="You already '
          'have a pending reservation for this book")\n'
          '    reservation = Reservation(\n'
          '        book_id=book.id,\n'
          '        member_id=current.id,\n'
          '        expiry_date=datetime.utcnow() + '
          'timedelta(days=payload.days_valid),\n'
          '    )\n'
          '    db.add(reservation)\n'
          '    db.commit()\n'
          '    db.refresh(reservation)\n'
          '    return reservation\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get(f"{settings.API_PREFIX}/reservations", '
          'response_model=List[ReservationOut], tags=["reservations"])\n'
          'def list_reservations(\n'
          '    res_status: Optional[ReservationStatus] = None,\n'
          '    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n'
          '    current: User = Depends(get_current_user),\n'
          '):\n'
          '    query = db.query(Reservation)\n'
          '    if current.role == UserRole.MEMBER.value:\n'
          '        query = query.filter(Reservation.member_id == current.id)\n'
          '    if res_status:\n'
          '        query = query.filter(Reservation.status == '
          'res_status.value)\n'
          '    return '
          'query.order_by(Reservation.reservation_date.desc()).all()\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.delete(f"{settings.API_PREFIX}/reservations/{{reservation_id}}", '
          'status_code=204, tags=["reservations"])\n'
          'def cancel_reservation(\n'
          '    reservation_id: int,\n'
          '    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n'
          '    current: User = Depends(get_current_user),\n'
          '):\n'
          '    res = db.query(Reservation).filter(Reservation.id == '
          'reservation_id).first()\n'
          '    if not res:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Reservation '
          'not found")\n'
          '    if current.role == UserRole.MEMBER.value and res.member_id != '
          'current.id:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Not '
          'authorized")\n'
          '    res.status = ReservationStatus.CANCELLED.value\n'
          '    db.commit()\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '# '
          '---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n'
          '# Reviews\n'
          '# '
          '---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n'
          '@app.post(f"{settings.API_PREFIX}/books/{{book_id}}/reviews", '
          'response_model=ReviewOut, status_code=201, tags=["reviews"])\n'
          'def create_review(\n'
          '    book_id: int,\n'
          '    payload: ReviewCreate,\n'
          '    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n'
          '    current: User = Depends(get_current_user),\n'
          '):\n'
          '    book = db.query(Book).filter(Book.id == book_id).first()\n'
          '    if not book:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Book not '
          'found")\n'
          '    has_borrowed = db.query(Loan).filter(\n'
          '        Loan.book_id == book_id,\n'
          '        Loan.member_id == current.id,\n'
          '    ).first()\n'
          '    if not has_borrowed and current.role == UserRole.MEMBER.value:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="You can only '
          'review books you\'ve borrowed")\n'
          '    review = Review(book_id=book_id, member_id=current.id, '
          '**payload.model_dump())\n'
          '    db.add(review)\n'
          '    db.commit()\n'
          '    db.refresh(review)\n'
          '    return review\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get(f"{settings.API_PREFIX}/books/{{book_id}}/reviews", '
          'response_model=List[ReviewOut], tags=["reviews"])\n'
          'def list_reviews(book_id: int, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):\n'
          '    return db.query(Review).filter(Review.book_id == '
          'book_id).order_by(Review.created_at.desc()).all()\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.delete(f"{settings.API_PREFIX}/reviews/{{review_id}}", '
          'status_code=204, tags=["reviews"])\n'
          'def delete_review(review_id: int, db: Session = Depends(get_db), '
          'current: User = Depends(get_current_user)):\n'
          '    review = db.query(Review).filter(Review.id == '
          'review_id).first()\n'
          '    if not review:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Review not '
          'found")\n'
          '    if review.member_id != current.id and current.role not in '
          '(UserRole.ADMIN.value, UserRole.LIBRARIAN.value):\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Not '
          'authorized")\n'
          '    db.delete(review)\n'
          '    db.commit()\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '# '
          '---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n'
          '# Statistics / Dashboard\n'
          '# '
          '---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n'
          '@app.get(f"{settings.API_PREFIX}/statistics", '
          'response_model=StatisticsOut, tags=["stats"])\n'
          'def statistics(db: Session = Depends(get_db), _: User = '
          'Depends(require_staff)):\n'
          '    total_books = '
          'db.query(func.coalesce(func.sum(Book.total_copies), 0)).scalar() or '
          '0\n'
          '    total_unique = db.query(Book).count()\n'
          '    total_members = db.query(User).filter(User.role == '
          'UserRole.MEMBER.value).count()\n'
          '    active_loans = db.query(Loan).filter(Loan.status == '
          'LoanStatus.ACTIVE.value).count()\n'
          '    overdue_loans = db.query(Loan).filter(Loan.status == '
          'LoanStatus.OVERDUE.value).count()\n'
          '    pending_res = db.query(Reservation).filter(Reservation.status '
          '== ReservationStatus.PENDING.value).count()\n'
          '    fines = db.query(func.coalesce(func.sum(Loan.fine_amount), '
          '0.0)).filter(\n'
          '        Loan.status.in_([LoanStatus.OVERDUE.value, '
          'LoanStatus.LOST.value])\n'
          '    ).scalar() or 0.0\n'
          '\n'
          '    most_borrowed = (\n'
          '        db.query(Book.id, Book.title, '
          'func.count(Loan.id).label("borrow_count"))\n'
          '        .join(Loan, Loan.book_id == Book.id)\n'
          '        .group_by(Book.id, Book.title)\n'
          '        .order_by(func.count(Loan.id).desc())\n'
          '        .limit(5)\n'
          '        .all()\n'
          '    )\n'
          '    most_active = (\n'
          '        db.query(User.id, User.username, '
          'func.count(Loan.id).label("loan_count"))\n'
          '        .join(Loan, Loan.member_id == User.id)\n'
          '        .group_by(User.id, User.username)\n'
          '        .order_by(func.count(Loan.id).desc())\n'
          '        .limit(5)\n'
          '        .all()\n'
          '    )\n'
          '\n'
          '    return StatisticsOut(\n'
          '        total_books=int(total_books),\n'
          '        total_unique_titles=total_unique,\n'
          '        total_members=total_members,\n'
          '        active_loans=active_loans,\n'
          '        overdue_loans=overdue_loans,\n'
          '        pending_reservations=pending_res,\n'
          '        total_fines_outstanding=float(fines),\n'
          '        most_borrowed_books=[{"id": b.id, "title": b.title, '
          '"borrow_count": b.borrow_count} for b in most_borrowed],\n'
          '        most_active_members=[{"id": m.id, "username": m.username, '
          '"loan_count": m.loan_count} for m in most_active],\n'
          '    )\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '# '
          '---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n'
          '# Entry Point\n'
          '# '
          '---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n'
          'if __name__ == "__main__":\n'
          '    import uvicorn\n'
          '    uvicorn.run("main:app", host="0.0.0.0", port=8000, '
          'reload=True)\n'
          '```\n'
          '\n'
          "## What's included\n"
          '\n'
          '| Domain | Endpoints |\n'
          '|---|---|\n'
          '| **Auth** | Register, login (OAuth2 password flow), whoami |\n'
          '| **Users** | List, view, update, delete (role-aware) |\n'
          '| **Authors** | Full CRUD + list books by author |\n'
          '| **Categories** | Create, list, delete |\n'
          '| **Books** | Full CRUD, ISBN lookup, advanced search '
          '(author/category/year/availability/language), sorting & pagination '
          '|\n'
          '| **Loans** | Create, list, view, return, renew (with '
          'reservation-aware logic), mark-lost, background overdue refresh |\n'
          '| **Reservations** | Create (only when unavailable), list, cancel '
          '|\n'
          '| **Reviews** | Create (must have borrowed), list per book, delete '
          '|\n'
          '| **Statistics** | Aggregated dashboard for staff (most borrowed '
          'books, active members, fines, etc.) |\n'
          '\n'
          '## Key engineering details\n'
          '\n'
          '- **JWT auth** with `OAuth2PasswordBearer` + bcrypt hashing\n'
          '- **Role-based access control** via `require_roles()` factory '
          'dependency\n'
          '- **SQLAlchemy** with proper many-to-many for `Book↔Author` and '
          '`Book↔Category`\n'
          '- **Lifespan handler** auto-creates tables and seeds an admin user\n'
          '- **Business rules** enforced: max loans per member, overdue fine '
          "calculation, can't delete books with active loans, "
          'reservation-aware renewals, reduce-total-copies safety check\n'
          '- **Pagination & filtering** baked into list endpoints\n'
          '- **Background tasks** for refreshing overdue loans\n'
          '- **Custom validation exception handler**\n'
          '\n'
          '## Quickstart\n'
          '\n'
          '```bash\n'
          'pip install fastapi[all] sqlalchemy passlib[bcrypt] '
          'python-jose[cryptography]\n'
          'python main.py\n'
          '# Visit http://localhost:8000/docs\n'
          '# Default admin: admin / admin12345\n'
          '```',
