          'class TokenSchema(BaseModel):\n'
          '    access_token: str\n'
          '    token_type: str = "bearer"\n'
          '    expires_in: int\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'class TokenData(BaseModel):\n'
          '    user_id: Optional[int] = None\n'
          '    role: Optional[str] = None\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '# --- User Schemas ---\n'
          'class UserBase(BaseModel):\n'
          '    email: EmailStr\n'
          '    username: str = Field(..., min_length=3, max_length=100)\n'
          '    full_name: str = Field(..., min_length=1, max_length=255)\n'
          '    phone: Optional[str] = None\n'
          '    address: Optional[str] = None\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'class UserCreate(UserBase):\n'
          '    password: str = Field(..., min_length=8, max_length=100)\n'
          '    role: UserRole = UserRole.MEMBER\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'class UserUpdate(BaseModel):\n'
          '    email: Optional[EmailStr] = None\n'
          '    full_name: Optional[str] = None\n'
          '    phone: Optional[str] = None\n'
          '    address: Optional[str] = None\n'
          '    is_active: Optional[bool] = None\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'class UserResponse(UserBase):\n'
          '    model_config = ConfigDict(from_attributes=True)\n'
          '    id: int\n'
          '    role: UserRole\n'
          '    is_active: bool\n'
          '    membership_id: Optional[str]\n'
          '    created_at: datetime\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '# --- Author Schemas ---\n'
          'class AuthorBase(BaseModel):\n'
          '    first_name: str = Field(..., min_length=1, max_length=100)\n'
          '    last_name: str = Field(..., min_length=1, max_length=100)\n'
          '    biography: Optional[str] = None\n'
          '    birth_date: Optional[date] = None\n'
          '    death_date: Optional[date] = None\n'
          '    nationality: Optional[str] = None\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'class AuthorCreate(AuthorBase): pass\n'
          'class AuthorUpdate(BaseModel):\n'
          '    first_name: Optional[str] = None\n'
          '    last_name: Optional[str] = None\n'
          '    biography: Optional[str] = None\n'
          '    birth_date: Optional[date] = None\n'
          '    death_date: Optional[date] = None\n'
          '    nationality: Optional[str] = None\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'class AuthorResponse(AuthorBase):\n'
          '    model_config = ConfigDict(from_attributes=True)\n'
          '    id: int\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '# --- Category Schemas ---\n'
          'class CategoryBase(BaseModel):\n'
          '    name: str = Field(..., min_length=1, max_length=100)\n'
          '    description: Optional[str] = None\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'class CategoryCreate(CategoryBase): pass\n'
          'class CategoryResponse(CategoryBase):\n'
          '    model_config = ConfigDict(from_attributes=True)\n'
          '    id: int\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '# --- Publisher Schemas ---\n'
          'class PublisherBase(BaseModel):\n'
          '    name: str = Field(..., min_length=1, max_length=255)\n'
          '    address: Optional[str] = None\n'
          '    website: Optional[str] = None\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'class PublisherCreate(PublisherBase): pass\n'
          'class PublisherResponse(PublisherBase):\n'
          '    model_config = ConfigDict(from_attributes=True)\n'
          '    id: int\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '# --- Book Schemas ---\n'
          'class BookBase(BaseModel):\n'
          '    isbn: str = Field(..., min_length=10, max_length=20)\n'
          '    title: str = Field(..., min_length=1, max_length=255)\n'
          '    subtitle: Optional[str] = None\n'
          '    description: Optional[str] = None\n'
          '    publication_year: Optional[int] = Field(None, ge=1000, '
          'le=2100)\n'
          '    language: str = "English"\n'
          '    pages: Optional[int] = Field(None, gt=0)\n'
          '    edition: Optional[str] = None\n'
          '    cover_image: Optional[str] = None\n'
          '    location: Optional[str] = None\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'class BookCreate(BookBase):\n'
          '    total_copies: int = Field(1, ge=1)\n'
          '    publisher_id: Optional[int] = None\n'
          '    author_ids: List[int] = []\n'
          '    category_ids: List[int] = []\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'class BookUpdate(BaseModel):\n'
          '    title: Optional[str] = None\n'
          '    subtitle: Optional[str] = None\n'
          '    description: Optional[str] = None\n'
          '    publication_year: Optional[int] = None\n'
          '    language: Optional[str] = None\n'
          '    pages: Optional[int] = None\n'
          '    edition: Optional[str] = None\n'
          '    cover_image: Optional[str] = None\n'
          '    location: Optional[str] = None\n'
          '    total_copies: Optional[int] = None\n'
          '    publisher_id: Optional[int] = None\n'
          '    author_ids: Optional[List[int]] = None\n'
          '    category_ids: Optional[List[int]] = None\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'class BookResponse(BookBase):\n'
          '    model_config = ConfigDict(from_attributes=True)\n'
          '    id: int\n'
          '    total_copies: int\n'
          '    available_copies: int\n'
          '    status: BookStatus\n'
          '    publisher: Optional[PublisherResponse]\n'
          '    authors: List[AuthorResponse]\n'
          '    categories: List[CategoryResponse]\n'
          '    created_at: datetime\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'class BookStats(BaseModel):\n'
          '    book_id: int\n'
          '    title: str\n'
          '    total_loans: int\n'
          '    active_loans: int\n'
          '    average_rating: Optional[float]\n'
          '    total_reviews: int\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '# --- Loan Schemas ---\n'
          'class LoanCreate(BaseModel):\n'
          '    book_id: int\n'
          '    member_id: int\n'
          '    loan_period_days: Optional[int] = None\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'class LoanResponse(BaseModel):\n'
          '    model_config = ConfigDict(from_attributes=True)\n'
          '    id: int\n'
          '    book_id: int\n'
          '    member_id: int\n'
          '    loan_date: datetime\n'
          '    due_date: datetime\n'
          '    return_date: Optional[datetime]\n'
          '    status: LoanStatus\n'
          '    renewal_count: int\n'
          '    notes: Optional[str]\n'
          '    book: Optional[BookResponse] = None\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'class LoanRenewal(BaseModel):\n'
          '    additional_days: int = Field(7, ge=1, le=30)\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '# --- Reservation Schemas ---\n'
          'class ReservationCreate(BaseModel):\n'
          '    book_id: int\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'class ReservationResponse(BaseModel):\n'
          '    model_config = ConfigDict(from_attributes=True)\n'
          '    id: int\n'
          '    book_id: int\n'
          '    member_id: int\n'
          '    reserved_at: datetime\n'
          '    expires_at: Optional[datetime]\n'
          '    status: ReservationStatus\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '# --- Review Schemas ---\n'
          'class ReviewCreate(BaseModel):\n'
          '    book_id: int\n'
          '    rating: int = Field(..., ge=1, le=5)\n'
          '    title: Optional[str] = None\n'
          '    comment: Optional[str] = None\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'class ReviewResponse(BaseModel):\n'
          '    model_config = ConfigDict(from_attributes=True)\n'
          '    id: int\n'
          '    book_id: int\n'
          '    member_id: int\n'
          '    rating: int\n'
          '    title: Optional[str]\n'
          '    comment: Optional[str]\n'
          '    created_at: datetime\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '# --- Fine Schemas ---\n'
          'class FineResponse(BaseModel):\n'
          '    model_config = ConfigDict(from_attributes=True)\n'
          '    id: int\n'
          '    loan_id: int\n'
          '    member_id: int\n'
          '    amount: float\n'
          '    reason: str\n'
          '    is_paid: bool\n'
          '    issued_at: datetime\n'
          '    paid_at: Optional[datetime]\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '# --- Pagination ---\n'
          'class PaginatedResponse(BaseModel):\n'
          '    total: int\n'
          '    page: int\n'
          '    page_size: int\n'
          '    pages: int\n'
          '    items: List\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '# --- Dashboard ---\n'
          'class DashboardStats(BaseModel):\n'
          '    total_books: int\n'
          '    total_available_books: int\n'
          '    total_members: int\n'
          '    total_active_loans: int\n'
          '    total_overdue_loans: int\n'
          '    total_reservations: int\n'
          '    total_unpaid_fines: float\n'
          '    most_borrowed_books: List[BookStats]\n'
          '\n'
          '# '
          '==============================================================================\n'
          '# AUTH & SECURITY\n'
          '# '
          '==============================================================================\n'
          '\n'
          'pwd_context = CryptContext(schemes=["bcrypt"], deprecated="auto")\n'
          'oauth2_scheme = OAuth2PasswordBearer(tokenUrl="/auth/login")\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'def hash_password(password: str) -> str:\n'
          '    return pwd_context.hash(password)\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'def verify_password(plain: str, hashed: str) -> bool:\n'
          '    return pwd_context.verify(plain, hashed)\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'def create_access_token(data: dict, expires_delta: '
          'Optional[timedelta] = None) -> str:\n'
          '    to_encode = data.copy()\n'
          '    expire = datetime.utcnow() + (expires_delta or '
          'timedelta(minutes=settings.ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES))\n'
          '    to_encode.update({"exp": expire})\n'
          '    return jwt.encode(to_encode, settings.SECRET_KEY, '
          'algorithm=settings.ALGORITHM)\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'def get_current_user(token: Annotated[str, Depends(oauth2_scheme)], '
          'db: DBSession) -> User:\n'
          '    credentials_exception = HTTPException(\n'
          '        status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,\n'
          '        detail="Could not validate credentials",\n'
          '        headers={"WWW-Authenticate": "Bearer"},\n'
          '    )\n'
          '    try:\n'
          '        payload = jwt.decode(token, settings.SECRET_KEY, '
          'algorithms=[settings.ALGORITHM])\n'
          '        user_id: int = payload.get("user_id")\n'
          '        if user_id is None:\n'
          '            raise credentials_exception\n'
          '    except JWTError:\n'
          '        raise credentials_exception\n'
          '    user = db.query(User).filter(User.id == user_id).first()\n'
          '    if user is None or not user.is_active:\n'
          '        raise credentials_exception\n'
          '    return user\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'def require_role(*allowed_roles: UserRole):\n'
          '    def role_checker(current_user: Annotated[User, '
          'Depends(get_current_user)]) -> User:\n'
          '        if current_user.role not in allowed_roles:\n'
          '            raise HTTPException(\n'
          '                status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,\n'
          '                detail=f"Operation requires role: {[r.value for r '
          'in allowed_roles]}",\n'
          '            )\n'
          '        return current_user\n'
          '    return role_checker\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'CurrentUser = Annotated[User, Depends(get_current_user)]\n'
          'AdminOnly = Annotated[User, Depends(require_role(UserRole.ADMIN))]\n'
          'StaffOnly = Annotated[User, Depends(require_role(UserRole.ADMIN, '
          'UserRole.LIBRARIAN))]\n'
          '\n'
          '# '
          '==============================================================================\n'
          '# UTILITY FUNCTIONS\n'
          '# '
          '==============================================================================\n'
          '\n'
          'def paginate(query, page: int, page_size: int) -> dict:\n'
          '    total = query.count()\n'
          '    items = query.offset((page - 1) * '
          'page_size).limit(page_size).all()\n'
          '    return {\n'
          '        "total": total,\n'
          '        "page": page,\n'
          '        "page_size": page_size,\n'
          '        "pages": (total + page_size - 1) // page_size,\n'
          '        "items": items,\n'
          '    }\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'def calculate_fine(loan: Loan) -> float:\n'
          '    if not loan.return_date:\n'
          '        end_date = datetime.utcnow()\n'
          '    else:\n'
          '        end_date = loan.return_date\n'
          '    days_late = (end_date - loan.due_date).days\n'
          '    return max(0, days_late) * settings.FINE_PER_DAY\n'
          '\n'
          '# '
          '==============================================================================\n'
          '# APPLICATION LIFECYCLE\n'
          '# '
          '==============================================================================\n'
          '\n'
          '@asynccontextmanager\n'
          'async def lifespan(app: FastAPI):\n'
          '    # Startup: create tables and seed admin\n'
          '    Base.metadata.create_all(bind=engine)\n'
          '    db = SessionLocal()\n'
          '    try:\n'
          '        if not db.query(User).filter(User.role == '
          'UserRole.ADMIN).first():\n'
          '            admin = User(\n'
          '                email="admin@library.com",\n'
          '                username="admin",\n'
          '                full_name="System Administrator",\n'
          '                hashed_password=hash_password("admin123"),\n'
          '                role=UserRole.ADMIN,\n'
          '                membership_id="ADMIN-001",\n'
          '            )\n'
          '            db.add(admin)\n'
          '            db.commit()\n'
          '            print("✅ Default admin created: admin@library.com / '
          'admin123")\n'
          '    finally:\n'
          '        db.close()\n'
          '    yield\n'
          '    # Shutdown\n'
          '    print("👋 Shutting down library API")\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '# '
          '==============================================================================\n'
          '# FASTAPI APPLICATION\n'
          '# '
          '==============================================================================\n'
          '\n'
          'app = FastAPI(\n'
          '    title=settings.APP_NAME,\n'
          '    version=settings.APP_VERSION,\n'
          '    description="A comprehensive library management system",\n'
          '    lifespan=lifespan,\n'
          ')\n'
          '\n'
          'app.add_middleware(\n'
          '    CORSMiddleware,\n'
          '    allow_origins=["*"],\n'
          '    allow_credentials=True,\n'
          '    allow_methods=["*"],\n'
          '    allow_headers=["*"],\n'
          ')\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.exception_handler(Exception)\n'
          'async def global_exception_handler(request: Request, exc: '
          'Exception):\n'
          '    if isinstance(exc, HTTPException):\n'
          '        raise exc\n'
          '    return JSONResponse(\n'
          '        status_code=500,\n'
          '        content={"detail": "Internal server error", "error": '
          'str(exc)},\n'
          '    )\n'
          '\n'
          '# '
          '==============================================================================\n'
          '# ROOT & HEALTH\n'
          '# '
          '==============================================================================\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get("/", tags=["Root"])\n'
          'def read_root():\n'
          '    return {\n'
          '        "app": settings.APP_NAME,\n'
          '        "version": settings.APP_VERSION,\n'
          '        "docs": "/docs",\n'
          '        "redoc": "/redoc",\n'
          '    }\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get("/health", tags=["Root"])\n'
          'def health_check(db: DBSession):\n'
          '    try:\n'
          '        db.execute("SELECT 1")\n'
          '        return {"status": "healthy", "database": "connected"}\n'
          '    except Exception:\n'
          '        return {"status": "unhealthy", "database": "disconnected"}\n'
          '\n'
          '# '
          '==============================================================================\n'
          '# AUTHENTICATION ENDPOINTS\n'
          '# '
          '==============================================================================\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.post("/auth/register", response_model=UserResponse, '
          'status_code=201, tags=["Auth"])\n'
          'def register_user(user_data: UserCreate, db: DBSession):\n'
          '    """Register a new library member."""\n'
          '    if db.query(User).filter(or_(User.email == user_data.email, '
          'User.username == user_data.username)).first():\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(400, "Email or username already '
          'registered")\n'
          '\n'
          '    new_user = User(\n'
          '        email=user_data.email,\n'
          '        username=user_data.username,\n'
          '        full_name=user_data.full_name,\n'
          '        hashed_password=hash_password(user_data.password),\n'
          '        role=UserRole.MEMBER,  # Self-registration always creates '
          'members\n'
          '        phone=user_data.phone,\n'
          '        address=user_data.address,\n'
          '        membership_id=f"LIB-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8].upper()}",\n'
          '    )\n'
          '    db.add(new_user)\n'
          '    db.commit()\n'
          '    db.refresh(new_user)\n'
          '    return new_user\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.post("/auth/login", response_model=TokenSchema, '
          'tags=["Auth"])\n'
          'def login(form_data: Annotated[OAuth2PasswordRequestForm, '
          'Depends()], db: DBSession):\n'
          '    """Authenticate and receive an access token."""\n'
          '    user = db.query(User).filter(\n'
          '        or_(User.username == form_data.username, User.email == '
          'form_data.username)\n'
          '    ).first()\n'
          '    if not user or not verify_password(form_data.password, '
          'user.hashed_password):\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(401, "Invalid credentials")\n'
          '    if not user.is_active:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(403, "Account is disabled")\n'
          '\n'
          '    access_token = create_access_token({"user_id": user.id, "role": '
          'user.role.value})\n'
          '    return TokenSchema(\n'
          '        access_token=access_token,\n'
          '        token_type="bearer",\n'
          '        expires_in=settings.ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES * 60,\n'
          '    )\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get("/auth/me", response_model=UserResponse, tags=["Auth"])\n'
          'def get_my_profile(current_user: CurrentUser):\n'
          '    """Get the current authenticated user\'s profile."""\n'
          '    return current_user\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.put("/auth/me", response_model=UserResponse, tags=["Auth"])\n'
          'def update_my_profile(update: UserUpdate, current_user: '
          'CurrentUser, db: DBSession):\n'
          '    """Update current user\'s profile."""\n'
          '    for field, value in update.model_dump(exclude_unset=True, '
          'exclude={"is_active"}).items():\n'
          '        setattr(current_user, field, value)\n'
          '    db.commit()\n'
          '    db.refresh(current_user)\n'
          '    return current_user\n'
          '\n'
          '# '
          '==============================================================================\n'
          '# USER MANAGEMENT ENDPOINTS (ADMIN/LIBRARIAN)\n'
          '# '
          '==============================================================================\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get("/users", response_model=PaginatedResponse, '
          'tags=["Users"])\n'
          'def list_users(\n'
          '    db: DBSession,\n'
          '    _: StaffOnly,\n'
          '    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),\n'
          '    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=100),\n'
          '    role: Optional[UserRole] = None,\n'
          '    search: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="Search by '
          'name, email, or username"),\n'
          '    is_active: Optional[bool] = None,\n'
          '):\n'
          '    """List all users with filtering and pagination (staff '
          'only)."""\n'
          '    query = db.query(User)\n'
          '    if role:\n'
          '        query = query.filter(User.role == role)\n'
          '    if is_active is not None:\n'
          '        query = query.filter(User.is_active == is_active)\n'
          '    if search:\n'
          '        like = f"%{search}%"\n'
          '        query = query.filter(or_(\n'
          '            User.full_name.ilike(like),\n'
          '            User.email.ilike(like),\n'
          '            User.username.ilike(like),\n'
          '        ))\n'
          '    result = paginate(query.order_by(User.created_at.desc()), page, '
          'page_size)\n'
          '    result["items"] = [UserResponse.model_validate(u) for u in '
          'result["items"]]\n'
          '    return result\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.post("/users", response_model=UserResponse, status_code=201, '
          'tags=["Users"])\n'
          'def create_user(user_data: UserCreate, db: DBSession, _: '
          'AdminOnly):\n'
          '    """Create a user with any role (admin only)."""\n'
          '    if db.query(User).filter(or_(User.email == user_data.email, '
          'User.username == user_data.username)).first():\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(400, "Email or username already '
          'registered")\n'
          '    new_user = User(\n'
          '        **user_data.model_dump(exclude={"password"}),\n'
          '        hashed_password=hash_password(user_data.password),\n'
          '        membership_id=f"LIB-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8].upper()}",\n'
          '    )\n'
          '    db.add(new_user)\n'
          '    db.commit()\n'
          '    db.refresh(new_user)\n'
          '    return new_user\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get("/users/{user_id}", response_model=UserResponse, '
          'tags=["Users"])\n'
          'def get_user(user_id: int, db: DBSession, current_user: '
          'CurrentUser):\n'
          '    """Get user by ID. Members can only view their own profile."""\n'
          '    if current_user.role == UserRole.MEMBER and current_user.id != '
          'user_id:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(403, "You can only view your own '
          'profile")\n'
          '    user = db.query(User).filter(User.id == user_id).first()\n'
          '    if not user:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(404, "User not found")\n'
          '    return user\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.put("/users/{user_id}", response_model=UserResponse, '
          'tags=["Users"])\n'
          'def update_user(user_id: int, update: UserUpdate, db: DBSession, _: '
          'AdminOnly):\n'
          '    """Update any user (admin only)."""\n'
          '    user = db.query(User).filter(User.id == user_id).first()\n'
          '    if not user:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(404, "User not found")\n'
          '    for field, value in '
          'update.model_dump(exclude_unset=True).items():\n'
          '        setattr(user, field, value)\n'
          '    db.commit()\n'
          '    db.refresh(user)\n'
          '    return user\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.delete("/users/{user_id}", status_code=204, tags=["Users"])\n'
          'def delete_user(user_id: int, db: DBSession, admin: AdminOnly):\n'
          '    """Delete a user (admin only). Cannot delete users with active '
          'loans."""\n'
          '    if admin.id == user_id:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(400, "Cannot delete yourself")\n'
          '    user = db.query(User).filter(User.id == user_id).first()\n'
          '    if not user:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(404, "User not found")\n'
          '    active_loans = db.query(Loan).filter(\n'
          '        Loan.member_id == user_id, Loan.status == '
          'LoanStatus.ACTIVE\n'
          '    ).count()\n'
          '    if active_loans:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(400, f"User has {active_loans} active '
          'loan(s)")\n'
          '    db.delete(user)\n'
          '    db.commit()\n'
          '\n'
          '# '
          '==============================================================================\n'
          '# AUTHOR ENDPOINTS\n'
          '# '
          '==============================================================================\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get("/authors", response_model=PaginatedResponse, '
          'tags=["Authors"])\n'
          'def list_authors(\n'
          '    db: DBSession,\n'
          '    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),\n'
          '    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=100),\n'
          '    search: Optional[str] = None,\n'
          '    nationality: Optional[str] = None,\n'
          '):\n'
          '    query = db.query(Author)\n'
          '    if search:\n'
          '        like = f"%{search}%"\n'
          '        query = query.filter(or_(Author.first_name.ilike(like), '
          'Author.last_name.ilike(like)))\n'
          '    if nationality:\n'
          '        query = '
          'query.filter(Author.nationality.ilike(f"%{nationality}%"))\n'
          '    result = paginate(query.order_by(Author.last_name), page, '
          'page_size)\n'
          '    result["items"] = [AuthorResponse.model_validate(a) for a in '
          'result["items"]]\n'
          '    return result\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'

          '# '
          '==============================================================================\n'
          '# BOOK ENDPOINTS\n'
          '# '
          '==============================================================================\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get("/books", response_model=PaginatedResponse, '
          'tags=["Books"])\n'
          'def list_books(\n'
          '    db: DBSession,\n'
          '    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),\n'
          '    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=100),\n'
          '    search: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="Search '
          'title/ISBN/description"),\n'
          '    author_id: Optional[int] = None,\n'
          '    category_id: Optional[int] = None,\n'
          '    publisher_id: Optional[int] = None,\n'
          '    language: Optional[str] = None,\n'
          '    status_filter: Optional[BookStatus] = Query(None, '
          'alias="status"),\n'
          '    available_only: bool = False,\n'
          '    year_from: Optional[int] = None,\n'
          '    year_to: Optional[int] = None,\n'
          '    sort_by: str = Query("title", '
          'regex="^(title|publication_year|created_at)$"),\n'
          '    order: str = Query("asc", regex="^(asc|desc)$"),\n'
          '):\n'
          '    """Powerful book search with multiple filters."""\n'
          '    query = db.query(Book).options(\n'
          '        joinedload(Book.authors),\n'
          '        joinedload(Book.categories),\n'
          '        joinedload(Book.publisher),\n'
          '    )\n'
          '    if search:\n'
          '        like = f"%{search}%"\n'
          '        query = query.filter(or_(\n'
          '            Book.title.ilike(like), Book.isbn.ilike(like),\n'
          '            Book.description.ilike(like), '
          'Book.subtitle.ilike(like),\n'
          '        ))\n'
          '    if author_id:\n'
          '        query = query.filter(Book.authors.any(Author.id == '
          'author_id))\n'
          '    if category_id:\n'
          '        query = query.filter(Book.categories.any(Category.id == '
          'category_id))\n'
          '    if publisher_id:\n'
          '        query = query.filter(Book.publisher_id == publisher_id)\n'
          '    if language:\n'
          '        query = query.filter(Book.language.ilike(f"%{language}%"))\n'
          '    if status_filter:\n'
          '        query = query.filter(Book.status == status_filter)\n'
          '    if available_only:\n'
          '        query = query.filter(Book.available_copies > 0)\n'
          '    if year_from:\n'
          '        query = query.filter(Book.publication_year >= year_from)\n'
          '    if year_to:\n'
          '        query = query.filter(Book.publication_year <= year_to)\n'
          '\n'
          '    sort_col = getattr(Book, sort_by)\n'
          '    query = query.order_by(sort_col.desc() if order == "desc" else '
          'sort_col.asc())\n'
          '\n'
          '    result = paginate(query, page, page_size)\n'
          '    result["items"] = [BookResponse.model_validate(b) for b in '
          'result["items"]]\n'
          '    return result\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.post("/books", response_model=BookResponse, status_code=201, '
          'tags=["Books"])\n'
          'def create_book(book_data: BookCreate, db: DBSession, _: '
          'StaffOnly):\n'
          '    if db.query(Book).filter(Book.isbn == book_data.isbn).first():\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(400, "Book with this ISBN already '
          'exists")\n'
          '\n'
          '    authors = '
          'db.query(Author).filter(Author.id.in_(book_data.author_ids)).all()\n'
          '    if len(authors) != len(book_data.author_ids):\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(400, "One or more author IDs are '
          'invalid")\n'
          '\n'
          '    categories = '
          'db.query(Category).filter(Category.id.in_(book_data.category_ids)).all()\n'
          '    if len(categories) != len(book_data.category_ids):\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(400, "One or more category IDs are '
          'invalid")\n'
          '\n'
          '    if book_data.publisher_id and not '
          'db.query(Publisher).filter(Publisher.id == '
          'book_data.publisher_id).first():\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(400, "Invalid publisher ID")\n'
          '\n'
          '    new_book = Book(\n'
          '        **book_data.model_dump(exclude={"author_ids", '
          '"category_ids"}),\n'
          '        available_copies=book_data.total_copies,\n'
          '    )\n'
          '    new_book.authors = authors\n'
          '    new_book.categories = categories\n'
          '    db.add(new_book)\n'
          '    db.commit()\n'
          '    db.refresh(new_book)\n'
          '    return new_book\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get("/books/{book_id}", response_model=BookResponse, '
          'tags=["Books"])\n'
          'def get_book(book_id: int, db: DBSession):\n'
          '    book = db.query(Book).options(\n'
          '        joinedload(Book.authors),\n'
          '        joinedload(Book.categories),\n'
          '        joinedload(Book.publisher),\n'
          '    ).filter(Book.id == book_id).first()\n'
          '    if not book:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(404, "Book not found")\n'
          '    return book\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.put("/books/{book_id}", response_model=BookResponse, '
          'tags=["Books"])\n'
          'def update_book(book_id: int, update: BookUpdate, db: DBSession, _: '
          'StaffOnly):\n'
          '    book = db.query(Book).filter(Book.id == book_id).first()\n'
          '    if not book:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(404, "Book not found")\n'
          '\n'
          '    data = update.model_dump(exclude_unset=True)\n'
          '    if "author_ids" in data:\n'
          '        ids = data.pop("author_ids")\n'
          '        book.authors = '
          'db.query(Author).filter(Author.id.in_(ids)).all()\n'
          '    if "category_ids" in data:\n'
          '        ids = data.pop("category_ids")\n'
          '        book.categories = '
          'db.query(Category).filter(Category.id.in_(ids)).all()\n'
          '    if "total_copies" in data:\n'
          '        diff = data["total_copies"] - book.total_copies\n'
          '        book.available_copies = max(0, book.available_copies + '
          'diff)\n'
          '    for field, value in data.items():\n'
          '        setattr(book, field, value)\n'
          '    db.commit()\n'
          '    db.refresh(book)\n'
          '    return book\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.delete("/books/{book_id}", status_code=204, tags=["Books"])\n'
          'def delete_book(book_id: int, db: DBSession, _: AdminOnly):\n'
          '    book = db.query(Book).filter(Book.id == book_id).first()\n'
          '    if not book:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(404, "Book not found")\n'
          '    active = db.query(Loan).filter(Loan.book_id == book_id, '
          'Loan.status == LoanStatus.ACTIVE).count()\n'
          '    if active:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(400, f"Cannot delete: {active} active '
          'loan(s)")\n'
          '    db.delete(book)\n'
          '    db.commit()\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get("/books/{book_id}/stats", response_model=BookStats, '
          'tags=["Books"])\n'
          'def get_book_stats(book_id: int, db: DBSession):\n'
          '    book = db.query(Book).filter(Book.id == book_id).first()\n'
          '    if not book:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(404, "Book not found")\n'
          '    total_loans = db.query(Loan).filter(Loan.book_id == '
          'book_id).count()\n'
          '    active_loans = db.query(Loan).filter(Loan.book_id == book_id, '
          'Loan.status == LoanStatus.ACTIVE).count()\n'
          '    avg_rating = '
          'db.query(func.avg(Review.rating)).filter(Review.book_id == '
          'book_id).scalar()\n'
          '    total_reviews = db.query(Review).filter(Review.book_id == '
          'book_id).count()\n'
          '    return BookStats(\n'
          '        book_id=book.id, title=book.title,\n'
          '        total_loans=total_loans, active_loans=active_loans,\n'
          '        average_rating=round(avg_rating, 2) if avg_rating else '
          'None,\n'
          '        total_reviews=total_reviews,\n'
          '    )\n'
          '\n'
          '# '
          '==============================================================================\n'
          '# LOAN ENDPOINTS\n'
          '# '
          '==============================================================================\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.post("/loans", response_model=LoanResponse, status_code=201, '
          'tags=["Loans"])\n'
          'def create_loan(loan_data: LoanCreate, db: DBSession, staff: '
          'StaffOnly):\n'
          '    """Issue a book loan to a member (staff only)."""\n'
          '    book = db.query(Book).filter(Book.id == '
          'loan_data.book_id).first()\n'
          '    if not book:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(404, "Book not found")\n'
          '    if book.available_copies < 1:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(400, "No available copies")\n'
          '\n'
          '    member = db.query(User).filter(User.id == '
          'loan_data.member_id).first()\n'
          '    if not member or not member.is_active:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(404, "Member not found or inactive")\n'
          '\n'
          '    active_loans = db.query(Loan).filter(\n'
          '        Loan.member_id == member.id, Loan.status == '
          'LoanStatus.ACTIVE\n'
          '    ).count()\n'
          '    if active_loans >= settings.MAX_LOANS_PER_MEMBER:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(400, f"Member has reached max loans '
          '({settings.MAX_LOANS_PER_MEMBER})")\n'
          '\n'
          '    unpaid = db.query(func.sum(Fine.amount)).filter(\n'
          '        Fine.member_id == member.id, Fine.is_paid == False\n'
          '    ).scalar() or 0\n'
          '    if unpaid > 0:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(400, f"Member has unpaid fines of '
          '${unpaid:.2f}")\n'
          '\n'
          '    days = loan_data.loan_period_days or settings.LOAN_PERIOD_DAYS\n'
          '    new_loan = Loan(\n'
          '        book_id=book.id, member_id=member.id,\n'
          '        due_date=datetime.utcnow() + timedelta(days=days),\n'
          '    )\n'
          '    book.available_copies -= 1\n'
          '    if book.available_copies == 0:\n'
          '        book.status = BookStatus.BORROWED\n'
          '    db.add(new_loan)\n'
          '    db.commit()\n'
          '    db.refresh(new_loan)\n'
          '    return new_loan\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get("/loans", response_model=PaginatedResponse, '
          'tags=["Loans"])\n'
          'def list_loans(\n'
          '    db: DBSession,\n'
          '    current_user: CurrentUser,\n'
          '    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),\n'
          '    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=100),\n'
          '    status_filter: Optional[LoanStatus] = Query(None, '
          'alias="status"),\n'
          '    member_id: Optional[int] = None,\n'
          '    book_id: Optional[int] = None,\n'
          '    overdue_only: bool = False,\n'
          '):\n'
          '    query = db.query(Loan).options(joinedload(Loan.book))\n'
          '\n'
          '    # Members only see their own loans\n'
          '    if current_user.role == UserRole.MEMBER:\n'
          '        query = query.filter(Loan.member_id == current_user.id)\n'
          '    elif member_id:\n'
          '        query = query.filter(Loan.member_id == member_id)\n'
          '\n'
          '    if book_id:\n'
          '        query = query.filter(Loan.book_id == book_id)\n'
          '    if status_filter:\n'
          '        query = query.filter(Loan.status == status_filter)\n'
          '    if overdue_only:\n'
          '        query = query.filter(\n'
          '            Loan.due_date < datetime.utcnow(),\n'
          '            Loan.status == LoanStatus.ACTIVE,\n'
          '        )\n'
          '\n'
          '    result = paginate(query.order_by(Loan.loan_date.desc()), page, '
          'page_size)\n'
          '    result["items"] = [LoanResponse.model_validate(l) for l in '
          'result["items"]]\n'
          '    return result\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get("/loans/{loan_id}", response_model=LoanResponse, '
          'tags=["Loans"])\n'
          'def get_loan(loan_id: int, db: DBSession, current_user: '
          'CurrentUser):\n'
          '    loan = '
          'db.query(Loan).options(joinedload(Loan.book)).filter(Loan.id == '
          'loan_id).first()\n'
          '    if not loan:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(404, "Loan not found")\n'
          '    if current_user.role == UserRole.MEMBER and loan.member_id != '
          'current_user.id:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(403, "Access denied")\n'
          '    return loan\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.post("/loans/{loan_id}/return", response_model=LoanResponse, '
          'tags=["Loans"])\n'
          'def return_book(loan_id: int, db: DBSession, _: StaffOnly):\n'
          '    """Mark a loan as returned and calculate any fines."""\n'
          '    loan = db.query(Loan).filter(Loan.id == loan_id).first()\n'
          '    if not loan:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(404, "Loan not found")\n'
          '    if loan.status != LoanStatus.ACTIVE:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(400, f"Loan is not active (status: '
          '{loan.status})")\n'
          '\n'
          '    loan.return_date = datetime.utcnow()\n'
          '    loan.status = LoanStatus.RETURNED\n'
          '\n'
          '    # Restore book copy\n'
          '    book = loan.book\n'
          '    book.available_copies += 1\n'
          '    if book.status == BookStatus.BORROWED and book.available_copies '
          '> 0:\n'
          '        book.status = BookStatus.AVAILABLE\n'
          '\n'
          '    # Generate fine if overdue\n'
          '    fine_amount = calculate_fine(loan)\n'
          '    if fine_amount > 0:\n'
          '        fine = Fine(\n'
          '            loan_id=loan.id, member_id=loan.member_id,\n'
          '            amount=fine_amount,\n'
          '            reason=f"Late return: {(loan.return_date - '
          'loan.due_date).days} days overdue",\n'
          '        )\n'
          '        db.add(fine)\n'
          '\n'
          '    db.commit()\n'
          '    db.refresh(loan)\n'
          '    return loan\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.post("/loans/{loan_id}/renew", response_model=LoanResponse, '
          'tags=["Loans"])\n'
          'def renew_loan(loan_id: int, renewal: LoanRenewal, db: DBSession, '
          'current_user: CurrentUser):\n'
          '    loan = db.query(Loan).filter(Loan.id == loan_id).first()\n'
          '    if not loan:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(404, "Loan not found")\n'
          '    if current_user.role == UserRole.MEMBER and loan.member_id != '
          'current_user.id:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(403, "Access denied")\n'
          '    if loan.status != LoanStatus.ACTIVE:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(400, "Only active loans can be '
          'renewed")\n'
          '    if loan.renewal_count >= 2:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(400, "Maximum renewals reached")\n'
          '\n'
          '    # Cannot renew if reserved by others\n'
          '    pending = db.query(Reservation).filter(\n'
          '        Reservation.book_id == loan.book_id,\n'
          '        Reservation.status == ReservationStatus.PENDING,\n'
          '    ).count()\n'
          '    if pending:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(400, "Cannot renew: book has pending '
          'reservations")\n'
          '\n'
          '    loan.due_date += timedelta(days=renewal.additional_days)\n'
          '    loan.renewal_count += 1\n'
          '    db.commit()\n'
          '    db.refresh(loan)\n'
          '    return loan\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.post("/loans/{loan_id}/mark-lost", '
          'response_model=LoanResponse, tags=["Loans"])\n'
          'def mark_loan_lost(loan_id: int, db: DBSession, _: StaffOnly):\n'
          '    loan = db.query(Loan).filter(Loan.id == loan_id).first()\n'
          '    if not loan:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(404, "Loan not found")\n'
          '    loan.status = LoanStatus.LOST\n'
          '    book = loan.book\n'
          '    book.total_copies = max(0, book.total_copies - 1)\n'
          '    fine = Fine(\n'
          '        loan_id=loan.id, member_id=loan.member_id,\n'
          '        amount=50.0, reason="Lost book replacement fee",\n'
          '    )\n'
          '    db.add(fine)\n'
          '    db.commit()\n'
          '    db.refresh(loan)\n'
          '    return loan\n'
          '\n'
          '# '
          '==============================================================================\n'
          '# RESERVATION ENDPOINTS\n'
          '# '
          '==============================================================================\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.post("/reservations", response_model=ReservationResponse, '
          'status_code=201, tags=["Reservations"])\n'
          'def create_reservation(data: ReservationCreate, db: DBSession, '
          'current_user: CurrentUser):\n'
          '    """Reserve a book that is currently unavailable."""\n'
          '    book = db.query(Book).filter(Book.id == data.book_id).first()\n'
          '    if not book:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(404, "Book not found")\n'
          '    if book.available_copies > 0:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(400, "Book is available, no need to '
          'reserve")\n'
          '\n'
          '    existing = db.query(Reservation).filter(\n'
          '        Reservation.book_id == book.id,\n'
          '        Reservation.member_id == current_user.id,\n'
          '        Reservation.status.in_([ReservationStatus.PENDING, '
          'ReservationStatus.READY]),\n'
          '    ).first()\n'
          '    if existing:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(400, "You already have an active '
          'reservation for this book")\n'
          '\n'
          '    new_res = Reservation(\n'
          '        book_id=book.id, member_id=current_user.id,\n'
          '        expires_at=datetime.utcnow() + '
          'timedelta(days=settings.RESERVATION_EXPIRY_DAYS),\n'
          '    )\n'
          '    db.add(new_res)\n'
          '    db.commit()\n'
          '    db.refresh(new_res)\n'
          '    return new_res\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get("/reservations", response_model=List[ReservationResponse], '
          'tags=["Reservations"])\n'
          'def list_reservations(\n'

          '    if status_filter:\n'
          '        query = query.filter(Reservation.status == status_filter)\n'
          '    return query.order_by(Reservation.reserved_at.desc()).all()\n'
          '\n'

          'tags=["Reservations"])\n'
          'def cancel_reservation(reservation_id: int, db: DBSession, '

          '    res = db.query(Reservation).filter(Reservation.id == '
          'reservation_id).first()\n'
          '    if not res:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(404, "Reservation not found")\n'
          '    if current_user.role == UserRole.MEMBER and res.member_id != '
          'current_user.id:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(403, "Access denied")\n'
          '    res.status = ReservationStatus.CANCELLED\n'
          '    db.commit()\n'

          'status_code=201, tags=["Reviews"])\n'

          'current_user: CurrentUser):\n'
          '    """Submit a review for a book (member must have borrowed it '

          '    book = db.query(Book).filter(Book.id == '

          '    if not book:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(404, "Book not found")\n'
          '\n'
          '    has_borrowed = db.query(Loan).filter(\n'
          '        Loan.book_id == review.book_id,\n'
          '        Loan.member_id == current_user.id,\n'

          '    if not has_borrowed and current_user.role == UserRole.MEMBER:\n'

          '        Review.book_id == review.book_id,\n'
          '        Review.member_id == current_user.id,\n'
          '    ).first()\n'
          '    if existing:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(400, "You have already reviewed this '
          'book")\n'
          '\n'
          '    new_review = Review(**review.model_dump(), '
          'member_id=current_user.id)\n'
          '    db.add(new_review)\n'
          '    db.commit()\n'
          '    db.refresh(new_review)\n'
          '    return new_review\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get("/books/{book_id}/reviews", '
          'response_model=List[ReviewResponse], tags=["Reviews"])\n'
          'def get_book_reviews(book_id: int, db: DBSession):\n'
          '    return db.query(Review).filter(Review.book_id == '
          'book_id).order_by(Review.created_at.desc()).all()\n'

          '    if review.member_id != current_user.id and current_user.role '

          '    fine = db.query(Fine).filter(Fine.id == fine_id).first()\n'
          '    if not fine:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(404, "Fine not found")\n'
          '    if fine.is_paid:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(400, "Fine already paid")\n'
          '    fine.is_paid = True\n'
          '    fine.paid_at = datetime.utcnow()\n'
          '    db.commit()\n'
          '    db.refresh(fine)\n'
          '    return fine\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.post("/fines/{fine_id}/waive", response_model=FineResponse, '
          'tags=["Fines"])\n'
          'def waive_fine(fine_id: int, db: DBSession, _: AdminOnly):\n'
          '    fine = db.query(Fine).filter(Fine.id == fine_id).first()\n'
          '    if not fine:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(404, "Fine not found")\n'
          '    fine.is_paid = True\n'
          '    fine.paid_at = datetime.utcnow()\n'
          '    fine.reason += " [WAIVED BY ADMIN]"\n'
          '    db.commit()\n'

          '    return fine\n'

          '\n'
          '@app.get("/dashboard", response_model=DashboardStats, '
          'tags=["Dashboard"])\n'
          'def get_dashboard(db: DBSession, _: StaffOnly):\n'
          '    """High-level statistics for librarians and admins."""\n'
          '    total_books = db.query(func.sum(Book.total_copies)).scalar() or '
          '0\n'
          '    total_available = '
          'db.query(func.sum(Book.available_copies)).scalar() or 0\n'
          '    total_members = db.query(User).filter(User.role == '
          'UserRole.MEMBER).count()\n'
          '    active_loans = db.query(Loan).filter(Loan.status == '
          'LoanStatus.ACTIVE).count()\n'
          '    overdue_loans = db.query(Loan).filter(\n'
          '        Loan.status == LoanStatus.ACTIVE, Loan.due_date < '
          'datetime.utcnow()\n'
          '    ).count()\n'
          '    reservations = db.query(Reservation).filter(\n'
          '        Reservation.status == ReservationStatus.PENDING\n'
          '    ).count()\n'
          '    unpaid_fines = '
          'db.query(func.sum(Fine.amount)).filter(Fine.is_paid == '
          'False).scalar() or 0\n'
          '\n'
          '    # Top 5 most borrowed books\n'
          '    top_books_q = (\n'
          '        db.query(Book, func.count(Loan.id).label("loan_count"))\n'
          '        .join(Loan, Loan.book_id == Book.id)\n'
          '        .group_by(Book.id)\n'
          '        .order_by(func.count(Loan.id).desc())\n'
          '        .limit(5)\n'
          '        .all()\n'
          '    )\n'
          '    most_borrowed = []\n'
          '    for book, loan_count in top_books_q:\n'
          '        avg_rating = '
          'db.query(func.avg(Review.rating)).filter(Review.book_id == '
          'book.id).scalar()\n'
          '        most_borrowed.append(BookStats(\n'
          '            book_id=book.id, title=book.title,\n'
          '            total_loans=loan_count,\n'
          '            active_loans=db.query(Loan).filter(Loan.book_id == '
          'book.id, Loan.status == LoanStatus.ACTIVE).count(),\n'
          '            average_rating=round(avg_rating, 2) if avg_rating else '
          'None,\n'
          '            total_reviews=db.query(Review).filter(Review.book_id == '
          'book.id).count(),\n'
          '        ))\n'
          '\n'
          '    return DashboardStats(\n'
          '        total_books=int(total_books),\n'
          '        total_available_books=int(total_available),\n'
          '        total_members=total_members,\n'
          '        total_active_loans=active_loans,\n'
          '        total_overdue_loans=overdue_loans,\n'
          '        total_reservations=reservations,\n'
          '        total_unpaid_fines=round(unpaid_fines, 2),\n'
          '        most_borrowed_books=most_borrowed,\n'
          '    )\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get("/dashboard/overdue-report", '
          'response_model=List[LoanResponse], tags=["Dashboard"])\n'
          'def overdue_report(db: DBSession, _: StaffOnly):\n'
          '    """Detailed list of all overdue loans."""\n'
          '    return (\n'
          '        db.query(Loan)\n'
          '        .options(joinedload(Loan.book))\n'
          '        .filter(Loan.status == LoanStatus.ACTIVE, Loan.due_date < '
          'datetime.utcnow())\n'
          '        .order_by(Loan.due_date.asc())\n'
          '        .all()\n'
          '    )\n'
          '\n'
          '# '
          '==============================================================================\n'
          '# ENTRY POINT\n'
