          '    edition:          Optional[str]  = Field(None, max_length=50, '
          'example="First Edition")\n'
          '    language:         str            = Field(default="English", '
          'max_length=50)\n'
          '    pages:            Optional[int]  = Field(None, gt=0, '
          'example=328)\n'
          '    description:      Optional[str]  = Field(None, '
          'max_length=2000)\n'
          '    cover_image_url:  Optional[str]  = None\n'
          '    total_copies:     int            = Field(default=1, ge=1, '
          'example=3)\n'
          '    location_shelf:   Optional[str]  = Field(None, max_length=50, '
          'example="A-12")\n'
          '\n'
          '    @validator("isbn")\n'
          '    def validate_isbn(cls, v):\n'
          '        cleaned = v.replace("-", "").replace(" ", "")\n'
          '        if len(cleaned) not in (10, 13):\n'
          '            raise ValueError("ISBN must be 10 or 13 digits")\n'
          '        return v\n'
          '\n'
          'class BookCreate(BookBase):\n'
          '    pass\n'
          '\n'
          'class BookUpdate(BaseModel):\n'
          '    title:           Optional[str]  = None\n'
          '    category_id:     Optional[str]  = None\n'
          '    publisher:       Optional[str]  = None\n'
          '    published_date:  Optional[date] = None\n'
          '    edition:         Optional[str]  = None\n'
          '    language:        Optional[str]  = None\n'
          '    pages:           Optional[int]  = Field(None, gt=0)\n'
          '    description:     Optional[str]  = None\n'
          '    cover_image_url: Optional[str]  = None\n'
          '    total_copies:    Optional[int]  = Field(None, ge=1)\n'
          '    location_shelf:  Optional[str]  = None\n'
          '    status:          Optional[BookStatus] = None\n'
          '\n'
          'class BookResponse(BookBase):\n'
          '    id:              str\n'
          '    status:          BookStatus\n'
          '    available_copies: int\n'
          '    created_at:      datetime\n'
          '    updated_at:      datetime\n'
          '    author_name:     Optional[str] = None\n'
          '    category_name:   Optional[str] = None\n'
          '\n'
          '# ── Member ─────────────────────────────────\n'
          'class MemberBase(BaseModel):\n'
          '    first_name:   str   = Field(..., min_length=1, max_length=100, '
          'example="Jane")\n'
          '    last_name:    str   = Field(..., min_length=1, max_length=100, '
          'example="Doe")\n'
          '    email:        str   = Field(..., example="jane.doe@email.com")\n'
          '    phone:        Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=20, '
          'example="+1-555-0199")\n'
          '    address:      Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=500)\n'
          '    date_of_birth: Optional[date] = Field(None, '
          'example="1990-05-15")\n'
          '    membership_type: str = Field(default="standard", '
          'example="premium")\n'
          '\n'
          'class MemberCreate(MemberBase):\n'
          '    pass\n'
          '\n'
          'class MemberUpdate(BaseModel):\n'
          '    first_name:      Optional[str]  = None\n'
          '    last_name:       Optional[str]  = None\n'
          '    email:           Optional[str]  = None\n'
          '    phone:           Optional[str]  = None\n'
          '    address:         Optional[str]  = None\n'
          '    membership_type: Optional[str]  = None\n'
          '    status:          Optional[MemberStatus] = None\n'
          '\n'
          'class MemberResponse(MemberBase):\n'
          '    id:               str\n'
          '    status:           MemberStatus\n'
          '    membership_number: str\n'
          '    membership_expiry: date\n'
          '    created_at:       datetime\n'
          '    updated_at:       datetime\n'
          '    active_loans:     int = 0\n'
          '    total_loans:      int = 0\n'
          '    outstanding_fines: float = 0.0\n'
          '\n'
          '# ── Loan ───────────────────────────────────\n'
          'class LoanCreate(BaseModel):\n'
          '    book_id:    str = Field(..., example="book-uuid")\n'
          '    member_id:  str = Field(..., example="member-uuid")\n'
          '    due_date:   Optional[date] = Field(\n'
          '        None,\n'
          '        description="Defaults to 14 days from today",\n'
          '        example="2024-02-15"\n'
          '    )\n'
          '    notes:      Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=500)\n'
          '\n'
          'class LoanReturn(BaseModel):\n'
          '    condition_on_return: str = Field(\n'
          '        default="good",\n'
          '        example="good",\n'
          '        description="good | damaged | lost"\n'
          '    )\n'
          '    notes: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=500)\n'
          '\n'
          'class LoanRenew(BaseModel):\n'
          '    extension_days: int = Field(default=7, ge=1, le=30, example=7)\n'
          '\n'
          'class LoanResponse(BaseModel):\n'
          '    id:                  str\n'
          '    book_id:             str\n'
          '    member_id:           str\n'
          '    book_title:          Optional[str] = None\n'
          '    member_name:         Optional[str] = None\n'
          '    loan_date:           date\n'
          '    due_date:            date\n'
          '    return_date:         Optional[date] = None\n'
          '    status:              LoanStatus\n'
          '    renewal_count:       int = 0\n'
          '    fine_amount:         float = 0.0\n'
          '    condition_on_return: Optional[str] = None\n'
          '    notes:               Optional[str] = None\n'
          '    created_at:          datetime\n'
          '\n'
          '# ── Reservation ────────────────────────────\n'
          'class ReservationCreate(BaseModel):\n'
          '    book_id:   str = Field(..., example="book-uuid")\n'
          '    member_id: str = Field(..., example="member-uuid")\n'
          '    notes:     Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=500)\n'
          '\n'
          'class ReservationResponse(BaseModel):\n'
          '    id:           str\n'
          '    book_id:      str\n'
          '    member_id:    str\n'
          '    book_title:   Optional[str] = None\n'
          '    member_name:  Optional[str] = None\n'
          '    reserved_at:  datetime\n'
          '    expires_at:   datetime\n'
          '    queue_position: int\n'
          '    status:       str\n'
          '    notes:        Optional[str] = None\n'
          '\n'
          '# ── Search & Pagination ────────────────────\n'
          'class PaginatedResponse(BaseModel):\n'
          '    total:    int\n'
          '    page:     int\n'
          '    per_page: int\n'
          '    pages:    int\n'
          '    data:     List\n'
          '\n'
          'class LibraryStats(BaseModel):\n'
          '    total_books:       int\n'
          '    total_copies:      int\n'
          '    available_copies:  int\n'
          '    total_members:     int\n'
          '    active_members:    int\n'
          '    active_loans:      int\n'
          '    overdue_loans:     int\n'
          '    total_reservations: int\n'
          '    books_by_genre:    dict\n'
          '    top_borrowed_books: List[dict]\n'
          '    recent_activity:   List[dict]\n'
          '\n'
          '# ─────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
          '# Helpers / Dependencies\n'
          '# ─────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
          'def generate_id() -> str:\n'
          '    return str(uuid.uuid4())\n'
          '\n'
          'def now() -> datetime:\n'
          '    return datetime.utcnow()\n'
          '\n'
          'def today() -> date:\n'
          '    return date.today()\n'
          '\n'
          'def calculate_fine(due_date: date, return_date: date, rate: float = '
          '0.50) -> float:\n'
          '    """$0.50 per overdue day."""\n'
          '    if return_date > due_date:\n'
          '        overdue_days = (return_date - due_date).days\n'
          '        return round(overdue_days * rate, 2)\n'
          '    return 0.0\n'
          '\n'
          'def get_book_or_404(book_id: str) -> dict:\n'
          '    book = db["books"].get(book_id)\n'
          '    if not book:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"Book '
          '\'{book_id}\' not found")\n'
          '    return book\n'
          '\n'
          'def get_author_or_404(author_id: str) -> dict:\n'
          '    author = db["authors"].get(author_id)\n'
          '    if not author:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"Author '
          '\'{author_id}\' not found")\n'
          '    return author\n'
          '\n'
          'def get_member_or_404(member_id: str) -> dict:\n'
          '    member = db["members"].get(member_id)\n'
          '    if not member:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"Member '
          '\'{member_id}\' not found")\n'
          '    return member\n'
          '\n'
          'def get_loan_or_404(loan_id: str) -> dict:\n'
          '    loan = db["loans"].get(loan_id)\n'
          '    if not loan:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"Loan '
          '\'{loan_id}\' not found")\n'
          '    return loan\n'
          '\n'
          'def paginate(items: list, page: int, per_page: int) -> dict:\n'
          '    total  = len(items)\n'
          '    pages  = max(1, (total + per_page - 1) // per_page)\n'
          '    start  = (page - 1) * per_page\n'
          '    end    = start + per_page\n'
          '    return {\n'
          '        "total":    total,\n'
          '        "page":     page,\n'
          '        "per_page": per_page,\n'
          '        "pages":    pages,\n'
          '        "data":     items[start:end],\n'
          '    }\n'
          '\n'
          '# ─────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
          '# AUTHOR Endpoints\n'
          '# ─────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
          '@app.post(\n'
          '    "/authors",\n'
          '    response_model=AuthorResponse,\n'
          '    status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,\n'
          '    tags=["Authors"],\n'
          '    summary="Create a new author",\n'
          ')\n'
          'def create_author(payload: AuthorCreate):\n'
          '    author_id = generate_id()\n'
          '    author = {\n'
          '        "id":          author_id,\n'
          '        "full_name":   f"{payload.first_name} '
          '{payload.last_name}",\n'
          '        "book_count":  0,\n'
          '        "created_at":  now(),\n'
          '        "updated_at":  now(),\n'
          '        **payload.dict(),\n'
          '    }\n'
          '    db["authors"][author_id] = author\n'
          '    return author\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get(\n'
          '    "/authors",\n'
          '    response_model=PaginatedResponse,\n'
          '    tags=["Authors"],\n'
          '    summary="List all authors with optional search",\n'
          ')\n'
          'def list_authors(\n'
          '    search:   Optional[str] = Query(None, description="Search by '
          'name or nationality"),\n'
          '    page:     int = Query(1,  ge=1,  description="Page number"),\n'
          '    per_page: int = Query(10, ge=1, le=100, description="Results '
          'per page"),\n'
          '    sort_by:  str = Query("last_name", description="Field to sort '
          'by"),\n'
          '    order:    SortOrder = Query(SortOrder.ASC),\n'
          '):\n'
          '    authors = list(db["authors"].values())\n'
          '    if search:\n'
          '        s = search.lower()\n'
          '        authors = [\n'
          '            a for a in authors\n'
          '            if s in a["first_name"].lower()\n'
          '            or s in a["last_name"].lower()\n'
          '            or (a.get("nationality") and s in '
          'a["nationality"].lower())\n'
          '        ]\n'
          '    reverse = order == SortOrder.DESC\n'
          '    authors = sorted(authors, key=lambda a: a.get(sort_by, ""), '
          'reverse=reverse)\n'
          '    return paginate(authors, page, per_page)\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get(\n'
          '    "/authors/{author_id}",\n'
          '    response_model=AuthorResponse,\n'
          '    tags=["Authors"],\n'
          '    summary="Get a single author by ID",\n'
          ')\n'
          'def get_author(author_id: str = Path(..., description="Author '
          'UUID")):\n'
          '    return get_author_or_404(author_id)\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.patch(\n'
          '    "/authors/{author_id}",\n'
          '    response_model=AuthorResponse,\n'
          '    tags=["Authors"],\n'
          '    summary="Update author details",\n'
          ')\n'
          'def update_author(\n'
          '    payload:   AuthorUpdate,\n'
          '    author_id: str = Path(..., description="Author UUID"),\n'
          '):\n'
          '    author = get_author_or_404(author_id)\n'
          '    updates = payload.dict(exclude_unset=True)\n'
          '    author.update(updates)\n'
          '    author["updated_at"] = now()\n'
          '    if "first_name" in updates or "last_name" in updates:\n'
          '        author["full_name"] = f"{author[\'first_name\']} '
          '{author[\'last_name\']}"\n'
          '    db["authors"][author_id] = author\n'
          '    return author\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.delete(\n'
          '    "/authors/{author_id}",\n'
          '    status_code=status.HTTP_204_NO_CONTENT,\n'
          '    tags=["Authors"],\n'
          '    summary="Delete an author",\n'
          ')\n'
          'def delete_author(author_id: str = Path(..., description="Author '
          'UUID")):\n'
          '    get_author_or_404(author_id)\n'
          '    has_books = any(b["author_id"] == author_id for b in '
          'db["books"].values())\n'
          '    if has_books:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(\n'
          '            status_code=409,\n'
          '            detail="Cannot delete author with existing books. '
          'Remove books first.",\n'
          '        )\n'
          '    del db["authors"][author_id]\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get(\n'
          '    "/authors/{author_id}/books",\n'
          '    tags=["Authors"],\n'
          '    summary="Get all books by a specific author",\n'
          ')\n'
          'def get_books_by_author(author_id: str = Path(..., '
          'description="Author UUID")):\n'
          '    get_author_or_404(author_id)\n'
          '    books = [b for b in db["books"].values() if b["author_id"] == '
          'author_id]\n'
          '    return {"author_id": author_id, "count": len(books), "books": '
          'books}\n'
          '\n'
          '# ─────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
          '# CATEGORY Endpoints\n'
          '# ─────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
          '@app.post(\n'
          '    "/categories",\n'
          '    response_model=CategoryResponse,\n'
          '    status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,\n'
          '    tags=["Categories"],\n'
          '    summary="Create a new category",\n'
          ')\n'
          'def create_category(payload: CategoryCreate):\n'
          '    existing = [c for c in db["categories"].values() if '
          'c["name"].lower() == payload.name.lower()]\n'
          '    if existing:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="Category with '
          'this name already exists")\n'
          '    cat_id = generate_id()\n'
          '    category = {\n'
          '        "id":         cat_id,\n'
          '        "book_count": 0,\n'
          '        "created_at": now(),\n'
          '        **payload.dict(),\n'
          '    }\n'
          '    db["categories"][cat_id] = category\n'
          '    return category\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get(\n'
          '    "/categories",\n'
          '    response_model=List[CategoryResponse],\n'
          '    tags=["Categories"],\n'
          '    summary="List all categories",\n'
          ')\n'
          'def list_categories(genre: Optional[Genre] = Query(None, '
          'description="Filter by genre")):\n'
          '    categories = list(db["categories"].values())\n'
          '    if genre:\n'
          '        categories = [c for c in categories if c["genre"] == '
          'genre]\n'
          '    return categories\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get(\n'
          '    "/categories/{category_id}",\n'
          '    response_model=CategoryResponse,\n'
          '    tags=["Categories"],\n'
          '    summary="Get a single category",\n'
          ')\n'
          'def get_category(category_id: str = Path(...)):\n'
          '    cat = db["categories"].get(category_id)\n'
          '    if not cat:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Category not '
          'found")\n'
          '    return cat\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.patch(\n'
          '    "/categories/{category_id}",\n'
          '    response_model=CategoryResponse,\n'
          '    tags=["Categories"],\n'
          '    summary="Update a category",\n'
          ')\n'
          'def update_category(payload: CategoryUpdate, category_id: str = '
          'Path(...)):\n'
          '    cat = db["categories"].get(category_id)\n'
          '    if not cat:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Category not '
          'found")\n'
          '    cat.update(payload.dict(exclude_unset=True))\n'
          '    db["categories"][category_id] = cat\n'
          '    return cat\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.delete(\n'
          '    "/categories/{category_id}",\n'
          '    status_code=status.HTTP_204_NO_CONTENT,\n'
          '    tags=["Categories"],\n'
          '    summary="Delete a category",\n'
          ')\n'
          'def delete_category(category_id: str = Path(...)):\n'
          '    if not db["categories"].get(category_id):\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Category not '
          'found")\n'
          '    if any(b["category_id"] == category_id for b in '
          'db["books"].values()):\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="Category in '
          'use by existing books")\n'
          '    del db["categories"][category_id]\n'
          '\n'
          '# ─────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
          '# BOOK Endpoints\n'
          '# ─────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
          '@app.post(\n'
          '    "/books",\n'
          '    response_model=BookResponse,\n'
          '    status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,\n'
          '    tags=["Books"],\n'
          '    summary="Add a new book to the library",\n'
          ')\n'
          'def create_book(payload: BookCreate):\n'
          '    get_author_or_404(payload.author_id)\n'
          '    if not db["categories"].get(payload.category_id):\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Category not '
          'found")\n'
          '    if any(b["isbn"] == payload.isbn for b in '
          'db["books"].values()):\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="A book with '
          'this ISBN already exists")\n'
          '\n'
          '    book_id = generate_id()\n'
          '    author  = db["authors"][payload.author_id]\n'
          '    cat     = db["categories"][payload.category_id]\n'
          '    book    = {\n'
          '        "id":               book_id,\n'
          '        "status":           BookStatus.AVAILABLE,\n'
          '        "available_copies": payload.total_copies,\n'
          '        "author_name":      author["full_name"],\n'
          '        "category_name":    cat["name"],\n'
          '        "created_at":       now(),\n'
          '        "updated_at":       now(),\n'
          '        **payload.dict(),\n'
          '    }\n'
          '    db["books"][book_id] = book\n'
          '\n'
          '    # Update counts\n'
          '    db["authors"][payload.author_id]["book_count"] += 1\n'
          '    db["categories"][payload.category_id]["book_count"] += 1\n'
          '    return book\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get(\n'
          '    "/books",\n'
          '    response_model=PaginatedResponse,\n'
          '    tags=["Books"],\n'
          '    summary="Search and list books",\n'
          ')\n'
          'def list_books(\n'
          '    search:      Optional[str]        = Query(None, '
          'description="Search title, ISBN, author"),\n'
          '    status:      Optional[BookStatus] = Query(None, '
          'description="Filter by book status"),\n'
          '    genre:       Optional[Genre]      = Query(None, '
          'description="Filter by genre"),\n'
          '    author_id:   Optional[str]        = Query(None, '
          'description="Filter by author"),\n'
          '    category_id: Optional[str]        = Query(None, '
          'description="Filter by category"),\n'
          '    language:    Optional[str]        = Query(None, '
          'description="Filter by language"),\n'
          '    page:        int = Query(1,  ge=1),\n'
          '    per_page:    int = Query(10, ge=1, le=100),\n'
          '    sort_by:     str = Query("title", description="title | '
          'published_date | created_at"),\n'
          '    order:       SortOrder = Query(SortOrder.ASC),\n'
          '):\n'
          '    books = list(db["books"].values())\n'
          '\n'
          '    if search:\n'
          '        s = search.lower()\n'
          '        books = [\n'
          '            b for b in books\n'
          '            if s in b["title"].lower()\n'
          '            or s in b["isbn"]\n'
          '            or s in (b.get("author_name") or "").lower()\n'
          '        ]\n'
          '    if status:\n'
          '        books = [b for b in books if b["status"] == status]\n'
          '    if author_id:\n'
          '        books = [b for b in books if b["author_id"] == author_id]\n'
          '    if category_id:\n'
          '        books = [b for b in books if b["category_id"] == '
          'category_id]\n'
          '    if language:\n'
          '        books = [b for b in books if b["language"].lower() == '
          'language.lower()]\n'
          '    if genre:\n'
          '        cat_ids = {c["id"] for c in db["categories"].values() if '
          'c["genre"] == genre}\n'
          '        books = [b for b in books if b["category_id"] in cat_ids]\n'
          '\n'
          '    reverse = order == SortOrder.DESC\n'
          '    books   = sorted(books, key=lambda b: str(b.get(sort_by, "")), '
          'reverse=reverse)\n'
          '    return paginate(books, page, per_page)\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get(\n'
          '    "/books/{book_id}",\n'
          '    response_model=BookResponse,\n'
          '    tags=["Books"],\n'
          '    summary="Get a single book\'s details",\n'
          ')\n'
          'def get_book(book_id: str = Path(..., description="Book UUID")):\n'
          '    return get_book_or_404(book_id)\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.patch(\n'
          '    "/books/{book_id}",\n'
          '    response_model=BookResponse,\n'
          '    tags=["Books"],\n'
          '    summary="Update book information",\n'
          ')\n'
          'def update_book(\n'
          '    payload: BookUpdate,\n'
          '    book_id: str = Path(..., description="Book UUID"),\n'
          '):\n'
          '    book    = get_book_or_404(book_id)\n'
          '    updates = payload.dict(exclude_unset=True)\n'
          '\n'
          '    if "total_copies" in updates:\n'
          '        loaned_copies = book["total_copies"] - '
          'book["available_copies"]\n'
          '        new_available = updates["total_copies"] - loaned_copies\n'
          '        if new_available < 0:\n'
          '            raise HTTPException(\n'
          '                status_code=400,\n'
          '                detail=f"Cannot reduce copies below currently '
          'loaned amount ({loaned_copies})"\n'
          '            )\n'
          '        updates["available_copies"] = new_available\n'
          '\n'
          '    book.update(updates)\n'
          '    book["updated_at"] = now()\n'
          '    db["books"][book_id] = book\n'
          '    return book\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.delete(\n'
          '    "/books/{book_id}",\n'
          '    status_code=status.HTTP_204_NO_CONTENT,\n'
          '    tags=["Books"],\n'
          '    summary="Remove a book from the library",\n'
          ')\n'
          'def delete_book(book_id: str = Path(..., description="Book '
          'UUID")):\n'
          '    book = get_book_or_404(book_id)\n'
          '    active = [\n'
          '        l for l in db["loans"].values()\n'
          '        if l["book_id"] == book_id and l["status"] in '
          '(LoanStatus.ACTIVE, LoanStatus.OVERDUE)\n'
          '    ]\n'
          '    if active:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="Book has '
          'active loans and cannot be deleted")\n'
          '    db["authors"][book["author_id"]]["book_count"] -= 1\n'
          '    db["categories"][book["category_id"]]["book_count"] -= 1\n'
          '    del db["books"][book_id]\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get(\n'
          '    "/books/{book_id}/availability",\n'
          '    tags=["Books"],\n'
          '    summary="Check real-time availability of a book",\n'
          ')\n'
          'def check_book_availability(book_id: str = Path(...)):\n'
          '    book = get_book_or_404(book_id)\n'
          '    active_loans = [\n'
          '        l for l in db["loans"].values()\n'
          '        if l["book_id"] == book_id and l["status"] in '
          '(LoanStatus.ACTIVE, LoanStatus.RENEWED, LoanStatus.OVERDUE)\n'
          '    ]\n'
          '    reservations = [\n'
          '        r for r in db["reservations"].values()\n'
          '        if r["book_id"] == book_id and r["status"] == "active"\n'
          '    ]\n'
          '    return {\n'
          '        "book_id":          book_id,\n'
          '        "title":            book["title"],\n'
          '        "total_copies":     book["total_copies"],\n'
          '        "available_copies": book["available_copies"],\n'
          '        "status":           book["status"],\n'
          '        "active_loans":     len(active_loans),\n'
          '        "reservations_queue": len(reservations),\n'
          '        "is_available":     book["available_copies"] > 0,\n'
          '        "next_available_estimate": (\n'
          '            min(l["due_date"] for l in active_loans) if '
          'active_loans and book["available_copies"] == 0 else None\n'
          '        ),\n'
          '    }\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get(\n'
          '    "/books/{book_id}/loan-history",\n'
          '    tags=["Books"],\n'
          '    summary="Get full loan history for a book",\n'
          ')\n'
          'def get_book_loan_history(\n'
          '    book_id:  str = Path(...),\n'
          '    page:     int = Query(1, ge=1),\n'
          '    per_page: int = Query(10, ge=1, le=100),\n'
          '):\n'
          '    get_book_or_404(book_id)\n'
          '    loans = [l for l in db["loans"].values() if l["book_id"] == '
          'book_id]\n'
          '    loans.sort(key=lambda l: l["loan_date"], reverse=True)\n'
          '    return paginate(loans, page, per_page)\n'
          '\n'
          '# ─────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
          '# MEMBER Endpoints\n'
          '# ─────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
          '@app.post(\n'
          '    "/members",\n'
          '    response_model=MemberResponse,\n'
          '    status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,\n'
          '    tags=["Members"],\n'
          '    summary="Register a new library member",\n'
          ')\n'
          'def create_member(payload: MemberCreate):\n'
          '    if any(m["email"] == payload.email for m in '
          'db["members"].values()):\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="Email already '
          'registered")\n'
          '\n'
          '    member_id = generate_id()\n'
          '    member_num = f"LIB-{str(member_id)[:8].upper()}"\n'
          '    member = {\n'
          '        "id":                member_id,\n'
          '        "status":            MemberStatus.ACTIVE,\n'
          '        "membership_number": member_num,\n'
          '        "membership_expiry": (today() + '
          'timedelta(days=365)).isoformat(),\n'
          '        "active_loans":      0,\n'
          '        "total_loans":       0,\n'
          '        "outstanding_fines": 0.0,\n'
          '        "created_at":        now(),\n'
          '        "updated_at":        now(),\n'
          '        **payload.dict(),\n'
          '    }\n'
          '    db["members"][member_id] = member\n'
          '    return member\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get(\n'
          '    "/members",\n'
          '    response_model=PaginatedResponse,\n'
          '    tags=["Members"],\n'
          '    summary="List all members",\n'
          ')\n'
          'def list_members(\n'
          '    search:          Optional[str]         = Query(None, '
          'description="Search name, email, or membership number"),\n'
          '    status:          Optional[MemberStatus] = Query(None),\n'
          '    membership_type: Optional[str]         = Query(None),\n'
          '    page:            int = Query(1, ge=1),\n'
          '    per_page:        int = Query(10, ge=1, le=100),\n'
          '):\n'
          '    members = list(db["members"].values())\n'
          '    if search:\n'
          '        s = search.lower()\n'
          '        members = [\n'
          '            m for m in members\n'
          '            if s in m["first_name"].lower()\n'
          '            or s in m["last_name"].lower()\n'
          '            or s in m["email"].lower()\n'
          '            or s in m["membership_number"].lower()\n'
          '        ]\n'
          '    if status:\n'
          '        members = [m for m in members if m["status"] == status]\n'
          '    if membership_type:\n'
          '        members = [m for m in members if m["membership_type"] == '
          'membership_type]\n'
          '    return paginate(members, page, per_page)\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get(\n'
          '    "/members/{member_id}",\n'
          '    response_model=MemberResponse,\n'
          '    tags=["Members"],\n'
          '    summary="Get a member\'s profile",\n'
          ')\n'
          'def get_member(member_id: str = Path(...)):\n'
          '    return get_member_or_404(member_id)\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.patch(\n'
          '    "/members/{member_id}",\n'
          '    response_model=MemberResponse,\n'
          '    tags=["Members"],\n'
          '    summary="Update member information",\n'
          ')\n'
          'def update_member(payload: MemberUpdate, member_id: str = '
          'Path(...)):\n'
          '    member  = get_member_or_404(member_id)\n'
          '    updates = payload.dict(exclude_unset=True)\n'
          '    if "email" in updates:\n'
          '        conflict = [\n'
          '            m for m in db["members"].values()\n'
          '            if m["email"] == updates["email"] and m["id"] != '
          'member_id\n'
          '        ]\n'
          '        if conflict:\n'
          '            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="Email '
          'already in use")\n'
          '    member.update(updates)\n'
          '    member["updated_at"] = now()\n'
          '    db["members"][member_id] = member\n'
          '    return member\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.delete(\n'
          '    "/members/{member_id}",\n'
          '    status_code=status.HTTP_204_NO_CONTENT,\n'
          '    tags=["Members"],\n'
          '    summary="Delete a member",\n'
          ')\n'
          'def delete_member(member_id: str = Path(...)):\n'
          '    member = get_member_or_404(member_id)\n'
          '    if member["active_loans"] > 0:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="Member has '
          'active loans. Resolve before deletion.")\n'
          '    if member["outstanding_fines"] > 0:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="Member has '
          'outstanding fines. Clear before deletion.")\n'
          '    del db["members"][member_id]\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.post(\n'
          '    "/members/{member_id}/renew",\n'
          '    response_model=MemberResponse,\n'
          '    tags=["Members"],\n'
          '    summary="Renew a member\'s membership",\n'
          ')\n'
          'def renew_membership(\n'
          '    member_id: str = Path(...),\n'
          '    months:    int = Query(12, ge=1, le=60, description="Months to '
          'extend"),\n'
          '):\n'
          '    member = get_member_or_404(member_id)\n'
          '    current_expiry = '
          'date.fromisoformat(str(member["membership_expiry"]))\n'
          '    base = max(current_expiry, today())\n'
          '    member["membership_expiry"] = (base + timedelta(days=30 * '
          'months)).isoformat()\n'
          '    member["status"]            = MemberStatus.ACTIVE\n'
          '    member["updated_at"]        = now()\n'
          '    db["members"][member_id]    = member\n'
          '    return member\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get(\n'
          '    "/members/{member_id}/loans",\n'
          '    tags=["Members"],\n'
          '    summary="Get all loans for a member",\n'
          ')\n'
          'def get_member_loans(\n'
          '    member_id: str = Path(...),\n'
          '    status:    Optional[LoanStatus] = Query(None),\n'
          '):\n'
          '    get_member_or_404(member_id)\n'
          '    loans = [l for l in db["loans"].values() if l["member_id"] == '
          'member_id]\n'
          '    if status:\n'
          '        loans = [l for l in loans if l["status"] == status]\n'
          '    loans.sort(key=lambda l: l["loan_date"], reverse=True)\n'
          '    return {"member_id": member_id, "count": len(loans), "loans": '
          'loans}\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get(\n'
          '    "/members/{member_id}/fines",\n'
          '    tags=["Members"],\n'
          '    summary="Get outstanding fines for a member",\n'
          ')\n'
          'def get_member_fines(member_id: str = Path(...)):\n'
          '    get_member_or_404(member_id)\n'
          '    overdue_loans = [\n'
          '        l for l in db["loans"].values()\n'
          '        if l["member_id"] == member_id and l["fine_amount"] > 0\n'
          '    ]\n'
          '    total_fines = sum(l["fine_amount"] for l in overdue_loans)\n'
          '    return {\n'
          '        "member_id":      member_id,\n'
          '        "total_fines":    total_fines,\n'
          '        "fined_loans":    overdue_loans,\n'
          '        "fine_breakdown": [\n'
          '            {\n'
          '                "loan_id":    l["id"],\n'
          '                "book_title": l.get("book_title"),\n'
          '                "fine":       l["fine_amount"],\n'
          '                "due_date":   l["due_date"],\n'
          '            }\n'
          '            for l in overdue_loans\n'
          '        ],\n'
          '    }\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.post(\n'
          '    "/members/{member_id}/pay-fines",\n'
          '    tags=["Members"],\n'
          '    summary="Record fine payment for a member",\n'
          ')\n'
          'def pay_fines(\n'
          '    member_id: str  = Path(...),\n'
          '    amount:    float = Query(..., gt=0, description="Amount paid in '
          'USD"),\n'
          '):\n'
          '    member = get_member_or_404(member_id)\n'
          '    outstanding = member["outstanding_fines"]\n'
          '    if amount > outstanding:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=f"Payment '
          'exceeds outstanding fines (${outstanding:.2f})")\n'
          '    member["outstanding_fines"] = round(outstanding - amount, 2)\n'
          '    member["updated_at"]        = now()\n'
          '    db["members"][member_id]    = member\n'
          '    return {\n'
          '        "message":        "Payment recorded",\n'
          '        "amount_paid":    amount,\n'
          '        "remaining_fines": member["outstanding_fines"],\n'
          '    }\n'
          '\n'
          '# ─────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
          '# LOAN Endpoints\n'
          '# ─────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
          '@app.post(\n'
          '    "/loans",\n'
          '    response_model=LoanResponse,\n'
          '    status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,\n'
          '    tags=["Loans"],\n'
          '    summary="Issue a book loan to a member",\n'
          ')\n'
          'def create_loan(payload: LoanCreate):\n'
          '    book   = get_book_or_404(payload.book_id)\n'
          '    member = get_member_or_404(payload.member_id)\n'
          '\n'
          '    # Validations\n'
          '    if member["status"] != MemberStatus.ACTIVE:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Member account '
          'is not active")\n'
          '    if member["outstanding_fines"] > 0:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Member has '
          'unpaid fines. Clear before borrowing.")\n'
          '    if date.fromisoformat(str(member["membership_expiry"])) < '
          'today():\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Member '
          'membership has expired")\n'
          '    if book["available_copies"] < 1:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="No copies '
          'available. Consider reserving the book.")\n'
          '\n'
          '    active_member_loans = [\n'
          '        l for l in db["loans"].values()\n'
          '        if l["member_id"] == payload.member_id\n'
          '        and l["status"] in (LoanStatus.ACTIVE, LoanStatus.RENEWED, '
          'LoanStatus.OVERDUE)\n'
          '    ]\n'
          '    if len(active_member_loans) >= 5:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Member has '
          'reached the maximum loan limit (5)")\n'
          '\n'
          '    already_borrowed = any(\n'
          '        l["book_id"] == payload.book_id\n'
          '        for l in active_member_loans\n'
          '    )\n'
          '    if already_borrowed:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="Member already '
          'has this book on loan")\n'
          '\n'
          '    due = payload.due_date or (today() + timedelta(days=14))\n'
          '    loan_id = generate_id()\n'
          '    loan = {\n'
          '        "id":                  loan_id,\n'
          '        "book_id":             payload.book_id,\n'
          '        "member_id":           payload.member_id,\n'
          '        "book_title":          book["title"],\n'
          '        "member_name":         f"{member[\'first_name\']} '
          '{member[\'last_name\']}",\n'
          '        "loan_date":           today(),\n'
          '        "due_date":            due,\n'
          '        "return_date":         None,\n'
          '        "status":              LoanStatus.ACTIVE,\n'
          '        "renewal_count":       0,\n'
          '        "fine_amount":         0.0,\n'
          '        "condition_on_return": None,\n'
          '        "notes":               payload.notes,\n'
          '        "created_at":          now(),\n'
          '    }\n'
          '    db["loans"][loan_id] = loan\n'
          '\n'
          '    # Update book & member counts\n'
          '    db["books"][payload.book_id]["available_copies"] -= 1\n'
          '    if db["books"][payload.book_id]["available_copies"] == 0:\n'
          '        db["books"][payload.book_id]["status"] = BookStatus.LOANED\n'
          '    db["members"][payload.member_id]["active_loans"] += 1\n'
          '    db["members"][payload.member_id]["total_loans"]  += 1\n'
          '\n'
          '    return loan\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get(\n'
          '    "/loans",\n'
          '    response_model=PaginatedResponse,\n'
          '    tags=["Loans"],\n'
          '    summary="List and filter all loans",\n'
          ')\n'
          'def list_loans(\n'
          '    status:    Optional[LoanStatus] = Query(None),\n'
          '    member_id: Optional[str]        = Query(None),\n'
          '    book_id:   Optional[str]        = Query(None),\n'
          '    overdue:   Optional[bool]       = Query(None, '
          'description="Filter only overdue loans"),\n'
          '    page:      int = Query(1, ge=1),\n'
          '    per_page:  int = Query(10, ge=1, le=100),\n'
          '):\n'
          '    loans = list(db["loans"].values())\n'
          '\n'
          '    # Auto-update overdue status\n'
          '    for loan in loans:\n'
          '        if (\n'
          '            loan["status"] == LoanStatus.ACTIVE\n'
          '            and date.fromisoformat(str(loan["due_date"])) < '
          'today()\n'
          '        ):\n'
          '            loan["status"]      = LoanStatus.OVERDUE\n'
          '            loan["fine_amount"] = calculate_fine(\n'
          '                date.fromisoformat(str(loan["due_date"])), today()\n'
          '            )\n'
          '            db["loans"][loan["id"]] = loan\n'
          '\n'
          '    if status:\n'
          '        loans = [l for l in loans if l["status"] == status]\n'
          '    if member_id:\n'
          '        loans = [l for l in loans if l["member_id"] == member_id]\n'
          '    if book_id:\n'
          '        loans = [l for l in loans if l["book_id"] == book_id]\n'
          '    if overdue is True:\n'
          '        loans = [l for l in loans if l["status"] == '
          'LoanStatus.OVERDUE]\n'
          '\n'
          '    loans.sort(key=lambda l: str(l["loan_date"]), reverse=True)\n'
          '    return paginate(loans, page, per_page)\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get(\n'
          '    "/loans/{loan_id}",\n'
          '    response_model=LoanResponse,\n'
          '    tags=["Loans"],\n'
          '    summary="Get details of a single loan",\n'
          ')\n'
          'def get_loan(loan_id: str = Path(...)):\n'
          '    return get_loan_or_404(loan_id)\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.post(\n'
          '    "/loans/{loan_id}/return",\n'
          '    response_model=LoanResponse,\n'
          '    tags=["Loans"],\n'
          '    summary="Process a book return",\n'
          ')\n'
          'def return_book(payload: LoanReturn, loan_id: str = Path(...)):\n'
          '    loan = get_loan_or_404(loan_id)\n'
          '    if loan["status"] == LoanStatus.RETURNED:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Book has '
          'already been returned")\n'
          '\n'
          '    return_date  = today()\n'
          '    due_date_obj = date.fromisoformat(str(loan["due_date"]))\n'
          '    fine         = calculate_fine(due_date_obj, return_date)\n'
          '\n'
          '    loan["status"]              = LoanStatus.RETURNED\n'
          '    loan["return_date"]         = return_date\n'
          '    loan["fine_amount"]         = fine\n'
          '    loan["condition_on_return"] = payload.condition_on_return\n'
          '    loan["notes"]               = payload.notes or loan["notes"]\n'
          '    db["loans"][loan_id]        = loan\n'
          '\n'
          '    # Restore book copy\n'
          '    book = db["books"][loan["book_id"]]\n'
          '    book["available_copies"] += 1\n'
          '    if book["available_copies"] > 0:\n'
          '        book["status"] = BookStatus.AVAILABLE\n'
          '    if payload.condition_on_return == "lost":\n'
          '        book["available_copies"] -= 1\n'
          '        book["total_copies"]     -= 1\n'
          '        book["status"] = BookStatus.LOST if book["total_copies"] == '
          '0 else BookStatus.AVAILABLE\n'
          '    db["books"][loan["book_id"]] = book\n'
          '\n'
          '    # Update member\n'
          '    member = db["members"][loan["member_id"]]\n'
          '    member["active_loans"]      = max(0, member["active_loans"] - '
          '1)\n'
          '    member["outstanding_fines"] = round(member["outstanding_fines"] '
          '+ fine, 2)\n'
          '    db["members"][loan["member_id"]] = member\n'
          '\n'
          '    return loan\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.post(\n'
          '    "/loans/{loan_id}/renew",\n'
          '    response_model=LoanResponse,\n'
          '    tags=["Loans"],\n'
          '    summary="Renew an active loan",\n'
          ')\n'
          'def renew_loan(payload: LoanRenew, loan_id: str = Path(...)):\n'
          '    loan = get_loan_or_404(loan_id)\n'
          '\n'
          '    if loan["status"] not in (LoanStatus.ACTIVE, '
          'LoanStatus.RENEWED):\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Only active or '
          'previously renewed loans can be renewed")\n'
          '    if loan["renewal_count"] >= 3:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Maximum '
          'renewals (3) reached for this loan")\n'
          '\n'
          "    # Check if there's a reservation by another member\n"
          '    reservations = [\n'
          '        r for r in db["reservations"].values()\n'
          '        if r["book_id"] == loan["book_id"] and r["status"] == '
          '"active"\n'
          '    ]\n'
          '    if reservations:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(\n'
          '            status_code=400,\n'
          '            detail="Cannot renew: another member has reserved this '
          'book"\n'
          '        )\n'
          '\n'
          '    old_due           = date.fromisoformat(str(loan["due_date"]))\n'
          '    loan["due_date"]  = (old_due + '
          'timedelta(days=payload.extension_days)).isoformat()\n'
          '    loan["status"]    = LoanStatus.RENEWED\n'
          '    loan["renewal_count"] += 1\n'
          '    db["loans"][loan_id]  = loan\n'
          '    return loan\n'
          '\n'
          '# ─────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
          '# RESERVATION Endpoints\n'
          '# ─────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
          '@app.post(\n'
          '    "/reservations",\n'
          '    response_model=ReservationResponse,\n'
          '    status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,\n'
          '    tags=["Reservations"],\n'
          '    summary="Reserve a book for a member",\n'
          ')\n'
          'def create_reservation(payload: ReservationCreate):\n'
          '    book   = get_book_or_404(payload.book_id)\n'
          '    member = get_member_or_404(payload.member_id)\n'
          '\n'
          '    if member["status"] != MemberStatus.ACTIVE:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Member account '
          'is not active")\n'
          '\n'
          '    existing = any(\n'
          '        r["book_id"]   == payload.book_id\n'
          '        and r["member_id"] == payload.member_id\n'
          '        and r["status"] == "active"\n'
          '        for r in db["reservations"].values()\n'
          '    )\n'
          '    if existing:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="Member already '
          'has an active reservation for this book")\n'
          '\n'
          '    queue = [\n'
          '        r for r in db["reservations"].values()\n'
          '        if r["book_id"] == payload.book_id and r["status"] == '
          '"active"\n'
          '    ]\n'
          '    res_id = generate_id()\n'
          '    reservation = {\n'
          '        "id":             res_id,\n'
          '        "book_id":        payload.book_id,\n'
          '        "member_id":      payload.member_id,\n'
          '        "book_title":     book["title"],\n'
          '        "member_name":    f"{member[\'first_name\']} '
          '{member[\'last_name\']}",\n'
          '        "reserved_at":    now(),\n'
          '        "expires_at":     now() + timedelta(days=7),\n'
          '        "queue_position": len(queue) + 1,\n'
          '        "status":         "active",\n'
          '        "notes":          payload.notes,\n'
          '    }\n'
          '    db["reservations"][res_id] = reservation\n'
          '    db["books"][payload.book_id]["status"] = BookStatus.RESERVED\n'
          '    return reservation\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get(\n'
          '    "/reservations",\n'
          '    response_model=PaginatedResponse,\n'
          '    tags=["Reservations"],\n'
          '    summary="List all reservations",\n'
          ')\n'
          'def list_reservations(\n'
          '    book_id:   Optional[str] = Query(None),\n'
          '    member_id: Optional[str] = Query(None),\n'
          '    status:    Optional[str] = Query(None, description="active | '
          'fulfilled | cancelled | expired"),\n'
          '    page:      int = Query(1, ge=1),\n'
          '    per_page:  int = Query(10, ge=1, le=100),\n'
          '):\n'
          '    reservations = list(db["reservations"].values())\n'
          '    if book_id:\n'
          '        reservations = [r for r in reservations if r["book_id"] == '
          'book_id]\n'
          '    if member_id:\n'
          '        reservations = [r for r in reservations if r["member_id"] '
          '== member_id]\n'
          '    if status:\n'
          '        reservations = [r for r in reservations if r["status"] == '
          'status]\n'
          '    return paginate(reservations, page, per_page)\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.get(\n'
          '    "/reservations/{reservation_id}",\n'
          '    response_model=ReservationResponse,\n'
          '    tags=["Reservations"],\n'
          '    summary="Get a specific reservation",\n'
          ')\n'
          'def get_reservation(reservation_id: str = Path(...)):\n'
          '    res = db["reservations"].get(reservation_id)\n'
          '    if not res:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Reservation '
          'not found")\n'
          '    return res\n'
          '\n'
          '@app.delete(\n'
          '    "/reservations/{reservation_id}",\n'
          '    status_code=status.HTTP_204_NO_CONTENT,\n'
          '    tags=["Reservations"],\n'
          '    summary="Cancel a reservation",\n'
          ')\n'
          'def cancel_reservation(reservation_id: str = Path(...)):\n'
          '    res = db["reservations"].get(reservation_id)\n'
          '    if not res:\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Reservation '
          'not found")\n'
          '    if res["status"] != "active":\n'
          '        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Only active '
          'reservations can be cancelled")\n'
          '    res["status"] = "cancelled"\n'
          '    db["reservations"][reservation_id] = res\n'
          '\n'
          '    # Re-calculate queue positions for remaining reservations\n'
          '    remaining = sorted(\n'
          '        [r for r in db["reservations"].values()\n'
          '         if r["book_id"] == res["book_id"] and r["status"] == '
          '"active"],\n'
          '        key=lambda r: r["reserved_at"],\n'
          '    )\n'
          '    for idx, r in enumerate(remaining):\n'
          '        r["queue_position"] = idx + 1\n'
          '        db["reservations"][r["id"]] = r\n'
          '\n'
          '# ─────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
          '# SEARCH Endpoint (cross-entity)\n'

          '    include:  Optional[str] = Query(\n'
          '        "books,authors,members",\n'
          '        description="Comma-separated entities to search"\n'
          '    ),\n'
          '    page:     int = Query(1, ge=1),\n'
          '    per_page: int = Query(10, ge=1, le=50),\n'

          '    results  = {}\n'
          '\n'
          '    if "books" in entities:\n'
          '        s = q.lower()\n'
          '        books = [\n'
          '            b for b in db["books"].values()\n'
          '            if s in b["title"].lower()\n'
          '            or s in b["isbn"]\n'
          '            or s in (b.get("author_name") or "").lower()\n'
          '            or s in (b.get("description") or "").lower()\n'
          '        ]\n'
          '        results["books"] = {"count": len(books), "items": '
          'books[:per_page]}\n'
          '\n'
          '    if "authors" in entities:\n'
          '        s = q.lower()\n'
          '        authors = [\n'
          '            a for a in db["authors"].values()\n'
          '            if s in a["first_name"].lower()\n'
          '            or s in a["last_name"].lower()\n'
          '            or s in (a.get("biography") or "").lower()\n'
          '        ]\n'
          '        results["authors"] = {"count": len(authors), "items": '
          'authors[:per_page]}\n'
          '\n'
          '    if "members" in entities:\n'
          '        s = q.lower()\n'
          '        members = [\n'
          '            m for m in db["members"].values()\n'
          '            if s in m["first_name"].lower()\n'
          '            or s in m["last_name"].lower()\n'
          '            or s in m["email"].lower()\n'
          '            or s in m["membership_number"].lower()\n'
          '        ]\n'
          '        results["members"] = {"count": len(members), "items": '
          'members[:per_page]}\n'
          '\n'
          '    total_results = sum(v["count"] for v in results.values())\n'
          '    return {\n'
          '        "query":         q,\n'
          '        "total_results": total_results,\n'
          '        "results":       results,\n'
          '    }\n'

          '\n'
          '    # Books by genre\n'
          '    books_by_genre: dict = {}\n'
          '    for book in books:\n'
          '        cat = db["categories"].get(book["category_id"])\n'
          '        if cat:\n'
          '            genre_val = cat["genre"]\n'
          '            books_by_genre[genre_val] = '
          'books_by_genre.get(genre_val, 0) + 1\n'
          '\n'
          '    # Top 5 most borrowed books\n'
          '    from collections import Counter\n'
          '    book_loan_counts = Counter(l["book_id"] for l in loans)\n'
          '    top_books = []\n'
          '    for book_id, count in book_loan_counts.most_common(5):\n'
          '        b = db["books"].get(book_id)\n'
          '        if b:\n'
          '            top_books.append({"book_id": book_id, "title": '
          'b["title"], "loan_count": count})\n'
          '\n'
          '    # Recent activity (last 10 loans)\n'
          '    recent = sorted(loans, key=lambda l: str(l["created_at"]), '

          '    recent_activity = [\n'
          '        {\n'
          '            "type":        "loan",\n'
          '            "loan_id":     l["id"],\n'
          '            "book_title":  l.get("book_title"),\n'
          '            "member_name": l.get("member_name"),\n'
          '            "date":        str(l["loan_date"]),\n'
          '            "status":      l["status"],\n'
          '        }\n'
          '        for l in recent\n'
          '    ]\n'
          '\n'
          '    return {\n'
          '        "total_books":        len(books),\n'
          '        "total_copies":       sum(b["total_copies"] for b in '
          'books),\n'
          '        "available_copies":   sum(b["available_copies"] for b in '
          'books),\n'
          '        "total_members":      len(members),\n'
          '        "active_members":     sum(1 for m in members if m["status"] '
          '== MemberStatus.ACTIVE),\n'
          '        "active_loans":       len(active_loans),\n'
          '        "overdue_loans":      len(overdue_loans),\n'

          '    overdue = [\n'
          '        l for l in db["loans"].values()\n'

          '\n'
          '@app.get(\n'
          '    "/reports/popular-books",\n'
          '    tags=["Statistics"],\n'
          '    summary="Report: Most popular books by loan count",\n'
          ')\n'
          'def popular_books_report(\n'

          '                "book_id":     book_id,\n'
          '                "title":       book["title"],\n'
          '                "author":      book.get("author_name"),\n'
          '                "isbn":        book["isbn"],\n'
          '                "loan_count":  count,\n'
          '            })\n'
          '    return {\n'
          '        "generated_at": now().isoformat(),\n'
          '        "since_date":   str(since_date) if since_date else "all '
          'time",\n'
          '        "top_n":        top_n,\n'
          '        "books":        result,\n'
