          '\n'
          '    @validator("email")\n'
          '    def email_valid(cls, v: str) -> str:\n'
          '        parts = v.split("@")\n'
          '        if len(parts) != 2 or "." not in parts[1]:\n'
          '            raise ValueError("Invalid email address")\n'
          '        return v.lower().strip()\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'class MemberUpdate(BaseModel):\n'
          '    first_name:      Optional[str]            = Field(None, '
          'min_length=1, max_length=100)\n'
          '    last_name:       Optional[str]            = Field(None, '
          'min_length=1, max_length=100)\n'
          '    email:           Optional[str]            = None\n'
          '    phone:           Optional[str]            = Field(None, '
          'max_length=25)\n'
          '    date_of_birth:   Optional[date]           = None\n'
          '    membership_type: Optional[MembershipType] = None\n'
          '    address:         Optional[Address]        = None\n'
          '    is_active:       Optional[bool]           = None\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'class MemberResponse(BaseModel):\n'
          '    id:                 str\n'
          '    first_name:         str\n'
          '    last_name:          str\n'
          '    full_name:          str\n'
          '    email:              str\n'
          '    phone:              Optional[str]\n'
          '    date_of_birth:      Optional[date]\n'
          '    membership_type:    MembershipType\n'
          '    membership_number:  str\n'
          '    address:            Optional[Address]\n'
          '    join_date:          datetime\n'
          '    membership_expiry:  date\n'
          '    is_active:          bool\n'
          '    active_loans_count: int\n'
          '    total_loans_count:  int\n'
          '    outstanding_fines:  float\n'
          '    max_borrow_limit:   int\n'
          '    created_at:         datetime\n'
          '    updated_at:         datetime\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '# ── Loan '
          '──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
          '\n'
          'class LoanCreate(BaseModel):\n'
          '    book_id:   str           = Field(..., example="<book-id>")\n'
          '    member_id: str           = Field(..., example="<member-id>")\n'
          '    due_days:  int           = Field(DEFAULT_LOAN_DAYS, ge=1, '
          'le=60,\n'
          '                                     description="Days from today '
          'until the loan is due")\n'
          '    notes:     Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=500)\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'class LoanReturn(BaseModel):\n'
          '    condition: str           = Field("good", max_length=50, '
          'example="good",\n'
          '                                     description="Book condition: '
          'good | fair | damaged | lost")\n'
          '    notes:     Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=500)\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'class LoanRenewal(BaseModel):\n'
          '    extend_days: int           = Field(7, ge=1, le=30, '
          'description="Additional days to add to due date")\n'
          '    notes:       Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=500)\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'class LoanResponse(BaseModel):\n'
          '    id:                  str\n'
          '    book_id:             str\n'
          '    book_title:          str\n'
          '    book_isbn:           str\n'
          '    member_id:           str\n'
          '    member_name:         str\n'
          '    membership_number:   str\n'
          '    borrow_date:         datetime\n'
          '    due_date:            datetime\n'
          '    return_date:         Optional[datetime]\n'
          '    status:              LoanStatus\n'
          '    renewals_count:      int\n'
          '    max_renewals:        int\n'
          '    days_remaining:      Optional[int]\n'
          '    days_overdue:        Optional[int]\n'
          '    fine_amount:         float\n'
          '    fine_paid:           bool\n'
          '    condition_on_return: Optional[str]\n'
          '    notes:               Optional[str]\n'
          '    created_at:          datetime\n'
          '    updated_at:          datetime\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '# ── Reservation '
          '───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
          '\n'
          'class ReservationCreate(BaseModel):\n'
          '    book_id:   str           = Field(..., example="<book-id>")\n'
          '    member_id: str           = Field(..., example="<member-id>")\n'
          '    notes:     Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=500)\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'class ReservationResponse(BaseModel):\n'
          '    id:                str\n'
          '    book_id:           str\n'
          '    book_title:        str\n'
          '    member_id:         str\n'
          '    member_name:       str\n'
          '    membership_number: str\n'
          '    reservation_date:  datetime\n'
          '    expiry_date:       Optional[datetime]\n'
          '    queue_position:    int\n'
          '    status:            ReservationStatus\n'
          '    notes:             Optional[str]\n'
          '    fulfilled_at:      Optional[datetime]\n'
          '    created_at:        datetime\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '# ── Review '
          '────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
          '\n'
          'class ReviewCreate(BaseModel):\n'
          '    book_id:   str           = Field(..., example="<book-id>")\n'
          '    member_id: str           = Field(..., example="<member-id>")\n'
          '    rating:    int           = Field(..., ge=1, le=5, example=5)\n'
          '    title:     Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=200, '
          'example="A timeless masterpiece")\n'
          '    comment:   Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=2000)\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'class ReviewUpdate(BaseModel):\n'
          '    rating:  Optional[int] = Field(None, ge=1, le=5)\n'
          '    title:   Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=200)\n'
          '    comment: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=2000)\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'class ReviewResponse(BaseModel):\n'
          '    id:                   str\n'
          '    book_id:              str\n'
          '    book_title:           str\n'
          '    member_id:            str\n'
          '    member_name:          str\n'
          '    rating:               int\n'
          '    title:                Optional[str]\n'
          '    comment:              Optional[str]\n'
          '    is_verified_borrower: bool   # True if member has ever borrowed '
          'this book\n'
          '    helpful_count:        int\n'
          '    created_at:           datetime\n'
          '    updated_at:           datetime\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '# ── Fine '
          '──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
          '\n'
          'class FinePayment(BaseModel):\n'
          '    loan_id:        str           = Field(..., '
          'example="<loan-id>")\n'
          '    amount:         float         = Field(..., gt=0, example=5.50)\n'
          '    payment_method: str           = Field(..., max_length=50, '
          'example="cash")\n'
          '    notes:          Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=500)\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'class FineResponse(BaseModel):\n'
          '    loan_id:       str\n'
          '    book_title:    str\n'
          '    member_id:     str\n'
          '    member_name:   str\n'
          '    borrow_date:   datetime\n'
          '    due_date:      datetime\n'
          '    days_overdue:  int\n'
          '    fine_per_day:  float\n'
          '    total_fine:    float\n'
          '    paid_amount:   float\n'
          '    outstanding:   float\n'
          '    is_fully_paid: bool\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '# ── Statistics '
          '────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
          '\n'
          'class LibraryStatistics(BaseModel):\n'
          '    total_book_titles:       int\n'
          '    total_book_copies:       int\n'
          '    total_authors:           int\n'
          '    total_members:           int\n'
          '    active_members:          int\n'
          '    total_categories:        int\n'
          '    total_loans_all_time:    int\n'
          '    active_loans:            int\n'
          '    overdue_loans:           int\n'
          '    returned_loans:          int\n'
          '    total_reservations:      int\n'
          '    active_reservations:     int\n'
          '    total_reviews:           int\n'
          '    average_book_rating:     float\n'
          '    total_fines_outstanding: float\n'
          '    total_fines_collected:   float\n'
          '    loans_this_month:        int\n'
          '    loans_this_year:         int\n'
          '    books_with_availability: int\n'
          '    books_fully_borrowed:    int\n'
          '    books_by_genre:          Dict[str, int]\n'
          '    top_5_genres:            List[str]\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'class PopularBook(BaseModel):\n'
          '    rank:           int\n'
          '    id:             str\n'
          '    title:          str\n'
          '    author_name:    str\n'
          '    genre:          Genre\n'
          '    borrow_count:   int\n'
          '    average_rating: float\n'
          '    review_count:   int\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'class ActiveMember(BaseModel):\n'
          '    rank:              int\n'
          '    id:                str\n'
          '    full_name:         str\n'
          '    membership_number: str\n'
          '    membership_type:   MembershipType\n'
          '    total_loans:       int\n'
          '    active_loans:      int\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'class OverdueSummary(BaseModel):\n'
          '    total_overdue_loans:     int\n'
          '    total_fines_outstanding: float\n'
          '    oldest_overdue_days:     int\n'
          '    members_with_overdue:    int\n'
          '    overdue_loans:           List[Dict[str, Any]]\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '# '
          '══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n'
          '# IN-MEMORY DATA STORES\n'
          '# '
          '══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n'
          '\n'
          'authors_db:      Dict[str, dict] = {}\n'
          'books_db:        Dict[str, dict] = {}\n'
          'members_db:      Dict[str, dict] = {}\n'
          'loans_db:        Dict[str, dict] = {}\n'
          'reservations_db: Dict[str, dict] = {}\n'
          'reviews_db:      Dict[str, dict] = {}\n'
          'categories_db:   Dict[str, dict] = {}\n'
          'fines_db:        Dict[str, dict] = {}   # keyed by loan_id\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '# '
          '══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n'
          '# UTILITY / HELPER FUNCTIONS\n'
          '# '
          '══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n'
          '\n'
          'def new_id() -> str:\n'
          '    return str(uuid.uuid4())\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'def utcnow() -> datetime:\n'
          '    return datetime.utcnow()\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'def gen_membership_number() -> str:\n'
          '    return f"LIB-{random.randint(100_000, 999_999)}"\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'def paginate(items: list, page: int, per_page: int) -> Tuple[list, '
          'PaginationMeta]:\n'
          '    total       = len(items)\n'
          '    total_pages = max(1, math.ceil(total / per_page))\n'
          '    start       = (page - 1) * per_page\n'
          '    return items[start: start + per_page], PaginationMeta(\n'
          '        page=page, per_page=per_page,\n'
          '        total_items=total, total_pages=total_pages,\n'
          '        has_next=page < total_pages, has_prev=page > 1,\n'
          '    )\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'def get_loan_status(loan: dict) -> LoanStatus:\n'
          '    if loan.get("return_date") is not None:\n'
          '        return LoanStatus.RETURNED\n'
          '    return LoanStatus.OVERDUE if utcnow() > loan["due_date"] else '
          'LoanStatus.ACTIVE\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'def calc_fine(loan: dict) -> float:\n'
          '    """Total fine accrued for a loan (ignores payments)."""\n'
          '    check = loan.get("return_date") or utcnow()\n'
          '    if check <= loan["due_date"]:\n'
          '        return 0.0\n'
          '    return round((check - loan["due_date"]).days * FINE_PER_DAY, '
          '2)\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'def outstanding_fine(loan_id: str, loan: dict) -> float:\n'
          '    fine = calc_fine(loan)\n'
          '    paid = fines_db.get(loan_id, {}).get("paid_amount", 0.0)\n'
          '    return max(0.0, round(fine - paid, 2))\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'def member_total_outstanding(member_id: str) -> float:\n'
          '    return round(sum(\n'
          '        outstanding_fine(l["id"], l)\n'
          '        for l in loans_db.values()\n'
          '        if l["member_id"] == member_id\n'
          '    ), 2)\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '# ── Enrichment helpers '
          '────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
          '\n'
          'def enrich_loan(loan: dict) -> dict:\n'
          '    book    = books_db.get(loan["book_id"], {})\n'
          '    member  = members_db.get(loan["member_id"], {})\n'
          '    lstatus = get_loan_status(loan)\n'
          '\n'
          '    days_remaining = days_overdue = None\n'
          '    if lstatus == LoanStatus.ACTIVE:\n'
          '        days_remaining = max(0, (loan["due_date"] - '
          'utcnow()).days)\n'
          '    elif lstatus == LoanStatus.OVERDUE:\n'
          '        days_overdue = (utcnow() - loan["due_date"]).days\n'
          '\n'
          '    fine      = calc_fine(loan)\n'
          '    paid      = fines_db.get(loan["id"], {}).get("paid_amount", '
          '0.0)\n'
          '    fine_paid = (fine == 0.0) or (paid >= fine)\n'
          '\n'
          '    return {\n'
          '        **loan,\n'
          '        "book_title":          book.get("title", "Unknown"),\n'
          '        "book_isbn":           book.get("isbn",  "Unknown"),\n'
          '        "member_name":         f"{member.get(\'first_name\',\'\')} '
          '{member.get(\'last_name\',\'\')}".strip(),\n'
          '        "membership_number":   member.get("membership_number", '
          '""),\n'
          '        "status":              lstatus,\n'
          '        "days_remaining":      days_remaining,\n'
          '        "days_overdue":        days_overdue,\n'
          '        "fine_amount":         fine,\n'
          '        "fine_paid":           fine_paid,\n'
          '    }\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'def enrich_book(book: dict) -> dict:\n'
          '    author = authors_db.get(book.get("author_id", ""), {})\n'
          '\n'
          '    active_loan_count = sum(\n'
          '        1 for l in loans_db.values()\n'
          '        if l["book_id"] == book["id"] and l.get("return_date") is '
          'None\n'
          '    )\n'
          '    available    = max(0, book["total_copies"] - '
          'active_loan_count)\n'
          '    book_reviews = [r for r in reviews_db.values() if r["book_id"] '
          '== book["id"]]\n'
          '    avg_rating   = (sum(r["rating"] for r in book_reviews) / '
          'len(book_reviews)) if book_reviews else 0.0\n'
          '    borrow_count = sum(1 for l in loans_db.values() if l["book_id"] '
          '== book["id"])\n'
          '\n'
          '    # Derive live BookStatus\n'
          '    forced = book.get("status")\n'
          '    if forced in (BookStatus.LOST, BookStatus.MAINTENANCE, '
          'BookStatus.RETIRED):\n'
          '        live_status = forced\n'
          '    elif available > 0:\n'
          '        live_status = BookStatus.AVAILABLE\n'
          '    else:\n'
          '        has_pending = any(\n'
          '            r["status"] in (ReservationStatus.PENDING, '
          'ReservationStatus.READY)\n'
          '            for r in reservations_db.values() if r["book_id"] == '
          'book["id"]\n'
          '        )\n'
          '        live_status = BookStatus.RESERVED if has_pending else '
          'BookStatus.BORROWED\n'
          '\n'
          '    return {\n'
          '        **book,\n'
          '        "author_name":     f"{author.get(\'first_name\',\'\')} '
          '{author.get(\'last_name\',\'\')}".strip(),\n'
          '        "available_copies": available,\n'
          '        "borrowed_copies":  active_loan_count,\n'
          '        "status":           live_status,\n'
          '        "average_rating":   round(avg_rating, 2),\n'
          '        "review_count":     len(book_reviews),\n'
          '        "borrow_count":     borrow_count,\n'
          '    }\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'def enrich_member(member: dict) -> dict:\n'
          '    active_loans = sum(\n'
          '        1 for l in loans_db.values()\n'
          '        if l["member_id"] == member["id"] and l.get("return_date") '
          'is None\n'
          '    )\n'
          '    total_loans = sum(1 for l in loans_db.values() if '
          'l["member_id"] == member["id"])\n'
          '    return {\n'
          '        **member,\n'
          '        "full_name":          f"{member[\'first_name\']} '
          '{member[\'last_name\']}",\n'
          '        "active_loans_count": active_loans,\n'
          '        "total_loans_count":  total_loans,\n'
          '        "outstanding_fines":  '
          'member_total_outstanding(member["id"]),\n'
          '        "max_borrow_limit":   '
          'BORROW_LIMITS.get(member["membership_type"], 3),\n'
          '    }\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'def enrich_reservation(res: dict) -> dict:\n'
          '    book   = books_db.get(res["book_id"], {})\n'
          '    member = members_db.get(res["member_id"], {})\n'
          '    # Queue position = # of PENDING reservations for this book '
          'placed ≤ this one\n'
          '    position = sum(\n'
          '        1 for r in reservations_db.values()\n'
          '        if r["book_id"] == res["book_id"]\n'
          '        and r["status"] == ReservationStatus.PENDING\n'
          '        and r["created_at"] <= res["created_at"]\n'
          '    )\n'
          '    return {\n'
          '        **res,\n'
          '        "book_title":        book.get("title", "Unknown"),\n'
          '        "member_name":       f"{member.get(\'first_name\',\'\')} '
          '{member.get(\'last_name\',\'\')}".strip(),\n'
          '        "membership_number": member.get("membership_number", ""),\n'
          '        "queue_position":    position,\n'
          '    }\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'def enrich_review(review: dict) -> dict:\n'
          '    book    = books_db.get(review["book_id"], {})\n'
          '    member  = members_db.get(review["member_id"], {})\n'
          '    verified = any(\n'
          '        l["book_id"] == review["book_id"] and l["member_id"] == '
          'review["member_id"]\n'
          '        for l in loans_db.values()\n'
          '    )\n'
          '    return {\n'
          '        **review,\n'
          '        "book_title":           book.get("title", "Unknown"),\n'
          '        "member_name":          f"{member.get(\'first_name\',\'\')} '
          '{member.get(\'last_name\',\'\')}".strip(),\n'
          '        "is_verified_borrower": verified,\n'
          '    }\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          'def enrich_category(cat: dict) -> dict:\n'
          '    parent     = categories_db.get(cat.get("parent_id") or "", {})\n'
          '    book_count = sum(1 for b in books_db.values() if cat["id"] in '
          'b.get("category_ids", []))\n'
          '    return {\n'
          '        **cat,\n'
          '        "parent_name": parent.get("name") if parent else None,\n'
          '        "book_count":  book_count,\n'
          '    }\n'
          '\n'
          '\n'
          '# '
          '══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n'
          '# SEED DATA\n'
          '# '
          '══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n'
          '\n'
          'def seed_data() -> None:\n'
          '    """Populate in-memory stores with representative sample '
          'data."""\n'
          '\n'
          '    # ── Categories '
          '────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
          '    cat_specs = [\n'
          '        ("Classic Literature", "Timeless canonical works across the '
          'centuries"),\n'
          '        ("Dystopian Fiction",  "Novels of oppressive futures and '
          'surveillance states"),\n'
          '        ("Computer Science",   "Algorithms, systems design, and '
          'programming theory"),\n'
          '        ("World History",      "Historical events and human '
          'civilisations"),\n'
          '        ("Popular Science",    "Cutting-edge science written for '
          'general readers"),\n'
          '    ]\n'
          '    cat_ids: List[str] = []\n'
          '    for name, desc in cat_specs:\n'
          '        cid = new_id()\n'
          '        cat_ids.append(cid)\n'
          '        categories_db[cid] = dict(\n'
          '            id=cid, name=name, description=desc,\n'
          '            parent_id=None, created_at=utcnow()\n'
          '        )\n'
          '    # A nested sub-category\n'
          '    sub_id = new_id()\n'
          '    categories_db[sub_id] = dict(\n'
          '        id=sub_id, name="Cyberpunk",\n'
          '        description="Near-future tech-noir dystopias",\n'
          '        parent_id=cat_ids[1], created_at=utcnow()\n'
          '    )\n'
          '\n'
          '    # ── Authors '
          '───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n'
          '    def make_author(fn, ln, bio, nat, bd, dd=None, web=None) -> '
          'str:\n'
          '        aid = new_id()\n'
          '        authors_db[aid] = dict(\n'
          '            id=aid, first_name=fn, last_name=ln, bio=bio,\n'
          '            nationality=nat, birth_date=bd, death_date=dd,\n'
          '            website=web, created_at=utcnow(), updated_at=utcnow()\n'
          '        )\n'
          '        return aid\n'
          '\n'
          '    orwell_id  = make_author("George",  "Orwell",  "English '
          'novelist, essayist, and critic.",            "British",   '
          'date(1903,  6, 25), date(1950,  1, 21))\n'
          '    huxley_id  = make_author("Aldous",  "Huxley",  "English writer, '
          'novelist, and philosopher.",         "British",   date(1894,  7, '
          '26), date(1963, 11, 22))\n'
          '    knuth_id   = make_author("Donald",  "Knuth",   "American '
          'computer scientist & mathematician.",        "American",  '
          'date(1938,  1, 10), None, '
          '"https://www-cs-faculty.stanford.edu/~knuth/")\n'
          '    tolkien_id = make_author("J.R.R.",  "Tolkien", "English author, '
          'poet, and professor of linguistics.", "British",   date(1892,  1,  '
          '3), date(1973,  9,  2))\n'
          '    austen_id  = make_author("Jane",    "Austen",  "English '
          'novelist famed for social commentary.",       "British",   '

          '        )\n'
